Kupu Whakataki

'Bhavishya Malika' he kohinga rau o ngā tuhinga i tuhia tata ki te 600 tau ki muri e ngā Panchasakhas (e rima ngā hoa/whanaunga) o Shri Chaitanya Mahaprabhu. I huna ēnei tuhinga mō ngā rau tau, ā, i taua wā, kāore te nuinga o te iwi i mōhio ki aua mea. Heoi anō, ko te āwhiowhio nui i pā ki Odisha i te tau 1999 i kukume i te aro o te tini tangata ki 'Bhavishya Malika' nā te mea i kīia kua oti kē ngā poropititanga mō taua āwhiowhio te tuhituhi ki aua tuhinga.

Heoi anō, nā te mea i tuhia ki te reo Odia, i noho noa te Bhavishya Malika ki Odisha i taua wā. Kāore rawa he tangata i waho o Odisha i mōhio ki ēnei tuhinga. Engari āta haere, ka huri ngā wā. I te tau 2020, i kukume anō te mate urutā COVID-19 i te aro o te tangata ki Bhavishya Malika. Nō muri noa iho i tēnei mate urutā i tīmata ai te whakatairanga me te horahanga o ngā tuhinga Malika ki waho o Odisha, ā, kei te haere tonu tēnei mahi mai i taua wā.

Nā tēnei, he tini tangata kua tīmata ki te ako mō Bhavishya Malika i roto i ngā tau tata nei. Heoi anō, pērā i te nuinga o ngā wā, i te taha o tēnei whakatairanga pai, i tīmata hoki te horapa o ngā whakawāheke me ngā kōrero hē mō ētahi irava Malika. Hei tauira, i tīmata ētahi tangata ki te horapa kōrero pakiwaitara ko te tau o Kaliyuga he 10,000 tau; ko te ingoa o te kingi whakamutunga o Puri ko Mukunda Dev; ko ngā kaupapa matua o Malika me ngā mahi whakangaro (Sanhaar-Leela) a Ariki Kalki ka puta i te wā o te Meena-Shani Yoga e whai ake nei (te wā i waenga i te 2054-57); kāore te rohe o Khandagiri i Bhubaneswar engari kei tētahi atu wāhi; ā, he maha tonu ngā tau e toe ana i mua i te tīmatanga o Satyayuga, me ētahi atu.

I tīmata ēnei kōrero hē ki te whakaputa i te tini ruarua i roto i ngā hinengaro o ngā karakia. Nā te kore o te whakatau tika mō ēnei ruarua, i tīmata te heke o tō rātou whakapono ki te Malika. Ko te whāinga matua o tēnei tuhinga ehara i te mea ki te hoatu kōrero tika me te pūmau mō Bhavishya Malika ki ngā kaipānui hou anake, engari hoki ki te whakatau i ngā ruarua e puta ake ana i roto i ngā hinengaro o ngā karakia. E tūmanakohia ana mā te whiwhi i ngā kōrero tika mō ēnei kaupapa matua, ka kaha ake te whakapono o te hunga whakapono ki te Malika.

1 Bhavishya Malika – He Kupu Whakataki Poto

1.1 Kupu Whakataki

I waenganui i ngā rautau 15 me te 16, e rima ngā wairua tapu nui i whānau ki Odisha (Shishu Ananta Das, Yashobanta Das, Achyutananda Das, Balarama Das, me Jagannatha Das) i tito i ngā rau o ngā tuhinga pēnei i tuhia ai ngā poropititanga maha. I mōhiotia ēnei tuhinga ko 'Bhavishya Malika'. Ko Sri Chaitanya Mahaprabhu, i whānau i te tau 1486, ā, i kiia he whakatinatanga takirua o Bhagawan Sri Krishna me Jaganmata Radharani, he hononga hōhonu ki ēnei wairua tapu e rima. I karanga a Sri Chaitanya Mahaprabhu ki ēnei tokorima ko 'Panchasakha' (Ngā Hoa Tokorima). Nā te whakahau a Mahaprabhu tonu i tito ai ēnei Panchasakha i ngā tuhinga Malika. I tua atu i ngā Panchasakha, i kawe hoki ētahi atu wairua tapu nui i te tikanga o te tuhi Malika. Hei tauira, ko te tangata karakia a Salabega i whānau i te rautau 17, ko Hāto Arkhit Das me Hāto Hadidas i whānau i te rautau 18, ko Hāto Bhima Bhoi o te rautau 19, ko Thakur Shri Abhiram Paramahansa o te rautau 20, me ētahi atu.

I tuhia ngā poropititanga taipitopito ki ēnei tuhinga Malika mō ngā kaupapa pēnei i te tau o Kaliyuga, te whānautanga o te Ariki Kalki, te whakangaromanga o te hunga kino, te tiakitanga o ngā tangata karakia, te whakatūnga o Dharma, me te taenga mai o Satyayuga.

1.2 Ko wai ngā Panchasakha?

Ko tētahi o ngā Panchasakha, ko Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, i tuhi i roto i tētahi o āna tuhinga Malika:

Odia Sloka:

ବଳବନ୍ତ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ଅନନ୍ତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ,
ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ଦାସ ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ନାମେ ଖ୍ୟାତ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Balabanta Yaśhobanta Ananta Achyuta,
Jagannātha Dāsa Pañchasakhā nāme khyāta.

Tikanga:
Ko Balarama Das, Yashobanta Das, Shishu Ananta Das, Achyutananda Das, me Jagannatha Das i rongonui hei Panchasakha.

He mea nui kia mōhio, he tawhito rawa te hītori o ngā Panchasakha. I te mutunga o ia wā (Yuga), ka heke iho ngā Panchasakha ki te Ao, i whānau mai i tētahi wāhanga o te Ariki, ki te tuku i tā rātou takoha nui ki te kaupapa a te Ariki ki te whakatū anō i te Dharma.

I tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Udaya Kahani’:

Odia Sloka:

ଆମ୍ଭେ ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ପଞ୍ଚକୁଳରେ,
ଜନମ ହୋଇଲୁ କୃଷ୍ଣ ଆଜ୍ଞାରେ।
ଯୁଗ ଯୁଗରେ ଯେ ଜନମ ହେଉ,
କୃଷ୍ଣଲୀଳାମାନ ମୁଖରେ ଗାଉ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Ambhe Pañchasakhā pañchakulare,
janama hoilu Kṛṣhṇa ājñāre.
yuga yugare je janama heu,
Kṛṣhṇalīḷāmāna mukhare gau.

Tikanga:
I roto i tēnei irārangi, e kī ana a Hāto Achyutananda Das: "Ko mātou, ngā hoa tokorima, ka whānau ki roto i ngā whakapapa rerekē e rima i runga i te whakahau a te Ariki Krishna. I ia tau, ka whānau mātou, ā, ka waiata i ngā mahi tapu (Leelas) a Krishna i ō mātou waha."

1.3 Te Whakaaturanga o ngā Panchasakha puta noa i ngā Wā e Whā

Ko Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Udaya Kahani’, e whakaahua ana i ngā āhua i tangohia e ngā wairua tapu nui e rima i ia o ngā Yuga e whā:

Odia Sloka:

ଆଦିଅନନ୍ତ ଯୁଗଟି ଅନ୍ତରେ, ସତ୍ୟ ପ୍ରବେଶ ହୋଇଲା ମହୀରେ।
ଅସୁର ବିନାଶ କରିବା ପାଇଁ, ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମିଲେ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣେ ଯାଇଁ।
ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଋଷିତନୁ ବହିଲେ, କୃପାଜଳ ଋଷି ଗାଗର୍ବ ହେଲେ।
ମାର୍କଣ୍ଡ ଯେ ସ୍ୱୟଂଭୂ ନାରଦ, ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଜନମ କହିଲି ଭେଦ।
ରାମ ଜନମ ତ୍ରେତୟା ଯୁଗରେ, ତ୍ରେତୟା ଗୁଣ କହିଲେ ନ ସରେ।
ତ୍ରେତୟାରେ ନଳ ନୀଳ ସୁଷେଣ, ଜାମ୍ବବ ହନୁ ପଞ୍ଚସଖାମାନ।
ତ୍ରେତୟା ଅନ୍ତେ ଦ୍ୱାପର ପ୍ରବେଶ, ଗୋପାଳ ମୂରତି ଶ୍ରୀକୃଷ୍ଣ ବେଶ।
ପଞ୍ଚସଖାମାନେ ହେଲେ ଜନମ, ସୁବଳ ସୁବାହୁ ସୁଦାମ ଦାମ।
ଶ୍ରୀବତ୍ସ ଘେନି ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ହେଲେ, ଦ୍ୱାପର ଲୀଳାମାନ ସାଙ୍ଗ କଲେ।
ଅବନୀ ମଧ୍ୟରେ ନାମ ସ୍ଥାପିଲେ, ହରିନାମରେ ଜଗତ ମୋହିଲେ।
ସ୍ତ୍ରୀ ଶୂଦ୍ର ଆଦି ପାଷାଣ୍ଡ ଯେତେ, ହରିନାମ ଶୁଣି ହେଲେ ମୁକତେ।
ଆମ୍ଭେ ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଜନମ ହେଲୁ, ଅନନ୍ତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ନାମ ବହିଲୁ।
ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ହୋଇଲେ, ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ମେଳ ବଳରାମରେ।
ଏମନ୍ତ ନାମରେ ଆମ୍ଭେ ଜନମ, କହିଦେଲୁ ବାବୁ ତୋର ଆଗେଣ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Adi Ananta Yugaṭi Antare, Satya Prabesha Hoilā Mahire.
Asura Bināsha Karibā Pāiñ, Prabhu Janamile Brāhmaṇe Jāiñ.
Pañchasakhā Ṛuṣi Tanu Bahile, Kṛupājala Ṛuṣi Gāgarba Hele.
Mārkaṇḍa Je Swayambhū Nārada, Pañchasakhā Janama Kahili Bheda.
Rāma Janama Tretayā Yugare, Tretayā Guṇa Kahile Na Sare.
Tretayāre Naḷa Nīḷa Suṣeṇa, Jāmbaba Hanu Pañchasakhāmāna.
Tretayā Ante Dwāpara Prabesha, Gopāḷa Mūrati Shrī Kṛuṣṇa Besha.
Pañchasakhāmāne Hele Janama, Subaḷa Subāhu Sudāma Dāma.
Shrībatsa Gheni Pañchasakhā Hele, Dwāpara Līḷāmāna Sāṅga Kale.
Abanī Madhyare Nāma Sthāpile, Harināmare Jagata Mohile.
Strī Shūdra Ādi Pāṣāṇḍa Jete, Harināma Shuṇi Hele Mukate.
Ambhe Pañchasakhā Janama Helu, Ananta Achyuta Nāma Bahilu.
Jagannātha Jashobanta Hoile, Pañchasakhā Meḷa Baḷarāmare.
Emanta Nāmare Ambhe Janama, Kahidelu bābu tora āgeṇa.

Tikanga:

Arā, i ngā wā onamata, i te mutunga o te Ananta Yuga i te tīmatanga o Satyayuga, i whānau te Ariki Nui i roto i te āhua Brahmin ki te whakangaro i ngā rewera. I taua wā, i puta mai ngā wairua tapu e rima i te tinana o te Ariki tonu hei Rātā: Kripajala, Garga (Gagarba), Markandeya, Swayambhu, me Narada.

I muri i a Satyayuga, i Tretayuga, i te wā i mahi ai te Ariki i ngā mahi tapu maha i roto i tana whakatinanatanga Rama, ka heke iho ēnei hoa tokorima ki te Ao i roto i ngā āhua o Nala, Nila, Sushena, Jambavan (Jambeba), me Hanuman.

I te mutunga o Tretayuga, ka tae mai a Dwaparyuga, ā, i whānau te Ariki i roto i te āhua o te tamaiti hepara kau, a Shri Krishna. I tēnei wā, ka noho a Subala, Subahu, Sudama, Dama, me Shribatsa hei Panchasakha, ā, i whai wāhi ki ngā mahi tapu atua me Shri Krishna.

I tua atu, i Kaliyuga, i te wā i puta mai ai te Ariki i roto i tana whakatinanatanga Chaitanya, i whānau ēnei hoa tokorima ki te horapa i te ingoa tapu (Hari-naam), me te whakatū i tētahi ara māmā noa ki te whakaoranga mō te katoa. I Kaliyuga, i rongonui rātou ko Shishu Ananta Das, Achyutananda Das, Jagannatha Das, Yashobanta Das, me Balarama Das.

1.4 Te Whakapapa Taipitopito o ngā Panchasakha puta noa i ngā Wā

I te wā i pātai ai te ākonga a Ramadas i ngā ingoa motuhake o ngā Panchasakha puta noa i ngā wā e whā, i hoatu e Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tēnei kōrero taipitopito i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Udaya Kahani’.

Odia Sloka:

ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗରେ ଋଷି କୃପାଜଳ, ତ୍ରେତୟା ସଖାଟି ଅଟଇ ନଳ।
ଦ୍ୱାପରେ ସୁଦାମ ସଖାଟି ହୋଇ, କଳି ଯୁଗରେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବୋଲାଇ।
ଗାର୍ଗବ ଋଷି ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗରେ ହେଲେ, ତ୍ରେତୟା ଜାମ୍ବବ ନାମ ବହିଲେ।
ଦ୍ୱାପରେ ସୁବାହୁ ବ୍ରଜ ଅଟନ୍ତି, ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ନାମ କଳିରେ ପାନ୍ତି।
ସ୍ୱୟଂଭୂ ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗରେ ସେ ଜାଣେ, ତ୍ରେତୟାରେ ଅଟେ ସେହି ସୁଷେଣ।
ଗୋପରେ ଶ୍ରୀବତ୍ସ ଦ୍ୱାପରେ ଜାତ, କଳି ଯୁଗରେ ନାମ ଶିଶୁ ଅନନ୍ତ।
ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗରେ ସଖା ନାରଦ ଋଷି, ତ୍ରେତୟାରେ ନୀଳ ନାମ ପ୍ରକାଶି।
ଦାମ ଗୋପାଳ ଦ୍ୱାପର ଯୁଗରେ, ବଳରାମ ଦାସ ନାମ କଳିରେ।
ମାର୍କଣ୍ଡ ଋଷି ଅଟେ ହନୁମାନ, ସୁବଳ ସଖାଟି ଦ୍ୱାପରେ ପୁଣ।
ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ନାମ କଳିରେ ହେଲେ, ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ନାମ ପ୍ରକାଶ କଲେ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Satya Yugare Ṛuṣi Kṛupājala, Tretayā Sakhāṭi Aṭai Naḷa.
Dwāpare Sudāma Sakhāṭi Hoi, Kaḷi Yugare Achyuta Bolāi.
Gārgaba Ṛuṣi Satya Yugare Hele, Tretayā Jāmbaba Nāma Bahile.
Dwāpare Subāhu Braja Aṭanti, Jashobanta Nāma Kaḷire Pānti.
Swayambhū Satya Yugare Se Jāṇe, Tretayāre Aṭe Sehi Suṣeṇa.
Gopare Shrībatsa Dwāpare Jāta, Kaḷi Yugare Nāma Shishu Ananta.
Satya Yugare Sakhā Nārada Ṛuṣi, Tretayāre Nīḷa Nāma Prakāshi.
Dāma Gopāḷa Dwāpara Yugare, Baḷarāma Dāsa Nāma Kaḷire.
Mārkaṇḍa Ṛuṣi Aṭe Hanumāna, Subaḷa Sakhāṭi Dwāpare Puṇa.
Jagannātha Nāma Kaḷire Hele, Pañchasakhā Nāma Prakāsha Kale.

Whakawhitinga o ngā Tuakiri:
  • Achyutananda Das: Ko ia te Rātā Kripajala i Satyayuga, ko Nala i Tretayuga, me Sudama i Dwaparayuga.
  • Yashobanta Das: Ko ia te Rātā Garga i Satyayuga, ko Jambavan i Tretayuga, me Subahu i Dwaparayuga.
  • Shishu Ananta Das: Ko ia te Rātā Swayambhu i Satyayuga, ko Sushena i Tretayuga, me Shribatsa i Dwaparayuga.
  • Balarama Das: Ko ia te Rātā Narada i Satyayuga, ko Nila i Tretayuga, me Dama i Dwaparayuga.
  • Jagannatha Das: Ko ia te Rātā Markandeya i Satyayuga, ko Hanuman i Tretayuga, me Subala i Dwaparayuga.

1.5 Ngā Mana Motuhake me ngā Pūkenga o ngā Panchasakha

He hunga mōhio-katoa, he hunga whai-mōhiotanga-whaiaro, he hunga whai-mōhiotanga-Atua ngā Panchasakha. He Yogis teitei rātou, he hunga rapu, he hunga karakia, he tohunga ia tangata ki tētahi wāhanga rerekē o te pūtaiao wairua. E whakaahua ana a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i tēnei i roto i tana Malika:

Odia Sloka:

ଆଗମ୍ୟ ଭାବ ଜାଣେ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ,
ଗାର କଟା ଯନ୍ତ୍ର ଜାଣେ ଅନନ୍ତ।
ଆଗତ ଅନାଗତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଭଣେ,
ବଳରାମ ଦାସ ତତ୍ତ୍ୱ ବଖାଣେ।
ଭକ୍ତିର ଭାବ ଜାଣେ ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ,
ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଏ ଓଡ଼ିଶା ମହନ୍ତ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Āgamya Bhāba Jāṇe Jashobanta,
Gāra Kaṭā Jantra Jāṇe Ananta.
Āgata Anāgata Achyuta Bhaṇe,
Baḷarāma Dāsa Tattwa Bakhāṇe.
Bhaktira Bhāba Jāṇe Jagannātha,
Pañchasakhā E Oḍiśā Mahanta.

Tikanga:
I roto i ngā Panchasakhas i whānau mai i Odisha:
  • Ko Yashobanta Das te tangata i mōhio ki ngā mea ngaro hōhonu o ngā wā o mua me ngā wā kei te heke mai (Agam-Nigam).
  • He tohunga a Shishu Ananta Das ki te pūtaiao o ngā hoahoa ngaro me ngā mihini (Yantra-Vigyan).
  • He matakite a Achyutananda Das mō ngā wā o mua, o nāianei, me ngā wā kei te heke mai (Trikal-Darshi).
  • He kairangahau hōhonu a Balarama Das mō te tūturu mutunga me te rapunga whakaaro (Tattwa-Vigyan).
  • Kāore he rite o Jagannatha Das i roto i te ngākau tapu o te karakia pono (Bhakti-Bhaav).

1.6 Ngā Whare Karakia (Ashrams) o ngā Panchasakhas

I whakapūmau ngā Panchasakhas i ō rātou Peethas me ō rātou whare noho mō ngā tohunga ki ngā wāhi rerekē e rima puta noa i Odisha. Ka whakamārama a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i tēnei i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ନେମାଳେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଅଢ଼ଙ୍ଗେ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ, ବାଲିପାଟଣାରେ ରହିଲେ ଅନନ୍ତ।
ସାତ ଲହଡ଼ିରେ ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ରହି, କ୍ଷେତ୍ରବରେ ବଳବନ୍ତ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Nemāḷe Achyuta Aḍhaṅge Jashobanta, Bālipāṭaṇāre rahiḷe Ananta.
Sāta Lahaḍire Jagannātha rahi, Khetrabare Baḷabanta.

Tikanga:
  • I whakapūmau a Achyutananda Das i tana nohoanga ki Nemalo (rohe o Cuttack).
  • I whakatū a Yashobanta Das i tana Peetha ki Adhanga (rohe o Jagatsinghpur).
  • I whakapūmau a Shishu Ananta Das (Ananta) i tana whare karakia ki Balipatna (rohe o Khurda).
  • I whakapūmau a Jagannatha Das i tana Ashram ki Sata Lahadi.
  • I whakatū a Balarama Das (Balabanta) i Shrikhetra (Puri) hei wāhi noho mōna.

1.7 Panchasakhas – Ngā Whakaarikitanga Tapu o Te Ariki

He maha ngā wā i kōrerohia e ngā Panchasakhas i roto i ā rātou tuhinga Malika he whakaarikitanga wāhanga (Anshavatar) rātou nō Te Ariki Nui tonu. Ka tuhi a Mahapurush Shishu Ananta Das i roto i tana tuhinga 'Chumbak Malika':

Odia Sloka:

ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବୋଲିଣ କଳିଯୁଗେ ନାମ ସୁଦାମ ସଖା ଅଟନ୍ତି,
ଶ୍ରୀଅଙ୍ଗରୁ ଜାତ କଟିରୁ ଭକତ ଶୁଣ ହୋ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ଚେତି।
ମୁଖୁଁ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ହୃଦୁଁ ଆମ୍ଭେ ଜାତ ବାହୁ ମୂଁଳୁ ବଳରାମ,
ରାଧାଙ୍କ ହାସ୍ୟରୁ ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ଦାସ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ହେତୁରେ ଘେନ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Achyuta boliṇa Kaḷiyuge nāma Sudāma sakhā aṭanti,
Shrī-aṅgaru jāta kaṭiru bhakata shuṇa ho Bāraṅga cheti.
Mukhuṃ Jashobanta hṛuduṃ ambhe jāta bāhu mūṃḷu Baḷarāma,
Rādhāṅka hāsyaru Jagannātha Dāsa Bāraṅga heture ghena.

Tikanga:
I Kaliyuga, ko Achyutananda Das te hoa a Sudama nō Dwaparayuga, ā, i whānau mai i te hope (Kati) o Te Ariki. I whānau mai a Yashobanta Das i te waha tapu (Shri-mukh) o Mahaprabhu. I puta mai a Shishu Ananta Das i te ngākau (Hriday) o Te Ariki, ā, i whānau mai a Balarama Das i ngā ringa (Bahu) o Te Ariki. I tua atu, i heke mai a Jagannatha Das i te katakata (Haasya) o Whaea Radharani.

Ehara i te mea ko Shishu Ananta Das anake, engari kua tuhia hoki e Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tēnei i roto i tana tuhinga Malika, 'Chakada Madana'. Ka tuhi te Mahapurush:

Odia Sloka:

ନିରାକାର ଦେହୁ ପାଞ୍ଚଟି ଜ୍ୟୋତି,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଅନନ୍ତ ପଞ୍ଚକ ମୂରତି।
ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ବଳରାମ,
ଜନମ ହୋଇଲୁ ଏ ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟ ଧାମ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Nirākāra dehu pāṃchaṭi jyoti,
Achyuta Anaṃta paṃchaka mūrati.
Jashobaṃta Jagannātha Balarāma,
janama hoilu e martya dhāma.

Tikanga:
Arā, nō te tinana marama o Te Ariki Kāore he Āhua, i heke mai mātou ngā Panchasakhas e rima, ā, i whānau mai mātou ki tēnei ao i raro i ngā ingoa o Achyutananda, Shishu Ananta, Jashobanta, Jagannatha, me Balarama.

Ka tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i te tuhinga Malika, 'Barnatika':

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ କୃଷ୍ଣ ଶରୀର ପଞ୍ଚଭୂତରୁ,
ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଜନ୍ମ କହିଛି ଗୁରୁ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Shrī Kṛiṣhṇa sharīra paṃchabhūtaru,
paṃchasakhā janma kahichi guru.

Tikanga:
Arā, i puta te whānautanga o ngā Panchasakhas i ngā mea e rima o te tinana o Te Ariki Shri Krishna.

1.8 Ngā Poropititanga nā Te Whakahau a Te Ariki

E kiia ana ko ngā irārangi o te Malika ko ngā kupu a Mahaprabhu tonu. Ko tētahi o ngā Panchasakhas, ko Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, ka tuhi i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୁଁ କହୁଛି ବୋଲି ମୋର କଥା ନୁହେଁ ଆଦ୍ୟୁଁ ଖଞ୍ଜିଛନ୍ତି ଧାତା,
କର୍ମବାମ ହେଲେ କଳ୍ପନା ଗ୍ରାସିବ ପ୍ରକୃତି କାଟିବେ ଚିତା।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Muṃ kahuchi boli mora kathā nuheṃ ādyuṃ khañjichanti dhātā,
Karmabāma hele kalpanā grāsiba prakṛuti kāṭibe chitā.

Tikanga:
Ka kī te Mahapurush: "Ahakoa he aha taku e tuhi nei, ehara ēnei i aku kupu. He mea whakarite katoa ēnei e Te Ariki mai i te tīmatanga rawa."

I kōrero hoki a Mahapurush Shishu Ananta Das ki tana akonga i roto i ‘Patta Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଆହୁରି କଥାଏ ଶୁଣରେ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ହେତୁରଖ ଯାକୁସାର,
ଭାବବିନୋଦିଆ ହରିଙ୍କ ଏ ବାଣୀ ଅଟେ ସିନା ଅଗୋଚର।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Āhuri kathāe shuṇare Bāraṅga heturakha jākusāra,
Bhābabinodiā Hariṅka e bāṇī aṭe sinā agochara.

Tikanga:
"E te akonga e Baranga, whakarongo! Ehara ēnei i aku, engari ko ngā kupu kē a Te Ariki Shri Hari."

I tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika, 'Anakara Samhita':

Odia Sloka:

ଛତିଶ ସଂହିତା ଅଠସ୍ତରୀ ଗୀତା ବଂଶାନୁ ସପ୍ତବିଂଶରେ,
ଉପବଂଶାନୁ ଦ୍ୱାଦଶ ଖଣ୍ଡ ବେନି ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ଶତ ଖଣ୍ଡ ରେ।
ପଦ ପଦ୍ୟାବଳି ଲକ୍ଷେକ ଯେ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ସବୁ ଶ୍ରୀକୃଷ୍ଣ ମହିମା,
ତୋ ଆଗେ କହିଲୁଁ ବ୍ରଜ କୁମର ବ୍ରହ୍ମ ସାରସ୍ୱତ ସୀମା।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Chatisha saṃhitā aṭhastarī gītā baṃshānu saptabiṃshare,
upabaṃshānu dwādasha khaṃḍa beni bhabiṣhya shata khaṃḍa re.
Pada padyābali lakṣheka je graṃtha sabu Shrīkṛiṣhṇa mahimā,
to āge kahilu braja kumara brahma sāraswata sīmā.

Tikanga:
Ka kī a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ko ngā tuhinga i titoa e ia kei roto ko ngā Samhitas e 36, ngā Gitas e 78, ngā Banshanucharitras e 27, ngā Upabanshanucharitras e 24, ngā tuhinga Malika e 100, me ngā rau mano o ngā irārangi (padas). I kī anō te Mahapurush i titoa katoatia ēnei tuhinga nā te korōria, nā te atawhai rānei o Te Ariki Shri Krishna.

I tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika, 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta':

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀରଙ୍ଗା ଚରଣ ବିନୁ ନ ଜାଣଇ ନୀଳାଚଳେ ମନ ମୋର,
ତାଙ୍କ କଥାମାନ ଉଲ୍ଲେଖ କରଇ ନ କହେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଛାର।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Shrī raṃgācharaṇa binu na jāṇai nīlāchale mana mora,
tāṃka kathāmāna ullekha karai na kahe Achyuta chāra.

Tikanga:
I roto i tēnei sloka, ka kī a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das kāore tōna hinengaro e piri ki tētahi atu mea engari ki ngā waewae o Te Ariki Nui. Ka noho tōna hinengaro e rukuhia ana ki taua Nilachala Dhama, Puri, arā, ki te karakia aroha ki Te Ariki. Ka kī anō ia ahakoa he aha tana e tuhi nei i roto i ngā tuhinga Malika, he kupu tika katoa ēnei i kōrerohia e Te Ariki; kāore ia i tuhi i tētahi mea nā tōna hiahia ake.

I tuhi anō a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika, 'Parardha Jataka':

Odia Sloka:

ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କର ଆଜ୍ଞା ହେଲା ଯେପରି,
ଆମ୍ଭେ ଲେଖିଲୁ ପୋଥି ସେହିପରି।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Prabhunkara ājñā helā jepari,
āmbhe lekhilu pothi sehipari.

Tikanga:
Arā, i rite tonu ki te whakahau me te arataki a Te Ariki Nui, i titoa e au te tuhinga Malika i taua āhua tonu.

I tuhi a Achyutanandaji i roto i tana 'Agata Bhabishya Prabandha Malika':

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ ମୁଖରୁ ଯାହା ମୋତେ ଆଜ୍ଞା ହେଉଅଛି,
ସେହି ପଦମାନ ସବୁ ପୁରାଣେ ଲେଖୁଛି।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Shrī mukharu jāhā mote ājñā heuachi,
sehi padamāna sabu purāṇe lekhuchi.

Tikanga:
Arā, nā te whakahau a Te Ariki Nui, nā Tōna arataki tonu, kei te tuhi au i te Malika. Ahakoa he aha i puta mai i Te Ariki, kei te whakamārama au i aua slokas i roto i te Malika Purana.

1.9 He Tuhinga Ngaro te Malika, ā, e Huna ana Ōna Poropititanga

Ka tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i ‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’:

Odia Sloka:

ଯୋଗୀମାନେ ଯୋଗ ଅନ୍ତ ନ ପାଇବେ ଆଉ କେବା ସାମରଥା।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Yogīmāne yoga anta na pāibe āu kebā sāmarathā.

Tikanga:
Ahakoa ngā Yogis nunui me ngā tohunga kāore e kitea te mea ngaro o ēnei take huna mā te Yoga, waihoki ko ētahi atu tāngata.

Ka tuhi anō a Achyutananda Das mō te āhua whakamuna o te tuhinga:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଲଟ ପାଲଟ କକ୍ଷା ଏହି ଅଙ୍କ, କର ହୃଦେ ତୁହି ଠାବ।
କଳିଯୁଗ ଲୀଳା ଶେଷ ଯେ ହୋଇବ, ମସିହାରେ ନାହିଁ ଠାବ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Olaṭa pālaṭa kakshā ehi aṅka, kara hṛude tuhi ṭhāba.
Kaḷiyuga līḷā shesha je hoiba, masihāre nāhiṃ ṭhāba.

Tikanga:
Kua tuhia ngā poropititanga i runga i ngā tātaitanga tau, ā, kua whakamunatia ēnei tau mā te whakahuri i aua mea. Ka puta ngā kaupapa engari ehara i te mea e ai ki ngā tau maramataka noa; ka whai rātou i ngā tātaitanga tohu ngaro.

Mō te whānautanga o Kalki, ka tuhi ia:

Odia Sloka:

ଗୁପତରେ ଆମ୍ଭେ ଜନମ ହେବୁ,
ଗୁପତରେ ସନ୍ ସାଲ ଚାଲିବୁ।
ଗୁପତ ଭକତ ଜାଣନ୍ତି ଯାହା,
ଗୁପତ ଅଙ୍କ ଏ ଅଟେ ଏହା।
ଗୁପତରେ କେତେ ଖେଳ କରିବୁ,
ଗୁପତ ଅଙ୍କକୁ ଚଳାଇ ଥିବୁ।
ମୋର ଭକତଟି ଜାଣିବେ ସେହି,
ଗୁପତ ଅଙ୍କ ବୋଲିଟି ଯେ ଏହି।
ଗୁପତ ଗୁପତ ଅତି ଗୁପତ,
କହୁଅଛନ୍ତି ସେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Gupatare ambhe janama hebu,
gupatare san sāla chālibu.
Gupata bhakata jāṇanti jāhā,
gupata aṅka e aṭe ehā.
Gupatare kete kheḷa karibu,
gupata aṅkaku chaḷāi thibu.
Mora bhakataṭi jāṇibe sehi,
gupata aṅka boliṭi ye ehi.
Gupata gupata ati gupata,
kahuachanti se dīna Achyuta.

Tikanga:
Ka huna te whānautanga o Te Ariki. Ka haere ngā wā me ngā tau i te āhua huna. Ko ngā akonga tapu huna anake ka mōhio ki tēnei nā te mea kua whakamunatia ngā tau.

Ka tuhia e Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika, 'Chakada Madana':

Odia Sloka:

ଗୁପତ ଗୁପତ ଏ ମହା ଗୁପତ ଆନେ ନ କହିବୁ ତୁହି,
ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଯେ ଗୀତରେ କହଇ ମୋ ପାଦକୁ ଥାଅ ଛୁଇଁ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Gupata gupata e mahā gupata āne na kahibu tuhi,
dīna Achyuta je gītare kahai mo pādaku thāa chuiṃ.

Tikanga:
Arā, ehara noa i te kupu ngaro, engari he kupu tino ngaro rawa atu i tuhia e Achyutananda Das ji mā te mahara ki te Ariki me te whakaaroaro ki Ōna waewae puaawai.

I roto i tētahi atu tuhinga, ka kī te Mahapurush ki tana ākonga a Ramadasa:

Odia Sloka:

କଳ୍କୀ ପ୍ରକାଶ ହେଲେ ତୋହ ମୋହ ଦେଖା,
ତେରଜଣ ମଧ୍ୟେ ଥିବୁ ଆମ୍ଭେ ପଞ୍ଚସଖା।
ଗୁପତ ଅତି ଗୁପତ ଅଟଇ ହେ ରାମ,
କହୁଅଛି ବ୍ରଜ ବାଳ ହେତୁରେ ତୁ ଘେନ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Kalki prakāsha hele toha moha dekhā,
terajaṇa madhye thibu āmbhe pañchasakhā.
Gupata ati gupata aṭai he Rāma,
kahuachi braja bāla heture tu ghena.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka whānau mai te Ariki Kalki, ka puta tōna whakaaturanga atua, ā, ka whai wāhitanga pai mātou ko ngā Panchasakhas ki te mahi me te noho tuku iho ki Ōna waewae. Engari ko ēnei mahi atua e kore e puta puku noa, engari ka tino huna rawa atu.

Ka tuhi anō te Mahapurush:

Odia Sloka:

ଗୁପତ ଗୁପତ ଏ ମହା ଗୁପତ ଶୁଣ ତୁ ରେ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର,
ବ୍ରହ୍ମା ଶଙ୍କରକୁ ଭେଦ ତ ନଥିବ ନ ଜାଣିବେ ଇନ୍ଦ୍ର ଚନ୍ଦ୍ର।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Gupata gupata e mahā gupata shuṇa tu re Rāmachandra,
Brahmā Shaṅkaraku bheda ta nathiba na jāṇibe Indra Chandra.

Tikanga:
Arā, e te ākonga a Ramachandra, ehara noa i te kupu ngaro ngā kupu o te Malika, engari he tino ngaro rawa atu. Ahakoa a Prajapati Brahma me te Ariki Shankar e kore e mārama ki ō rātou mea ngaro, waihoki ko te Ariki Indra me te Ariki Chandra.

Kua tuhia anō e Sant Bhima Bhoi mō tēnei kaupapa i roto i tana tuhinga Malika, 'Stuti Chintamani':

Odia Sloka:

ସଂସାର ମଧ୍ୟରେ କେମନ୍ତେ ଜାଣିବେ ନର ଅଙ୍ଗେ ଦେହ ବହି,
ଗତ ଆଗତ ଯୁଗର ବ୍ୟବସ୍ଥା ସମସ୍ତଙ୍କୁ ଜଣାନାହିଁ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Saṃsāra madhyare kemaṃte jāṇibe nara aṅge deha bahi,
gata āgata yugara byabasthā samastaṃku jaṇānāhiṃ.

Tikanga:
Arā, ko te tini o ngā tāne me ngā wāhine o te ao e kore e whiwhi kōrero mō te raupapa o ngā wā, te mutunga o te Kali Yuga, te Malika, me ōna poropititanga. Ko rātou anake e homai ana e te Ariki tōna atawhai, ko rātou ngā akonga tapu o te Ariki i ngā wā o mua, ka mōhio ki ēnei poropititanga tino hōhonu.

Nā, ka puta te pātai, ko wai rā ngā akonga tapu o te Ariki nō ngā wā o mua ka homai e te Ariki tōna atawhai, ā, ka whakapono ki ngā kupu o te Malika, ka mōhio ki aua mea, ā, ka whai mātauranga tika mō te mutunga o te Kali Yuga me te avatar a Kalki!

I hoatu e Mahapurush Achyutananda Das te whakautu ki tēnei i roto i tana tuhinga Malika, 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta'. Ka tuhi te Mahapurush:

Odia Sloka:

କୋଟିକେ ଗୋଟିଏ ଜାଣନ୍ତି ଏ ରସ ତିନି ସସ୍ରେ ଗଣାସେହି,
ମହିମା ପ୍ରକାଶ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ସେହି ଦାସ ଆନେ ମୁଁ କହନ୍ତି ନାହିଁ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Koṭike goṭie jāṇaṃti e rasa tini sasre gaṇāसेhi,
mahimā prakāsha nishche sehi dāsa āne muṃ kahaṃti nāhiṃ.

Tikanga:
Arā, kotahi anake te tangata i roto i te kotahi crore (tekau miriona) ka mōhio ki ēnei poropititanga, ā, ko te katoa o aua tāngata i te ao katoa e 3,000 anake.

1.10 E kore e Hapa ngā Poropititanga o Malika

Ko Bhavishya Malika ngā kupu a te Ariki tonu, nō reira kāore he pātai mō te korenga e tutuki. I roto i te tuhinga Malika ‘Chakada Madana’, ka tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das:

Odia Sloka:

ପୃଥିବୀ ଦୁଇଫାଳ ହେଲେ ହୋଇବ, ପୂର୍ବ ସୂର୍ଯ୍ୟ ଅବା ପଶ୍ଚିମେ ଯିବ।
ପର୍ବତ ଶିଖରେ ଫୁଟିବ କଇଁ, ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ମିଥ୍ୟା ନୁହଇଁ।
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତର ବାଣୀ ଅଟେ ପଥରର ଗାର, ଆନ କରିବାକୁ ନାହିଁ କ୍ଷମତା କାହାର।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Pṛuthibī duiphāḷa hele hoiba, pūrba sūrya abā pashchime jiba.
Parbata shikhare phuṭiba kaiñ, Achyuta bachana mithyā nuhaiñ.
Achyutara bāṇī aṭe patharara gāra, āna karibāku nāhiñ khyamatā kāhāra.

Tikanga:
"Ahakoa ka wehe rua te Ao, ka whiti te rā i te rāwhiti ki te uru, ka puaawai he rengarenga i te tihi o te maunga—engari e kore rawa e hē ngā kupu a Achyutananda. He rite ki te rārangi i whaoa ki te kōhatu, ā, kāore he tangata e kaha ki te whakahē i aua kupu."

I roto i te ‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’, ka tuhi ia:

Odia Sloka:

ସୀତା ଠାକୁରାଣୀ ଅସତୀ ହୋଇବେ ପର୍ବତେ ଫୁଟିବ କଇଁ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ତିଳେ ନ ଟଳିବ ପାଷାଣର ଗାର ଏହି।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Sītā ṭhākurāṇī asatī hoibe parbate phuṭiba kaiñ,
Achyuta bachana tiḷe na taḷiba pāṣāṇara gāra ehi.

Tikanga:
I te Kaliyuga, tērā pea ka kī ngā tāngata he wahine puremu a Whaea Sita, ā, ka puaawai he rengarenga i te tihi o te maunga, engari e kore rawa e hapa ngā kupu a Achyutananda Das, nō te mea he rite ki te rārangi i whaoa ki te kōhatu.

1.11 I roto i te ‘Amara Jumara Samhita’, e kōrero ana i ngā kupu a te Ariki kore-āhua (Nirakar), ka tuhi ia:

Odia Sloka:

ଏ କଥା ଯେବେ ନ ହୋଇବ, ଆମ୍ଭ ଶ୍ରୀମୁଖ ମିଥ୍ୟା ହେବ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

E kathā jebe na hoiba, ambha Shrīmukha mithyā heba.

Tikanga:
I roto i tēnei irārangi, ka kī te Ariki kore-āhua tonu: "Ki te kore e tutuki ngā kupu o te Malika, ka hē taku waha tapu." Nō te mea he kupu ēnei nō te waha o te Ariki tonu, e kore rawa e hapa.

Ka tuhi anō te Mahapurush i roto i te 'Chakada Madana':

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ବାଣୀ ପଥରର ଗାର ପର୍ବତେ ଫୁଟିବ କଇଁ,
ପୂର୍ବ ସୂର୍ଯ୍ୟ ଅବା ପଶ୍ଚିମକୁ ଯିବ ମୋ ବଚନ ସତ୍ୟ ଏହି।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Shrī Achyuta bāṇī patharara gāra parbate phuṭiba kaiṃ,
pūrba sūrya abā pashchimaku jiba mo bachana satya ehi.

Tikanga:
Arā, tērā pea ka whiti te rā i te uru hei utu mō te rāwhiti, ka puaawai he rengarenga i te tihi o te maunga, engari e kore e taea te whakarereke ngā kupu a Achyutananda. Kua whaoa ngā kupu a Achyutananda ki te kōhatu; ko rātou te pono mutunga kore.

Ko tētahi whakahokinga mai o Achyutananda Das ji, a Sant Hadi Das, i whānau i te rau tau 1800 tata ki Chatia i te rohe o Jajpur i Odisha, i tito anō hoki i te maha o ngā tuhinga Malika.

I roto i tana tuhinga Malika 'Kali Chautisha', ka tuhi ia:

Odia Sloka:

ଟଳିବ ନାହିଁଟି ଏ କଥା ନିଶ୍ଚେ ହୋଇବ ଦିନେ,
ଟାକି ରହିଛନ୍ତି ଭରସା କରି ଭକତମାନେ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Ṭaliba nāhiṃṭi e kathā nishche hoiba dine,
ṭāki rahichaṃti bharasā kari bhakatamāne.

Tikanga:
Arā, ko ngā mea i tuhia ki ngā tuhinga Malika e kore rawa e whakarereketia – ka tino puta mai rātou i tētahi rā. Kei te tatari ngā akonga tapu katoa me te whakapono ki ēnei kaupapa.

Ka tuhi anō a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika, 'Agata Bhabishyat Malika':

Odia Sloka:

ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦାସ ବାରମ୍ବାର,
ବୁଝାଏ ଭକତଙ୍କୁ ଧୀର।
ନମଣ ଏହାକୁ ଯେ ଆନ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦାସ କରେ ଧ୍ୟାନ।
କଳି କଳ୍ମଷ ନାଶଯିବ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବାକ୍ୟ ନ ଟଳିବ।
ଅନନ୍ତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତଙ୍କ ଲୀଳା,
ସାରିବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଚକାଡୋଳା।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Achyuta dāsa bārambāra,
bujhāe bhakataṃku dhīra.
Namaṇa ehāku je āna,
Achyuta dāsa kare dhyāna.
Kali kalmaṣha nāshajiba,
Achyuta bākya na ṭaliba.
Anaṃta Achyutanka līlā,
sāribe prabhu chakāḍolā.

Tikanga:
Ka tuhi ia kei te whakamārama au i ngā akonga tapu me te manawanui. Kaua ngā akonga tapu e whakaaro he teka ēnei kupu, nō te mea i tito e Achyuta ēnei tuhinga i roto i te āhua o te whakaaroaro. Ka whakangaromia ngā hara me ngā mamae o te Kali Yuga, ā, ka tino puta ngā mahi atua a te Ariki Kalki. Ka tuhi anō te Mahapurush e kore rawa e whakarereketia ana kupu.

Ka tuhi anō te Mahapurush i roto i tētahi o ana tuhinga Malika:

Odia Sloka:

ଅମୃତ ଭକ୍ଷି ଯାଇପାରେ ପ୍ରାଣ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ନୋହିବ ଆନ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Amṛita bhakṣhi jāipāre prāṇa,
Achyuta bachana nohiba āna.

Tikanga:
Arā, ahakoa he mea kore e taea pēnei i te ngaro o te ora hei utu mō te whiwhi matekore mā te inu i te wai ora (Amrit) ka taea pea, engari e kore rawa e hē ngā kupu a Achyutananda Das.

Ka tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana 'Udaya Kahani':

Odia Sloka:

ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ଆନ ନୋହିବ,
କହିଲେ ଅପ୍ରାଧ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ପାଇବ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Achyuta bachana āna nohiba,
kahile aparādha nishche pāiba.

Tikanga:
Arā, e kore rawa e hē ngā kupu a Achyutananda Das. Ko te tangata e kī ana he teka ēnei kupu, ka tino uru ia ki taua hara.

Ka tuhi te Mahapurush i roto i te 'Chakada Madana':

Odia Sloka:

ମୋ ଶ୍ରୀମୁଖ ବାଣୀ ଅନ୍ୟଥା ନୋହିବ ପର୍ବତେ ଫୁଟିବ କଇଁ,
ନେମାଳ ବଟକୁ ଲୟ ଲଗାଇଣ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ କହଇ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Mo shrīmukha bāṇī anyathā nohiba parbate phuṭiba kaiṃ,
Nemāla baṭaku laya lagāiṇa dīna Achyutī kahai.

Tikanga:
Arā, e kore rawa aku kupu e rerekē, ahakoa ka puaawai he rengarenga i runga i te maunga! Kua tuhia ēnei kupu i roto i te āhua o te whakaaroaro i raro i te rākau banyan Nemal (Nemal Vata).

I roto i te tuhinga Malika 'Chakada Madana' tonu, ka tuhi anō a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das:

Odia Sloka:

ମହୀଫାଟି ଜଳୁପଛେ ପ୍ରଳୟ ଅନଳ,
ଯାହା କହିଛନ୍ତି ତାହା ସର୍ବଦା ଅଚଳ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Mahīphāṭi jalupache pralaya anala,
jāhā kahichaṃti tāhā sarbadā achala.

Tikanga:
Arā, ko te ahi ka puta mai i muri i te wehenga o te whenua ka kawe mai i te whakangaromanga (Pralaya) ki te ao katoa, engari e kore rawa aku kupu e teka. E kore e taea te whakahē, e kore e taea te whakarereke aku kupu.

2 Te Kaupapa o Malika – Te Whakaoranga o Ngā Akonga Tapu

He maha ngā tāngata ka mataku i muri i te pānui i ngā poropititanga o te Malika, ā, ka whakakore i aua mea hei "tinihanga whakamataku." Heoi, he tino hē tēnei whakaaro. Ko te whāinga matua ko te tiaki wairua me te ārahi i ngā akonga tapu i roto i te whakawhitinga o ngā wā.

Ka tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’:

Odia Sloka:

ହେତୁ ରସାଇବା ପାଇଁକି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଶାହାସ୍ତ୍ର ପୁରାଣ କଲା,
କଳିକାଳଠାରୁ ବଳି କାଳ ଯହିଁ ହକ କଥାଟା ଲେଖିଲା।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Hetu rasāibā pāiṃki Achyuta shāhāstra purāṇa kalā,
kalikālaṭhāru bali kāla jahiṃ haka kathāṭā lekhilā.

Tikanga:
I te mutunga o te Kaliyuga, hei whakaoho i te mahara moe o ngā akonga tapu, kei te tito ia i ēnei tuhinga Malika. Ko te kaupapa kia i roto i te Kaliyuga hōhonu, i te wā e kapi ana te pōuri o te kuare i ngā wāhi katoa, ka taea e ngā akonga tapu o te Ariki te tae atu ki a Mahaprabhu Kalki mā te Malika, kia taea ai te hononga o te akonga tapu me te Atua. Nō reira, kei te tuhi ia i ngā poropititanga nui i roto i ngā tuhinga Malika mai i te Kaliyuga ki te Sangam Yuga (Wā Whakawhiti) me te haere tonu ki te Ananta Yuga (Adi Satyayuga) e haere mai ana.

Ka tuhi anō a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das:

Odia Sloka:

ଅବନୀକୁ ଚେତେଇବା ପାଇଁ ଚେତାବନୀ,
ଅବନୀ ହିତେ ଲେଖା ଏ ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ବାଣୀ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Abanīku cheteibā pāiñ chetābanī,
Abanī hite lekhā e bhabishya bāṇī.

Tikanga:
Ko te kaupapa o ngā poropititanga i tuhia e au i roto i ngā tuhinga Malika he whakatūpato i te ao. Ka whakamārama ia i tuhia ēnei poropititanga mō te oranga o te ao.

Ka tuhi anō te Mahapurush i roto i te ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ପୂର୍ବର ବାସନା ମୂଳେ ଜ୍ଞାନ ଉଦୟ ହେଲା,
ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ହିତରେ ମାଳିକା ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଲେଖି ରଖିଲା।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Pūrbara bāsanā mūḷe jñāna udaya helā,
Bhaktanka hitare Māḷikā grantha lekhi rakhilā.

Tikanga:
Nō te 'vasana' o mua (ngā tohu), arā, te karakia ki te Ariki nō ngā whānautanga o mua, ka puta te mātauranga ki ahau i tēnei whānautanga, ā, nō reira i tito au i ngā tuhinga Malika mō te painga me te oranga o ngā akonga tapu.

Ka tuhi anō te Mahapurush i roto i te ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ସତର୍କ ଘଣ୍ଟି ଯେ ଚେତାବନୀ ଏହି ଜାଣ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa satarka ghaṇṭi je chetābanī ehi jāṇa.

Tikanga:
Ko ngā kupu o ngā tuhinga Malika he mea whakatūpato; kia mōhio koutou he puka whakatūpato.

I tuhi anō a Mahapurush:

Odia Sloka:

ସାରସ୍ୱତ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଏହି ଚେତାବନୀ ପୁଣ,
ବାରଣ ବେଳେ ପ୍ରକାଶ ଭକ୍ତ ହିତେ ପୁଣ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Sāraswata grantha ehi chetābanī puṇa,
bāraṇa beḷe prakāsha bhakta hite puṇa.

Tikanga:
Ko ngā irārangi Malika he puka whakatūpato mō te tangata. Kua tuhia ēnei mō te oranga o ngā akonga pono i te mutunga o Kaliyuga. Nō reira, me mārama ki a tātou kāore te Malika e horapa i te wehi, engari e para ana i te huarahi ki te whakaoranga. Ko te kaupapa matua o Bhavishya Malika ko te oranga o te ao.

2.1 Kāore te Malika mō te katoa

Me mōhio pai tātou kāore te Malika mō te katoa. Kua rāhuitia e ngā Panchasakha Mahapurushas ā rātou akonga i roto i ngā tuhinga Malika kia kaua e horapa i ēnei kupu ki mua i ngā wairangi. I kī a Mahapurush kāore ēnei mō te katoa; kāore ngā tāngata katoa e mōhio ki a rātou. Ko ngā akonga pono anake o te Ariki ka whakapono ki a rātou.

Ka tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଲକ୍ଷେ ପଞ୍ଚାଅଶୀ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ କଳିପୁଣି ଆଗତ ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ବାଣୀ,
ଭକତ ହିତରେ ପ୍ରକାଶ କରିଲି ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଯେ ଭଣି।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Lakshe pañchāashī grantha kaḷi puṇi āgata bhabishyabāṇī,
Bhakata hitare prakāsha karili dīna Achyuta je bhaṇi.

Tikanga:
I tito au i te katoa o ngā tuhinga 185,000, ā, i tua atu i tērā, i tuhi au i ngā matapae maha e haere mai ana. Kei te whakaputa au i ēnei katoa mō te painga o ngā akonga pono.

Ka kī atu a Achyutananda Das ki tana akonga a Ramadas i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ଏ ପରମତତ୍ତ୍ୱ ମୂର୍ଖକୁ ନ କର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ତତେ ସପ୍ତବାର ନିୟମ ଦେଉଛି କହଇ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।
ଲକ୍ଷେ ପଞ୍ଚାଅଶୀ ତିନି ଅଠର ମାଳିକା କଲି ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ବାରଣ ବେଳକୁ ଏ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ରଖିଛି ଜ୍ଞାନୀ ଜନଙ୍କ ପୀୟୂଷ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Shuṇa Rāmachandra e paramatattwa mūrkhaku na kara prakāsha,
tate saptabāra niyama deuchi kahai dīna Achyuta.
Lakshe pañchāashī tini aṭhara mālikā kali prakāsha,
bāraṇa beḷaku e grantha rakhichi jñānī janaṅka pīyūṣa.

Tikanga:
Whakarongo, e te akonga Ramadas, he kupu ngaro ēnei. Kaua e kōrero ki mua i ngā wairangi, ki te kore ka hara koe. Ko ēnei tuhinga he puka whakatūpato mō te tangata ka rite ki te wai ora mō ngā akonga pono i te mutunga o Kaliyuga.

I tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ହୋଇବ ଡାକିନୀ ଗୋଳ ପଡ଼ିବ ଚହଳ,
ଅବନୀ କମ୍ପିବ ସଦା ହୋଇ ଟଳଟଳ।
ନାନା ଉତ୍ପାତ ମାନ ଅନ୍ତରୀକ୍ଷେ ହେବ,
ଭକ୍ତ ବିନା ଅନ୍ୟ କେହି ଜାଣି ନ ପାରିବ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Hoiba ḍākinī goḷa paḍiba chahaḷa,
Abanī kampiba sadā hoi taḷataḷa.
Nānā utpāta māna antarikṣe heba,
Bhakta binā anya kehi jāṇi na pāriba.

Tikanga:
I te mutunga o Kaliyuga, i te wā o te whakatūnga o Dharma, ka puta he pakanga whakamataku, ā, ka noho he raruraru i te Ao. Ka wiri te Ao. He maha ngā raruraru ka puta i te āhuarangi. Engari haunga ngā akonga pono, kāore he tangata kē e mōhio ko ēnei ngā mahi tapu whakangaro a te Ariki.

Ka kī atu a Mahapurush ki te akonga a Ramadas i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ବାବୁରାମ ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ମୂର୍ଖଙ୍କୁ କହିବୁ ନାହିଁ,
ଘୋର କଳିକାଳ ଟାହି ଯେ ଟାପରା ବାନା ନିନ୍ଦା ହେବ ତହିଁ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Shuṇa Bāburāma Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa mūrkhanku kahibu nāhiñ,
Ghora Kaḷikāḷa ṭāhi je ṭāparā bānā nindā heba tahiñ.

Tikanga:
E te akonga Ramadas, kaua e kōrero i ngā kupu o tēnei tuhinga ‘Chakada Madana’ ki mua i ngā wairangi. I te hōhonutanga o Kaliyuga, ka tawai ngā wairangi ki a rātou, ka puta he whakahe ki te Ariki.

I tuhi anō a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୂର୍ଖଙ୍କୁ ଏ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଅଟଇଟି ବିଷ ଜ୍ଞାନୀଙ୍କୁ ଅଟେ ପିୟୂଷ,
ଅଜ୍ଞାନୀ ଆଗରେ ପ୍ରକାଶ କରିଲେ ମୋ ଅମୃତେ ହେବ ବିଷ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Mūrkhanku e grantha aṭaiṭi biṣa jñānīnku aṭe piyūṣa,
Ajñānī āgare prakāsha karile mo amṛute heba biṣa.

Tikanga:
Mō ngā wairangi, ko ēnei kupu Malika he rite ki te paihana, engari mō te hunga mātau he rite ki te 'Piyush' rānei ki te wai ora. Ko te kōrero i ēnei kupu ki te hunga wairangi he rite ki te whakaranu paihana ki te wai ora.

I tuhi anō a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର କହୁଛି ଏହି ନିଷ୍ଠା ବଚନ,
ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଅଟଇ ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ଗଣ୍ଠିଧନ।
ବାରଣ ବେଳକୁ ରଖିଛି ଏହା ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ପାଇଁ,
ଗୁପତରେ ଖେଳ ହୋଇବ ତୋତେ ଦେଲି ଚେତେଇ।
ଗଣ୍ଠିଆ ଭକତ ଅଟଇ ଗଣ୍ଠି କଥାରେ ମନ,
ସାତଶ ବାସ୍ତରୀ ପରାର୍ଦ୍ଧ ଭବିଷ୍ୟତ ପୁରାଣ।
ଧର୍ମ ସଂସ୍ଥାପନ ହୋଇବ ଭକ୍ତ ହେବେ ମୁକତ,
ଶୁଣ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ଦାସରେ ମୋର ବଚନ ସତ୍ୟ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Shuṇa Rāmachandra kahuchi ehi niṣṭhā bachana,
Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa aṭai bhaktaṅka gaṇṭhidhana.
Bāraṇa beḷaku rakhichi ehā bhaktaṅka pāiṃ,
Gupatare kheḷa hoiba tote deli chetei.
Gaṇṭhiā bhakata aṭai gaṇṭhi kathāre mana,
Sātasha bāstarī parārddha bhabishyata purāṇa.
Dharma saṃsthāpana hoiba bhakta hebe mukata,
Shuṇa Rāmachandra dāsare mora bachana satya.

Tikanga:
Whakarongo e te akonga Ramadas, ko ēnei kupu Malika nōku he rite ki te 'Ganthidhana' rānei ki te taonga nui mō ngā akonga pono. Ka whakatū te Ariki i te Dharma, ā, ka whakaorangia ngā akonga pono. Ka puta ēnei mahi tapu i roto i te āhua tino ngaro. Mō te whakaoranga anake o ngā akonga pono kei te tito au i ēnei tuhinga Malika. Ka whai hua ēnei ki a rātou i te ‘Barana Bela’ rānei i te mutunga o Kaliyuga.

I tuhi anō a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ନେମାଳ ବଟକୁ ଲୟ ଲଗେଇ,
ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଖେଦା ଲେଖଇ।
ସାରସ୍ୱତ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଅଟଇ ଏହି,
ବେଳକୁ ରଖିଛି ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ପାଇଁ।
ଉତ୍ତମ ଶିଷ୍ୟରେ କର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ବାନା ନିନ୍ଦା କହେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦାସ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Nemāḷa baṭaku laya lagei,
Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa khedā lekhai.
Sāraswata grantha aṭai ehi,
Beḷaku rakhichi bhaktaṅka pāiñ.
Uttama shiṣyare kara prakāsha,
Bānā nindā kahe Achyuta Dāsa.

Tikanga:
I raro i te rākau Banyan o Nemalo, kei te tuhi au i tēnei tuhinga Malika ‘Chakada Madana’. Ka whai hua tēnei mō ngā akonga pono i te mutunga o Kaliyuga. Whakaaturia anake ki waenga i ngā akonga pono pai rawa atu; ki te kore, ka puta he whakahe ki ēnei kupu me te Ariki.

Ka tuhi anō a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଲାଗି ଅଲଗାରେ ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଭକ୍ତ ଗଣ୍ଠିଧନ ଜାଣ,
ଭକତ ଜାଣିବେ ଏହାର ଯେ ଭେଦ ଶୂନ୍ୟରେ ଅଛି ପ୍ରମାଣ।
ବାରଣ ବେଳରେ ଏ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ପ୍ରକାଶ ମୂର୍ଖେ ନ କର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ନେମାଳ ବଟ ଯେ ସାଧୁ ପଦାଶ୍ରିତ ଭଣେ ପାମର ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Lāgi alagāre Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa bhakta gaṇṭhidhana jāṇa,
bhakata jāṇibe ehāra je bheda shūnyare achi pramāṇa.
Bāraṇa beḷare e grantha prakāsha mūrkhe na kara prakāsha,
Nemāḷa baṭa je sādhu padāshrita bhaṇe pāmara Achyuta.

Tikanga:
Ko Chakada Madana he taonga mō ngā akonga pono. I te mutunga o Kaliyuga, ko ngā akonga pono anake ka mōhio ki ōna wāhanga me ōna mea ngaro. Kaua e whakaatu ki mua i ngā wairangi.

I tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i ‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’:

Odia Sloka:

ଚେତୁଆ ଭକତମାନଙ୍କ ନିମନ୍ତେ ତିନି ଅଠର ଖଣ୍ଡ,
ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ଲେଖିଲା ପାମର ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ନ ଜାଣନ୍ତି ଦୁରୁଦଣ୍ଡ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Chetuā bhaktamānaṅka nimante tini aṭhara khaṇḍa,
Bhabishya lekhilā pāmara Achyuta na jāṇanti durudaṇḍa.

Tikanga:
Kua tito e te Mahapurush ngā pukapuka 318 o ngā matapae Malika mō te oranga anake o ngā akonga pono kua oho. Kāore ngā wairangi me ngā kaimahi kino e mōhio ki te mea ngaro o ēnei tuhinga.

I tuhi anō ia:

Odia Sloka:

ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛ ବିନାଶି ପ୍ରଭୁ ମେଦିନୀ ରଖିବେ,
ସନାତନ ଧର୍ମ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ପ୍ରଚାର କରିବେ।
କଳିଯୁଗ ଶେଷରେ ପ୍ରକାଶ ନରହରି,
ନାମ ଭଜଏ ଘୋର କଳିରେ ଯିବ ତରି।
ବିପଦ କାଳରେ ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ରକ୍ଷା ପାଇଁ,
ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ରଚଇ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Mlechha bināshi Prabhu medinī rakhibe,
Sanātana Dharma nishche prachāra karibe.
Kaḷiyuga sheshare prakāsha Narahari,
Nāma bhajae ghora kaḷire jiba tari.
Bipada kāḷare bhaktaṅka rakṣā pāiñ,
Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa grantha Achyuta rachai.

Tikanga:
I te mutunga o Kaliyuga, ka whānau te Ariki. Ka whakangaro ia i te hunga kino me te hunga hara hei tiaki i te Ao, ā, ka whakatū anō i te Sanatana Dharma. I tuhi te Mahapurush i taua wā, mō te ārahi o ngā akonga pono me tō rātou tiakitanga, kei te tito ia i ngā tuhinga Malika. I roto i tēnei irārangi, i tuhi mārama te Mahapurush ko te kaupapa o te tito Malika he tiaki i ngā akonga pono i ngā wā o te aituā, me te para i te huarahi ki tō rātou whakaoranga.

Ka tuhi anō a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika, 'Chakada Madana':

Odia Sloka:

ଧର୍ମ ସଂସ୍ଥାପନ ପାପ ନାଶନ,
ଖେଦା ଚେତାବନୀ ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ଜ୍ଞାନ।
ସବୁ ଲେଖିଗଲି ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ପାଇଁ,
ଭକ୍ତ ମୋହର ଯେ ସୋଦର ଭାଇ।
ଭକତ ମୋ ପଞ୍ଚ ପ୍ରାଣଟି ଜାଣ,
ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ନିମନ୍ତେ ଖେଦା ଲିଖନ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Dharma saṃsthāpana pāpa nāshana,
khedā chetābanī bhabishya jñāna.
Sabu lekhigali bhaktanka pāiṃ,
bhakta mohara je sodara bhāī.
Bhakata mo pañcha prāṇaṭi jāṇa,
bhaktanka nimaṃte khedā likhana.

Tikanga:
Arā, ko ngā matapae kua tuhia e au i roto i aku tuhinga Malika e pā ana ki te mutunga o ngā hara me te whakatūnga o Dharma i te mutunga o Kali Yuga, he mea katoa mō ngā akonga pono nō te mea he rite ngā akonga pono ki ōku tuakana. Ko ngā akonga pono ōku manawa ora e rima (Pancha-prana), ā, kua tuhia e au ēnei tuhinga Malika katoa mō rātou anake.

I tuhi anō te Mahapurush:

Odia Sloka:

ସାତଶ ବାସ୍ତରୀ ପରାର୍ଦ୍ଧ ଅଟଇ ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଜାଣ,
ଘୋର କଳିଯୁଗ ଦୁଷ୍ଟଜନ ଛନ୍ତି ଡର ଲାଗୁଅଛି ପୁଣ।
ମୂର୍ଖଙ୍କ ଆଗରେ ପ୍ରକାଶ ନ କର ନିୟମ ଦେଉଛି ମୁହିଁ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Sātasha bāstarī parārddha aṭai Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa jāṇa,
ghora kaliyuga duṣhṭajana chaṃti ḍara lāguachi puṇa.
Mūrkhanka āgare prakāsha na kara niyama deuchi muṃhi.

Tikanga:
Arā, e te akonga Rama, e 772 ngā Parardhas rānei ngā upoko i roto i tēnei tuhinga Malika ko 'Chakada Madana' te ingoa. Kaua e whakaatu ki mua i ngā wairangi. He maha ngā tāngata kino i roto i te Kali Yuga whakamataku; kia matara atu i a rātou katoa.

I roto i 'Chakada Madana' tonu, ka tuhi te Mahapurush:

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ରାମଦାସ ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ମୂର୍ଖେ ନ କର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ନୋହିଲା ନୋହିଲା ପଡୁଥିବ ହୁରି ଭକ୍ତ ଜାଣିବ ଅବଶ୍ୟ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Shuṇa Rāmadāsa Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa mūrkhe na kara prakāsha,
nohilā nohilā paḍuthiba huri bhakta jāṇiba abashya.

Tikanga:
Arā, e te akonga Ramadasa, kaua e whakaatu i ēnei matapae Malika ki mua i ngā wairangi. Ka kī rātou he teka ēnei kupu, kāore ēnei kaupapa e puta. Nō reira, kōrerotia anake ki ngā akonga pono; ko rātou anake ka whakapono ki tēnei.

3 Ngā Poropititanga o Malika Kua Tutuki

Ko te Bhavishya Malika kei roto ngā kupu a Mahaprabhu Tonu, nō reira kāore he mea miharo kua tutuki ngā matapae maha i roto i te Malika i tēnei wā. Ka kōrero tātou i ētahi o aua matapae kua tino pono 100%.

3.1 Matapae e pā ana ki a Atal Bihari Vajpayee Ji

I tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i ana tuhinga Malika 'Kalikalpa Gita' me 'Achyuta Thara':

Odia Sloka:

ତେର ଦିନ ପକ୍ଷ ହୋଇବ ଯେବେ, କଳ୍କୀଙ୍କ ଲୀଳା ଲାଗିବ ତେବେ।
କ୍ଷେତ୍ରରେ ହୋଇବ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ରାଜା, ଫେରିବ କିଛିତାହାର ତରିଜା।
କିଛି ଦିନ ଭୋଗ କରିବ ମହୀ, ତେର ଦିନ ସେହି ଛତ୍ର ଉଡ଼ାଇ।
ସେ ଅଟନ୍ତି ଅଟଳ ବିହାରୀ ବାଜପେୟୀ, ଦିଲ୍ଲୀ ରାଜ ସିଂହାସନେ ବସିଲେକ ତହିଁ।
କ୍ଷେତ୍ରରେ ହୋଇଲେ ଯେବେ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ଯେ ରାଜା, ତାଙ୍କ ସମୟରେ କିଛି ହୋଇଲା ତରିଜା।
ତେରଦିନ ଛତ୍ର ସେହି ଉଡ଼େଇଣ ଦେଲେ, ତାପରେ ପାଞ୍ଚ ବରଷ ମହୀ ଭୋଗ କଲେ।
ସେ ଅଟନ୍ତି ଅଟଳ ବିହାରୀ ବାଜପେୟୀ, ଦିଲ୍ଲୀ ରାଜ ସିଂହାସନେ ବସିଲେକ ତହିଁ।
ପ୍ରଥମେ ତେର ଦିନ ପ୍ରଧାନ ମନ୍ତ୍ରୀ ହେଲେ, ତାପରେ ପାଞ୍ଚ ବରଷ ଶାସନ କରିଲେ।
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ଅଟେ ପଥରର ଗାର, ଆନ କରିବାକୁ ନାହିଁ ଶକତି କାହାର।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Tera dina pakṣa hoiba jebe, Kaḷkīṅka līḷā lāgiba tebe.
Khetratre hoiba Brāhmaṇa rājā, pheriba kichhitāhāra tarijā.
Kichhi dina bhoga kariba mahī, tera dina sehi chhatra uḍāi.
Se aṭanti Aṭala Bihārī Bājapeyī, Dillī rāja siṃhāsane basileka tahiñ.
Khetratre hoile jebe Brāhmaṇa je rājā, tāṅka samayare kichhi hoilā tarijā.
Teradina chhatra sehi uḍeiṇa dele, tāpare pāñcha baraṣa mahī bhoga kale.
Se aṭanti Aṭala Bihārī Bājapeyī, Dillī rāja siṃhāsane basileka tahiñ.
Prathame tera dina pradhāna mantrī hele, tāpare pāñcha baraṣa shāsana karile.
Achyuta bachana aṭe patharara gāra, āna karibāku nāhiñ shakati kāhāra.

Tikanga:
Ngā rau tau ki muri, i tuhi te Mahapurush mō tētahi kaitōrangapū nui o Inia motuhake. I kī ia ka noho te Brahmin hei rangatira mō Inia, he maha ngā panoni ka kitea i te ao. Tuatahi, ka noho ia hei rangatira mō ngā rā 13, ā, muri iho ka pupuri i te mana mō ngā tau e rima. I tuhi te Mahapurush ko te ingoa o tēnei tangata e rangatira ana i Delhi ko "Atal Bihari Vajpayee". E ai ki te hītori, i mahi a Shri Vajpayee hei Pirimia mō ngā rā 13 i te tau 1996, ā, i muri mai mō te wā katoa e 5 tau mai i te 1999 ki te 2004.

3.2 Matapae e pā ana ki a Mahatma Gandhi

I hanga e Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, 600 tau ki muri i roto i tana tuhinga rongonui 'Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta', he matapae whānui e pā ana ki a Mahatma Gandhi.

Odia Sloka:

ଜନମ ହୋଇ ଗୁଜରାଟରେ, ମୋହନ ମୋହିବ ରାମ ନାମରେ।
ଅହିଂସାକୁ ଅସ୍ତ୍ର କରିବ ଜାଣ, ଫିରିଙ୍ଗୀ ହଟିବେ ସେ କାଳେ ଜାଣ।
ତା ଅନ୍ତେ ଧର୍ମ ହୋଇବ ହାଣି, ମୋହନ ରାଜୁତି ହୋଇବ ହାଣି।
ଗୁଳି ଚୋଟେ ତାର ପ୍ରାଣ ଯିବ, ଗଡସେ କଳିମଞ୍ଜି ପୋତିବ।
ଜାଣି ନ ପାରିବେ ସଂସାର ପ୍ରାଣୀ, ଟେକି କହୁଥିବେ କଥା ନ ଜାଣି।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Janama hoi Gujarāṭare, Mohana mohiba Rāma nāmare.
Ahinsāku astra kariba jāṇa, phiriṅgī haṭibe se kāḷe jāṇa.
Tā ante dharma hoiba hāṇi, Mohana rājuti hoiba hāṇi.
Guḷi choṭe tāra prāṇa jiba, Gaḍse kaḷimañji potiba.
Jāṇi na pāribe saṃsāra prāṇī, ṭeki kahuthibe kathā na jāṇi.

Tikanga:
"Ka whānau ia i te kawanatanga o Gujarat. Ko tōna ingoa ko 'Mohan', ā, ka karakia tonu ia i te ingoa o Rama. Ko 'Ahimsa' (Te kore-whakaeke) tōna patu, ā, nā ōna kaha, ka akiakihia ngā Pākehā (Firingis) kia wehe atu i Inia."

I tuhi anō te Mahapurush e kore a Mohan e pupuri i te mana, ā, i muri i tōna matenga, ka heke te Dharma. I whakahua motuhake ia ka patua ia e tētahi tangata ko "Godse" te ingoa ki te "matā" (Guli), ā, mā te mahi pērā, ka whakatō i ngā kākano o Kali ki te motu. I matapae anō ia he maha ngā tāngata e kore e mārama tika ki a Gandhi, ā, ka whakatika i te mahi a te kaikohuru nā te kuare. E mōhio ana tātou, i tutuki ngā kupu katoa o tēnei poropititanga mō te Matua o te Motu.

3.3 Poropititanga mō te Awhiowhio Nui o te tau 1999 me te Pakarutanga o te Kalpavata

I roto i tana tuhinga 'Jai Birachit Chautisha', i matapae te Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i te awhiowhio o te tau 1999, 600 tau i mua.

Odia Sloka:

ସମୁଦ୍ର ଉଠି ଆସିବ ବତାସ ବହିବ, ଶ୍ରୀ କଳ୍ପବଟ ମୋର ଭାଙ୍ଗି ପକାଇବ।
ସତ୍ୟଭାବ ହେ, ସାଲ ଅନେଶତ କର ଠାବ ହେ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Samudra uṭhi āsiba batāsa bahiba, Shrī Kaḷpabaṭa mora bhāṅgi pakāiba.
Satyabhāba he, sāla aneshata kara ṭhāba he.

Tikanga:
"Ka piki te moana, ā, ka pupuhi te hau kaha. Ka pakaru te manga o te Kalpavata tapu (te rākau Banyan tawhito i roto i te whare karakia o Jagannath)." I kī te Mahapurush ka tupu tēnei kaupapa i te tau "Anesata" (99).

I te 29 o Oketopa, 1999, i pā tētahi Awhiowhio Nui ki Odisha me ngā hau 250 km/h. I puta he whakangaro nui, i mate neke atu i te 10,000 tāngata. I rite ki te poropititanga, i pakaru tētahi manga nui o te rākau Kalpavata tawhito i roto i te whare karakia o Puri i te wā o tēnei āwhā.

3.4 Poropititanga mō te Rangatiratanga o Inia (1947)

I roto i te tuhinga 'Garuda Kalpa', i matapae te Hāto Achyutananda ka wātea a Inia i te mana o Ingarangi i te tau 1947 mā te whakamahi i tētahi waehere tau.

Odia Sloka:

ବ୍ରହ୍ମ ଭକ୍ତି ବେଦ ବାରକୁ ଥୋଇ, ଏ ଠାରେ ବିଦେଶୀ ଯିବେ ପଳାଇ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Brahma bhakti beda bāraku thoi, e ṭhāre bideshī jibe paḷāi.

Waehere Tau Wewete:
I whakamahi te Mahapurush i ngā kupu tohu mō ngā tau:
  • Brahma: E tohu ana i te 1 (Pērā i te "Ekam Brahma").
  • Bhakti: E tohu ana i te 9 (Ngā momo e iwa o Navadha Bhakti).
  • Veda: E tohu ana i te 4 (Ngā Veda e whā).
  • Baar: E tohu ana i te 7 (Ngā rā e whitu o te wiki).

Ka whakatakotoria ngātahi (1-9-4-7), ka puta ko 1947. E kī ana te poropititanga i tēnei tau, ka rere atu ngā tauiwi (Ingarangi) i tēnei whenua.

3.5 Poropititanga mō te Whakamate Kōhine i roto i te Kōpū

I roto i 'Jaiphula Malika', i matapae te Mahapurush i ngā kino o te hapori i te mutunga o Kaliyuga:

Odia Sloka:

କନ୍ୟା ରତ୍ନକୁ ଗର୍ଭର, ନଷ୍ଟ କରିବ ଥରକୁ ଥର ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ହୃଦୟେ ନ କରି ପାପ ବିଚାର।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Kanyā ratnaku garbhara, naṣṭa kariba tharaku thara lo jāiphula,
hṛudaye na kari pāpa bichāra.

Tikanga:
"Ka whakangaro te tangata i te kōhine (Kanya Ratna) i roto i te kōpū (Garbha) i ngā wā katoa me te kore e rongo i te hara i roto i ō rātou ngākau." E whakaatu tika ana tēnei i te wā pouri o te whakamate kōhine i roto i te kōpū i pōraru i te hapori i ngā tekau tau tata nei.

3.6 Poropititanga mō te Mate Urutā o te tau 2020 (COVID-19)

I tuhi te Mahapurush Hadidas, he whakatinanatanga o ngā Panchasakhas i whānau i te rau tau 18, i roto i 'Agata Bhavishyat Chautisha':

Odia Sloka:

ଦୁଇ ବିଂଶ ଯେବେ ବର୍ଷ ବୋଲାଇବ, ବହୁ ବିତିପାତ ହେବ।
ଅଜଣା ବ୍ୟାଧିରେ ବହୁ ନାଶ ଯିବେ, ହାହାକାର ପଡ଼ିଯିବ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Dui biṃsha jebe barsha bolāiba, bahu bitipāta heba.
Ajaṇā byādhire bahu nāsha jibe, hāhākāra paḍijiba.

Tikanga:
"Ka tae mai te tau 'Dui-Binsha' (Rua Tekau / 2020), ka puta ngā raruraru nui. Ka mate te tangata i te mate kāore i mōhiotia (Ajana Vyadhi), ā, ka puta ngā tangi o te pōuri (Haahakaar) i ngā wāhi katoa." He whakaahuatanga tino tika tēnei mō te mate urutā COVID-19 i mau i te ao i te tau 2020.

3.7 Poropititanga mō te Hirakud Dam

I roto i te tuhinga 'Chakada Madana' me 'Mahagupta Padmakalpa', i matapae ngā Hāto Achyutananda rāua ko Arkhit Das i te hanganga o tētahi raupapa nui i Odisha.

Odia Sloka:

ଛବିଶ ଠାବରେ ହୀରାକୁଦ ଉଛୁଳିବ, ଏ ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଅର୍ଦ୍ଧଭାଗ ଜଳମଗ୍ନ ହେବ।
ଧନଜନ ନଷ୍ଟ ହେବ କହିଲି ବୁଝାଇ, ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ କହଇ ବେଶୀ ବେଳ ନାହିଁ।
ହୀରାକୁଦ ବୋଲିଣଟି ଗ୍ରାମ ନାମ ଥିବ, ବନ୍ଧବାଡ଼ ଭାଙ୍ଗି ସବୁ ଏକାକାର ହେବ।
ସେହି ବେଳେ ଗଙ୍ଗା ପୁଣି ଆସିବେଟି ମାଡ଼ି, ବୈତରଣୀ, ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣୀ ଯେ ଦେବେ ଘୋର ରଡ଼ି।
ଓଡ଼ିଶାରେ ବଡ଼ ବନ୍ଧ ତିଆରି ଯେ ଥିବ, ସେଇବେଳେ ଶତ୍ରୁଦ୍ୱାରା ଭାଙ୍ଗିଣ ଯେ ଯିବ।
ଓଡ଼ିଶାରେ ଛଅ ଜିଲ୍ଲା ଜଳାର୍ଣ୍ଣବ ହୋଇ, ଯିବାପାଇଁ ବାଟ ନ ପାଇବେ ପୁଣି କେହି।
ହୀରାକୁଦ ବୋଲି ଗ୍ରାମର ଯେ ନାମ ଥିବ, ବନ୍ଧବାଡ଼ ଭାଙ୍ଗି ସବୁ ଏକ ଯେ ହୋଇବ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Chabiṣha ṭhābare Hīrākuda uchuḷiba, e Oḍiśhā arddhabhāga jaḷamagna heba.
Dhanajana naṣhṭa heba kahili bujhāi, dīna Achyuta kahai beśhī beḷa nāhiñ.
Hīrākuda boliṇaṭi grāma nāma thiba, bandhabāḍa bhāṅgi sabu ekākāra heba.
Sehi beḷe Gaṅgā puṇi āsibeṭi māḍi, Baitaraṇī, Brāhmaṇī je debe ghora raḍi.
Oḍiśhāre baḍa bandha tiāri je thiba, seibeḷe śhatrudwārā bhāṅgiṇa je jiba.
Oḍiśhāre chha-a jillā jaḷārṇnaba hoi, jibāpāiñ bāṭa na pāibe puṇi kehi.
Hīrākuda boli grāmara je nāma thiba, bandhabāḍa bhāṅgi sabu eka je hoiba.

Tikanga:
I tuhi a Achyutananda Das ji i roto i tana tuhinga Malika 'Chakada Madana' me te hāto Brahma Avadhut Arakhita Das, i whānau i te haurua tuarua o te rau tau tekau mā waru, ā, i mahi i te sadhana tino pai i te ana o Olashuni, i roto i tana tuhinga Malika 'Mahagupta Padmakalpa' ka hangaia he raupapa nui i te kāwanatanga o Odisha ko tōna ingoa ko "Hirakud". I kōrero anō ia i te wā mutunga o Kaliyuga, nā te whakaeke a ngā hoariri, ka pakaru taua raupapa, mā reira ka rukuhia e ono ngā takiwā o te kāwanatanga o Odisha, ka tū te waka, ā, ka nui te ngaronga o ngā rawa me ngā tāngata. I kī ngā Mahapurushas i taua wā anō ka whakaatu a Ganga i tōna āhua nanakia, ā, ka puta anō hoki he kino nui i ngā awa o Baitarani, Brahmani, me ētahi atu.

E mōhiotia ana, i te tau 1948 i te kāwanatanga o Odisha i Inia, i tīmata te mahi hanga o tētahi raupapa ko Hirakud te ingoa, i oti i te tau 1953. I te marama o Hānuere o te tau 1957, nā te Pirimia o taua wā, a Pandit Jawaharlal Nehru, i whakatuwheratia tēnei raupapa. Ki te roa katoa o te 26 km, i hangaia ki runga i te awa o Mahanadi i te takiwā o Sambalpur i Odisha, ko tēnei raupapa i tēnei rā te raupapa hanga-tangata roa rawa atu o te ao. I tēnei ara, i whakamatauria te tika o te wāhanga tuatahi o tēnei poropititanga.

Ka kite wawe te ao i te wāhanga tuarua o taua poropititanga e tutuki ana hoki, i te wā o te pakanga i waenganui i Inia me Haina, nā te whakaeke pere a Haina, ka pakaru tēnei raupapa, ā, ka pāngia te kāwanatanga o Odisha e te waipuke kino.

3.8 Poropititanga mō te Whānautanga o Pākistana Rau Tau i Mua

I whakahua a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das me ētahi atu i te hanganga me te ingoa o Pākistana 600 tau ki muri i roto i ngā tuhinga pērā i 'Gupta Kheda Malika' me 'Bhavishyat Malika'. Waihoki, i whakahua te hāto o te rau tau 18, a Arkhit Das, i roto i 'Mahagupta Padmakalpa'.

Odia Sloka:

ପାକିସ୍ତାନର ଦଶା ଯାହା ହେବ ସହସା, ମନ କର୍ଣ୍ଣ ଦଇ ଥରେ ଶୁଣ ସେ ଭାଷା।
ଏହି ଭାରତ ସଙ୍ଗେ ପାକିସ୍ତାନଟି ରଙ୍ଗେ, ମହାସମର କରିବ ଜାଣିଥା ବେଗେ।
ପ୍ରାଚ୍ୟ ପାଶ୍ଚାତ୍ୟ ଗଣ ଏହିପରି ମିଳିଣ, ପାକିସ୍ତାନ ଲାଗି ସର୍ବେ କରିବେ ରଣ।
ପାକିସ୍ତାନ ରାଜ୍ୟଗୋଟି ଛାରଖାର ହେବ, ଚାରିଆଡ଼େ ହାହାକାର ଶୁଭୁଣ ଯେ ଥିବ।
ଚୀନ ପାକିସ୍ତାନ ପୁଣି ଆମେରିକା ଜାଣ, ଋଷିଆ ଭାରତ ପୁଣି ଜର୍ମାନ ଜାପାନ।
ପାକିସ୍ତାନ ରାଜ୍ୟ ଗୋଟା ଛାରଖାର ହେବ, ପଛ ବେଳକୁ ତ ପୁଣି ବୁଦ୍ଧି ଯେ ସ୍ଫୁରିବ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Pākistānara dashā jāhā heba sahasā, mana karṇṇa dei thare shuṇa se bhāṣā.
Ehi Bhārata saṅge Pākistānaṭi raṅge, mahāsamara kariba jāṇithā bege.
Prāchya pāshchātya gaṇa ehipari miḷiṇa, Pākistāna lāgi sarbe karibe raṇa.
Pākistāna rājyagoṭi chhārakhāra heba, chāriāḍe hāhākāra shubhuṇa je thiba.
Chīna Pākistāna puṇi Āmerikā jāṇa, Ruṣiā Bhārata puṇi Jarmana Jāpāna.
Pākistāna rājya goṭā chhārakhāra heba, pachha beḷaku ta puṇi buddhi je sphuriba.

Tikanga:
"Ka puta ohorere mai he whenua ko Pākistana te ingoa. I ngā wā mutunga, ka whawhai kaha a Inia ki a Pākistana. Ka hono ngā mana o te Middle East me te Hauāuru; ka tautoko ngā iwi Muslim, ngā whenua o te Hauāuru, Haina, me Amerika i a Pākistana, engari ka āwhina a Ruhia, Tiamana, me Hapani i a Inia. Ka tino whakangaro a Pākistana (Chara-khara) i roto i tēnei pakanga, ā, i waenganui i te pōuri o te ao, ka mōhio ia ki ōna hapa."

He mea tika kia mōhiotia i hangaia te kupu 'Pākistana' i te tau 1933 anake, heoi, i tapaina e ēnei hāto i ngā rau tau i mua.

3.9 Ngā Poropititanga mō te Tāone o Bhubaneswar

I whakatakotoria te tūāpapa o Bhubaneswar hou i te tau 1948 e te Pirimia a Nehru. Heoi anō, ka puta tōna whakahua hei whakapaipai i roto i ngā tuhinga Malika pērā i 'Chakada Madana' me 'Kali Agata Bhabishyant'.

Odia Sloka:

ଭୈରବୀ ଡାକିବ ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର, ଚୈତ୍ର ମାସ ଯେ ମଙ୍ଗଳବାର।
ପୃଥିବୀରେ ଗୋଳ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଆରମ୍ଭିବ ଯେବେ, ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରକୁ ସୈନ୍ୟ ମାଡ଼ି ଯେ ଆସିବେ।
ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରରେ ଲାଗିବ ଗୋଳ, ଏକାମ୍ର ବନ ଯେ ଅଛି ସେଠାର।
ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ତ ପୁଣି ରାଜଧାନୀ ଜାଣ, ବିଦେଶୀ ଶତ୍ରୁଙ୍କ ଦୃଷ୍ଟି ପଡ଼ିବଟି ପୁଣ।
ଭୈରବ ଡାକ ହେବ ଜାଣ, ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରଟି ପ୍ରମାଣ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Bhairabī ḍākiba Bhubaneshwar, Chaitra māsa je Maṅgaḷabāra.
Pṛuthibīre goḷa yuddha ārambhiba jebe, Bhubaneshwaraku sainya māḍi je āsibe.
Bhubaneshwarare lāgiba goḷa, Ekāmra bana je achhi seṭhāra.
Bhubaneshwar ta puṇi rājadhānī jāṇa, bideshī shatruṅka dṛuṣṭi paḍibaṭi puṇa.
Bhairaba ḍāka heba jāṇa, Bhubaneshwarati pramāṇa.

Tikanga:
I matapae ngā hāto ka rangona he tangi kaha (te ngunguru o Bhairavi) i Bhubaneswar i tētahi Rātū i te marama o Chaitra. I te wā o te Pakanga o te Ao Tuatoru, ka tae ngā ope hoariri ki Bhubaneswar (Ekamra Bana). I whakahua mārama ngā tuhinga ko Bhubaneswar te whakapaipai, ā, he whāinga mō ngā hoariri tauiwi.

3.10 Poropititanga mō te Kaiako Wairua Rongonui a Shri Shri Thakur Anukulchandra

I matapae a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, i roto i āna tuhinga Malika i tuhia tata ki te 600 tau ki muri, i te whānautanga o tētahi "Yugapurusha" (Tangata o te Wā) ka mahi i ngā mahi atua maha, ā, ka horapa tōna rongo ki te ao katoa.

Odia Sloka:

ଲୀଳାମାନ ହେବ କୀର୍ତ୍ତି ରହି ଯିବ, ଅନୁକୂଳ ନାମ ଧରି।
ଦେଶ ବିଦେଶରୁ ଭକ୍ତ ଆସିବେ, ସତସଙ୍ଗ ଆଶ୍ରା କରି।
ସତନାମ ଗୋଟି ଉଚ୍ଚାରି ବୋଲିବେ, ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ହେବ ମେଳ।
ଶୂନ୍ୟ ମାର୍ଗେ ରହି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦେଖିବ, ଯୁଗପୁରୁଷଙ୍କ ଖେଳ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Līḷāmāna heba kīrtti rahi jiba, Anukūḷa nāma dhari.
Desha bidesharu bhakata āsibe, sat-saṅga āshrā kari.
Satanāma goṭi uchchāri bolibe, bhaktaṅka heba meḷa.
Shūnya mārge rahi Achyuta dekhiba, Yugapuruṣaṅka kheḷa.

Tikanga:
I tuhi te Mahapurush ka puta mai he hāto ko "Anukul" te ingoa, ā, ka haere mai ngā ākonga tapu mai i Inia me tāwāhi ki te rapu piringa i roto i tōna "Satsanga." Ka karakiatia te ingoa tapu (Satnaam), ā, ka puta he huihuinga nui o ngā ākonga tapu. I whakahua anō a Achyutananda Das ko ia tonu, e noho ana i te ao ngaro/kore (Shunya Maarga), ka kite i ngā mahi atua o tēnei Yugapurusha.

Hei whakatutuki i tēnei poropititanga, i whānau a Anukulchandra Chakravarty i te 14 o Hepetema, 1888, i te rohe o Pabna i Bengal kāore anō kia wehea (inaianei ko Bangladesh). E mōhiotia ana puta noa i te ao ko Shri Shri Thakur Anukulchandra, nāna i whakatū te 'Satsang Ashram' ki Deoghar, Jharkhand, i te tau 1946. He miriona ngā kaiwhaiwhai puta noa i te ao i uru ki tana kaupapa, he maha ngā wā i karanga ai rātou ki a ia ko 'Yuga Purushottam'.

3.11 Te Whakahua i te Poma Ngota i roto i ngā Tuhinga Malika

Ahakoa i whakawhanakehia te poma ngota i waenganui o te rautau 20, i whakahua a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das (i roto i te 'Gupta Kheda Malika' me te 'Bhavishyat Malika') me Balarama Das (i roto i te 'Kali Tandaba Gita') i taua mea i ngā rau tau ki muri.

Odia Sloka:

ପରମାଣୁ ଯେ ବୋମା କରିଦେବେଟି ଜମା, ପୋଡ଼ିଜାଳି ଦେବା ପାଇଁ ଭାରତ ସୀମା।
ପରମାଣୁ ଯେ ବୋମା ଯାହା ଲାଗି ଗାରିମା, ଦେଖାଇ ହୁଅନ୍ତି ଆଜି ପାଶ୍ଚାତ୍ୟ ସେନା।
ତାହା ଫୁଟିବ ନାହିଁ କେଣେ ଯିବ ମିଳାଇ, ଏହା ଦେଖି ବିଦେଶୀଏ ଯିବେ ପଳାଇ।
ଅଗ୍ନିର ଦାହିକା ଶକ୍ତି ଲୋ ବଉଳ ପ୍ରଭୁ କରିବେ ହରଣ, ପରମାଣୁ ବୋମା ଯାହାଲାଗି ଗାରିମା ଶୀତଳ ହୋଇବ ଜାଣ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Paramāṇu je bomā karidebeṭi jamā, poḍijāḷi debā pāiñ Bhārata sīmā.
Paramāṇu je bomā jāhā lāgi gārimā, dekhāi huanti āji pāshchātya senā.
Tāhā phuṭiba nāhiñ keṇe jiba miḷāi, ehā dekhi bideshīe jibe paḷāi.
Agnira dāhikā shakti lo bauḷa Prabhu karibe haraṇa, paramāṇu bomā jāhā lāgi gārimā shītaḷa hoiba jāṇa.

Tikanga:
I tuhia e ngā tapu i te wā o te Pakanga o te Ao Tuatoru, ka whakatakoto rautaki ngā hoariri o Inia ki te whakangaro i ngā rohe o Inia mā te whakamahi i ngā Poma Ngota (Paramanu Bomba). Heoi, ka kore e tutuki tā rātou mahere nā te mea ka horomia e te Ariki Kalki te kaha wera/whakangaro (Dahika Shakti) o ēnei poma. Ko ngā patu tonu e whakahīhīhia ana e ngā ope o te Hauāuru me ngā hoariri ka "mātao" (kore mahi) ā, karekau he pānga ki te whenua o Inia. I te kitenga i tēnei mahi kore e taea, ka oma ngā hoariri o tāwāhi i te wehi. E whakatūpato ana ngā tuhinga ka puta he pakanga karihi whakamataku (Anu-Paramanu Yuddha) ā muri ake nei, ka tata kore e nohoia te Ao, ka kitea e ngā kaiwhaiwhai.

3.12 Te Whakahua i a Madan Mohan Malaviya, Mahatma Gandhi, me Motilal Nehru

I whānau i te tau 1850, i hoatu e te tapu nui a Bhima Bhoi (he kaiwhaiwhai i te tikanga Panchasakhas) ngā kōrero mō ngā kaiārahi o te kaupapa herekore o Inia i roto i tana tuhinga 'Padmakalpa'.

Odia Sloka:

ମଦନମାଲବ୍ୟ ନାମ ବୋଲିକରି ଏକ ଭକ୍ତ ଥିବ ଜନ୍ମି,
ଗାନ୍ଧୀ ସହିତରେ ଅହିଂସା ଧର୍ମରେ ନିରନ୍ତରେ ଥିବେ ପୁଣି।
ଚୁଡ଼ଙ୍ଗ ବଂଶରେ ଉତପତ୍ତି ହୋଇ ମୋତିଲାଲ ଯେ ନେହେରୁ,
ମହାପ୍ରତାପୀ ଅଟଇ ସେହୁ ଭକ୍ତ ଆସିଛନ୍ତି ଶୂନ୍ୟପୁରୁ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Madanamālabya nāma bolikari eka bhakta thiba janmi,
Gāndhī sahitare ahinsā dharmare nirantare thibe puṇi.
Chuḍaṅga baṃshare utpatti hoi Motilāl je Neheru,
mahāpratāpī aṭai sehu bhakta āsichanti Shūnyapuru.

Tikanga:
I matapae te tuhinga i te whānautanga o tētahi kaiwhaiwhai ko "Madan Mohan Malaviya" te ingoa, nāna, me "Mahatma Gandhi," i ārahi i tētahi kaupapa i runga i te karakia o te kore-tūkino (Ahimsa). I tua atu, i whakahua i te whānautanga o te kaiwhaiwhai tino whai mana, kaha hoki, a "Motilal Nehru." Ko rātou tokotoru i noho hei kaiārahi matua o te Indian National Congress me te pakanga herekore.

3.13 Te Poropititanga o te Kohurutanga o Mahatma Gandhi e Nathuram Godse

I roto i te 'Jaiphula Malika', i matapae a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i te matenga o te Mahatma.

Odia Sloka:

ମୋହନ ଗାନ୍ଧୀ ମରଣ, ନାଥୁରାମ ହସ୍ତେ ହୋଇବ ଜାଣ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ଏ କଥା ସିଦ୍ଧ ଫଳ ରେ ନିପୁଣ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Mohana Gāndhī maraṇa, Nāthurāma haste hoiba jāṇa lo jāiphula,
e kathā siddha phaḷa re nipuṇa.

Tikanga:
"Ka mate a Mohan Gandhi i ngā ringa o Nathuram." E ai ki ngā rekoata o te hītori, i te 30 o Hānuere, 1948, i kohurutia e Nathuram Vinayak Godse a Mahatma Gandhi.

3.14 Te Rangatiratanga o Inia i raro i te Kaihautū o Mahatma Gandhi

I roto i te 'Kali Bhagabata', i whakaahua a Mahapurush Abhiram Paramahansa (he tapu o te rautau 20 i roto i te tikanga Malika) i te panonitanga tōrangapū.

Odia Sloka:

ସତ୍ୟାଗ୍ରହୀ ହୋଇଲେ ମେଳି ।
ଅନ୍ତର ହୋଇଯିବ କଳି ।।
ଇଉରୋପିଆନ ଯିବେ ଛାଡ଼ି ।
ମୋହନ ସେନା ଦେବେ କାଢ଼ି ।।
ପ୍ରଥମ ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ସେନାପତି ।
ହେବେ ମୋହନ ସୈନ୍ୟ ଘେତି ।।
ମୋହନ ବୋଲି ଯାଙ୍କୁ କହି ।
ମହତ ଆତ୍ମା ଅଟେ ସେହି ।।
ସେହି ନାରଦ ମହାମୁନ୍ୟ ।
ତାଙ୍କୁ କରନ୍ତି ସର୍ବେ ମାନ୍ୟ ।।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Satyāgrahī hoile meḷi,
antara hoijiba kaḷi.
Iuropiāna jibe chhāḍi,
Mohana senā debe kāḍhi.
Prathama yuddhe senāpati,
hebe Mohana sainya gheti.
Mohana boli jāṅku kahi,
mahata ātmā aṭe sehi.
Sehi Nārada mahāmunya,
tāṅku karanti sarbe mānya.

Tikanga:
I matapae te tapu mā te huihuinga o ngā Satyagrahis, ka akiakihia ngā Pākehā kia wehe. I tautohu ia i a "Mohan" (Gandhi) hei kaihautū/ārahi o tēnei pakanga nui tuatahi mō te rangatiratanga. I whakaahua anō ia i a Mahatma Gandhi hei wairua nui (Mahatma) me tētahi whakatinanatanga o te Narada, ka whakahōnorehia āna kupu e te katoa. I tutuki tēnei i te mea ko te kaihautū o Gandhi te kaha matua i arahina ai ngā Piritana kia wehe atu i te whenua o Inia.

4 Te Tau o Kaliyuga

E ai ki ngā karaipiture Sanatan, ko te tau katoa o Kaliyuga he 432,000 tau. E whakapūmau ana hoki te Bhavishya Malika i tēnei roanga katoa, engari e tāpiri ana i tētahi poropititanga nui: nā te nui rawa o ngā hara o te tangata, e kore a Kaliyuga e oti i tōna wā katoa.

4.1 Te Whakahau Karaipiture mō te 432,000 Tau

E tuhi ana a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i te 'Adi Samhita':

Odia Sloka:

କଳିରେ ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ଯାଏ, ବତିଶ ସହସ୍ର ନିର୍ଣ୍ଣୟ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Kaḷire chāri lakṣa jāe, batiśa sahasra nirṇaya.

Tikanga:
"I roto i Kaliyuga, e whā rau mano me toru tekau mā rua mano (432,000) tau kua whakatauhia hei roanga o tōna oranga."

Kātahi ka tuhia e Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga, 'Dashapatala Malika':

Odia Sloka:

କଳିଯୁଗ ଭୋଗ ପ୍ରମାଣ ସାର,
ଚାରିଲକ୍ଷ ଯେ ବତିଶ ହଜାର।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Kaliyuga bhoga pramāṇa sāra,
chārilakṣha je batisha hajāra.

Tikanga:
Arā, ko te roanga oranga kua whakaritea mō Kali Yuga he whā rau mano, toru tekau mā rua mano tau.

E tuhi ana ia i roto i tana tuhinga Malika, 'Parardha Jataka':

Odia Sloka:

ଚାରିଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶି ସହସ୍ର କଳି,
କରିଲି ତିନି ଯୁଗଠାରୁ ବଳି।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Chārilakṣha batishi sahasra kali,
karili tini yugaṭhāru bali.

Tikanga:
Arā, ko te roanga oranga o Kali Yuga he whā rau mano, toru tekau mā rua mano tau.

Heoi, kāore ngā Mahapurushas i mutu i muri i te kī noa i tēnei; i kī hoki rātou i roto i ā rātou tuhinga Malika nā te maha o ngā hara i mahia e te tangata, e kore a Kali Yuga e oti i tōna roanga katoa.

4.2 He aha ka mutu wawe ai a Kaliyuga

E whakamārama ana ngā Panchasakhas ahakoa ko te tau ariā he 432,000 tau, ko te "Bhoga" (te roanga tūturu i wheakoa) ka poto rawa atu nā te taumaha o ngā hara. I roto i te 'Dashapatala Malika', e tuhia ana:

Odia Sloka:

ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ଯେ ବତିଶ ସହସ୍ର, ଭୋଗ ନୋହିବ କହ ପୀତବାସ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Chāri lakṣa je batiśa sahasra, bhoga nohiba kaha Pītabāsa.

Tikanga:
"E te Ariki Pitabasa (Jagannath)! Kōrerotia mai ki a mātou me pēhea e kore ai a Kaliyuga e taea te wheako i tōna wā katoa o te 432,000 tau."

E tuhi ana te Mahapurush i roto i tana tuhinga Malika, 'Kalikalpa Gita':

Odia Sloka:

ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶ ସସ୍ର ବର୍ଷ ଆଉ,
ଭୋଗ ନହେବ ପାପଭାରା ଯହୁଁ।
ଆପଣେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ହୋଇବେ ଉଦିତ,
ପାପୀମାନଙ୍କୁ କରିବେ ମୁକତ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Chāri lakṣha batisha sasra barṣha āu,
bhoga naheba pāpabhārā jahuṃ.
Āpaṇe prabhu hoibe udita,
pāpīmānaṃku karibe mukata.

Tikanga:
Arā, nā te taumaha nui o ngā hara i Kali Yuga, e kore a Kali Yuga e taea te wheako i tōna roanga oranga o te 4,32,000 tau. Ahakoa i mua i tēnei wā, ka whakatinana te Ariki, ka whakangaroa ngā tangata hara, ā, ka whakatūria te Dharma.

E tuhi anō ana a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tētahi o āna tuhinga Malika:

Odia Sloka:

କଳି ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶ ସସ୍ର ନୋହିବ ଭୋଗ,
ପାପ ଯୋଗୁଁ କ୍ଷୟ ହୋଇବ ଅଳ୍ପ ପଡ଼ିବ ଭାଗ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Kali chāri lakṣha batisha sasra nohiba bhoga,
pāpa joguṃ kṣhaya hoiba alpa paḍiba bhāga.

Tikanga:
Arā, e kore a Kali Yuga e taea te wheako i tōna wā kua whakaritea o te 4,32,000 tau. Nā ngā hara, ka pirau tōna roanga oranga, arā, e kore e taea te wheako katoa.

4.3 Mēnā ehara i te 4,32,000 tau, he aha te roanga oranga i wheakoa o Kali Yuga?

I ēnei rā, kei te horahia e ētahi tāngata, e kī ana i te Malika, ko te roanga oranga i wheakoa o Kali Yuga he 10,000 tau. Kia mōhiotia he tino hē tēnei.

I tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika, 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta':

Odia Sloka:

ଦଶ ସହସ୍ରରେ କଳିନାଶ ହେବ ଏ ଅଟେ ବିଷମ ବାଣୀ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Dasha sahasrare kalināsha heba e aṭe biṣhama bāṇī.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka mutu a Kali Yuga i roto i te 10,000 tau – he kōrero kōhikohiko, teka rānei tēnei.

Kua tuhia e ngā Panchasakha Mahapurushas i ngā wāhi maha i roto i ā rātou tuhinga Malika ko te roanga oranga i wheakoa o Kali Yuga he 5,000 tau, i muri iho ka whakatinana te Ariki Kalki, ā, ka tae mai a Satya-Kali, Ananta Yuga rānei, Adya Satya Yuga rānei.

Haere mai, kia whiwhi tātou i ētahi atu kōrero mō tēnei kaupapa takitahi mā ngā sloka o te Malika.

Tuatahi, kia mōhio tātou ki ngā kōrero a te Ariki Shri Krishna tonu mō te roanga oranga o Kali Yuga.

4.4 Te Roanga Tūturu: 5,000 Tau

He rerekē ki ngā kōrero hē i horahia e ētahi e kī ana e 10,000 tau te roa o Kaliyuga, e kī ana ngā tuhinga Malika i ngā wā katoa ka mutu te mana tūturu o Kali i muri i te 5,000 tau, ka whai muri mai he wā whakawhiti.

E kōrero ana a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i ngā kupu a Te Ariki Krishna ki a Garuda i roto i te 'Agata Bhavishya Prabandha Malika':

Odia Sloka:

କଳି ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶ ସହସ୍ର ନୋହିବ ଭୋଗ, ପାପ ଯୋଗୁ କ୍ଷୟ ହୋଇବ ଅଳ୍ପ ପଡ଼ିବ ଭାଗ।
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଯେ ପଚାଶ ଭୋଗ ହେବ ନିକର, ଏଥୁ କିଛି ଉଣା ହୋଇବ ଶୁଣ ଗରୁଡ଼ ବୀର।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Kaḷi chāri lakṣa batiśa sahasra nohiba bhoga, pāpa jogu kṣaya hoiba alpa paḍiba bhāga.
Pāñcha sahasra je pachāsha bhoga heba nikara, ethu kichi uṇā hoiba shuṇa Garuḍa bīra.

Tikanga:
"E Garuda toa! Whakarongo mai, e kore a Kaliyuga e roa ki te 432,000 tau. Nā te hara, ka heke tōna roanga. Ka mutu tata ki te 5,050 tau.

4.5 Te Whakatairite o Ravana

Odia Sloka:

ରାମଦାସ ବୋଲଇ ହୋ ଶୁଣ ଗୁରୁଦେବ, କଳିଯୁଗ କେତେ ପୁଣ ଭୋଗ ଯେ ହୋଇବ।
ଚାରିଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶ ଯେ ସହସ୍ର ବରଷ, କେମନ୍ତେ ଯେ ଯୁଗ ପୁଣ ହୋଇବଟି ଶେଷ।
ଏଡ଼େ ବଡ଼ ଯୁଗ ପୁଣ ବିନାଶ ହୋଇବ, ସଂଶୟ ଫେଡ଼ିଣ ମୋତେ କହ ଗୁରୁଦେବ।
ରାମଦାସ ବାଣୀ ଶୁଣି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ କହନ୍ତି, ତୋତେ କହୁଅଛି ରାମ ଶୁଣ ଦେଇ ମତି।
ରାବଣ ଯେ ତ୍ରେତୟା ଯୁଗରେ ତପ କଲା, ଛପନ ଗଣ୍ଡା ଯୁଗକୁ ବର ସେ ପାଇଲା।
ପାପେ ଗଲା ତାର ତିନି ଭାଗ ଯେ ବିନାଶ, ଏକଭାଗ ଭୋଗ କଲା ବିଶ୍ରବାର ଶିଷ୍ୟ।
ସ୍ୱର୍ଗେ ପଶି ଅପସରୀ ମାନଙ୍କୁ ହରିଲା, ପର ଦ୍ରବ୍ୟ ହରି ଯେବେ ପାଦେ ଖଟାଇଲା।
ଆପେ ଜଗତମାତା ଯେ ଜନକ ନନ୍ଦିନୀ, ତାଙ୍କୁ ହରି ନେଲା ଗର୍ବେ ମୂଢ଼ ବିଂଶପାଣି।
ସୁରାପାନ କଲା ସେ କ୍ଷତ୍ରିଙ୍କୁ ବଧିଲା, ବ୍ରହ୍ମହତ୍ୟା ଆଦିକରି କେତେ ପାପ କଲା।
ପାପରୁ ଯେ ତିନି ଭାଗ ହୋଇଲା ବିନାଶ, ଏକ ଭାଗ ଭୋଗ କଲା ବିଶ୍ରବାର ଶିଷ୍ୟ।
ସେହିପରି କଳିଯୁଗ ବିନାଶ ହୋଇବ, ପାଞ୍ଚସସ୍ର ଭୋଗ ହେବ ଅନ୍ୟ ପାପେ ଯିବ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Rāmadāsa bolai ho shuṇa gurudeba, kaliyuga kete puṇa bhoga je hoiba.
Chārilakṣha batisha je sahasra baraṣha, kemaṃte je yuga puṇa hoibaṭi sheṣha.
Eḍe baḍa yuga puṇa bināsha hoiba, saṃshaya pheriṇa mote kaha gurudeba.
Rāmadāsa bāṇī shuṇi Achyuta kahaṃti, tote kahuachi Rāma shuṇa dei mati.
Rābaṇa je tretayā yugare tapa kalā, chapana gaṇḍā yugaku bara se pāilā.
Pāpe galā tāra tini bhāga je vināsha, ekabhāga bhoga kalā Bishrabāra shiṣhya.
Swarge pashi apasarī mānaṃku harilā, para drabya hari jebe pāde khaṭāilā.
Āpe jagatamātā je Janaka naṃdinī, tāṃku hari nelā garve mūḍha biṃshapāṇi.
Surāpāna kalā se kṣhatriṃṃku badhilā, brahmahatyā ādi kari kete pāpa kalā.
Pāparu je tini bhāga hoilā bināsha, eka bhāga bhoga kalā bishrabāra shiṣhya.
Sehipari kaliyuga bināsha hoiba, pāṃchasasra bhoga heba anya pāpe jiba.

I roto i te 'Kalpatika', e whakamārama ana a Achyutananda Das i tēnei āhuatanga mā te whakamahi i te tauira o Ravana. I mahi a Ravana i te tino tūpato, ā, i whiwhi i te roanga o te ora o "56 Ganda" (he roanga kōpiko nui). Heoi, nā te mea i kahakina e ia a Whaea Sita, i whakaiti i ngā atua wahine o te rangi, ā, i mahi i ngā hara kino pērā i te Brahman-hatya, kotahi anake te wāhanga o tana manaakitanga i pārekareka ki a ia, i te wā i whakangaromia ngā wāhanga e toru e ōna hara.

Waihoki, ko te roanga o Kaliyuga e 432,000 tau kei te "kainga" e te pikinga tere o te hara o te ao, e arahi ana ki tōna mutunga wawe i muri i te 5,000 tau o te wā i pā, e para ana i te huarahi mō te Ananta Yuga, te Satya Yuga rānei.

4.6 Te Tau o Kaliyuga: Te Tātari Whakamārama

I tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tētahi o ana tuhinga Malika:

Odia Sloka:

ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ଯେ ବତିଶ ସହସ୍ର,
କଳିଯୁଗର ଅଟଇ ଆୟୁଷ।
ପାପଭାରରେ ଆୟୁ କଟିଯିବ,
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର କଳି ଭୋଗ ହୋଇବ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Chāri lakṣa je batiśa sahasra,
Kaḷiyugara aṭai āyuṣa.
Pāpabhārare āyu kaṭijiba,
Pāñcha sahasra Kaḷi bhoga hoiba.

Tikanga:
Ko te tikanga, ko te tau o Kaliyuga he whā rau toru tekau mā rua mano (432,000) tau, engari nā te awe o ngā hara, ka poto tēnei tau, ā, ka mutu i roto i te rima mano (5,000) tau noa iho.

He whakahokinga mai o Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, i whānau i te rautau 18 i te rohe o Jajpur i Odisha, i kōrero a Hāto Mahapurush Hadi Das mō te tau o Kaliyuga i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ko ‘Kali Chautisha’ te ingoa. I tuhi ia:

Odia Sloka:

ସଂବତ୍ସର ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର କଳି ହୋଇବ ଶେଷ,
ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗ ଆଦ୍ୟ ହୋଇବ ଶୁଭଯୋଗେ ପ୍ରବେଶ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Saṃbatsara pāñcha sahasra Kaḷi hoiba sheṣa,
Satya yuga ādya hoiba shubhajoge prabesha.

Tikanga:
Ko te tikanga, i roto i te "Sambatsara Paancha Sahasra" (5,000 tau), ka mutu a Kaliyuga, ā, i muri i tērā, ka tīmata te Adya Satya Yuga i te wā pai.

I tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tētahi atu tuhinga Malika:

Odia Sloka:

ପାଞ୍ଚ ଯେ ହଜାର ଥାରୁ ମହାଭୟ ହେବ,
ଦିଲ୍ଲୀ ସମ୍ରାଟକୁ ଆସି ବିପଦ ପଡ଼ିବ।
ଗାନ୍ଧାର ସେନାଟି ଗୋଳ କରିବ ଅବଶ୍ୟ,
ପାଞ୍ଚ ହଜାର ପଚାଶ କଳିର ଆୟୁଷ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Pāñcha je hajāra thāru mahābhaya heba,
Dillī samrāṭaku āsi bipada paḍiba.
Gāndhāra senāṭi goḷa kariba abashya,
Pāñcha hajāra pachāsha Kaḷira āyuṣa.

Tikanga:
E tuhi ana hoki a Mahapurush i konei e muri i te 5,000 tau o Kaliyuga, ka puta he āhuarangi o te wehi nui; ka hinga ngā aituā ki runga i te Emepara o Delhi, te Pirimia rānei o te whenua. Ka tino whawhai te ope taua o Gandhara (Taliban) ki Inia. E tuhi anō a Mahapurush e puta katoa ēnei i muri i te mutunga o Kaliyuga, arā, i muri i te paunga o te 5,050 tau o Kaliyuga.

I tuhi hoki a Achyutananda Das ji i tētahi irārangi i roto i tana tuhinga Malika i kore ai ia i kī tika i te tau o Kaliyuga hei 5,000, hei 5,050 rānei, engari i kōrero mōna mā te tohu huna:

Odia Sloka:

ବିଂଶେ ମସିହା ହୋଇବ ଶେଷ,
ଅନନ୍ତ କରିବେ କଳି ବିନାଶ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Biṃshe masiha hoiba sheṣa,
Ananta karibe Kaḷi bināsha.

Tikanga:
Ko te tikanga, i ngā '20s'—mai i te tau rua mano (2000) ki te rua mano iwa tekau mā iwa (2099)—i tētahi wā i roto i tēnei wā, ka mutu a Kaliyuga. Ka whakangaromia e Te Ariki Ananta a Kaliyuga i te Ao.

Tētahi o ngā Panchasakhas, a Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das, i whakamārama i te tau o Kaliyuga i roto i te taipitopito i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Aagata Bhavishya Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ବାରଙ୍ଗ ବୋଲଇ ଶୁଣିମା ଗୋସାଇଁ କୁହ ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ବିଚାର,
କେତେବେଳେ କଳ୍କୀ ସ୍ୱରୂପ ହୋଇବେ ଶୁଣଇଁ ମୁଖୁ ତୁମ୍ଭର।
ଶିଶୁ ବୋଲନ୍ତି ହେ ଶୁଣିମା ବାରଙ୍ଗ କଳଙ୍କି ସ୍ୱରୂପ ତୁହି,
ଯୁଗଶନ୍ଧି ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ବରଷ ଯେବେ ଯିବ ଭୋଗ ହୋଇ।
ଜେସେନେକେ ନିଶି ପାହିଲେ ପ୍ରଭାତ ଯୁଗଶନ୍ଧି ଏହା ଜାଣ,
ଯେମନ୍ତ ସମୟେ କଳଙ୍କି ସ୍ୱରୂପ ହୋଇବେ ସେ ନାରାୟଣ।
ସମକ୍ଷର ବତା ଶୁଣି ଆଣିକରି ପ୍ରମାଣ ଏହାକୁ କର,
ସବୁ ଏକଠାବେ ମିଶାଇ କରିଣ କରିବୁ ପାଞ୍ଚ ହଜାର।
ଏହି ସମୟକୁ ତଳେ କରିଥିବୁ କହଇ ହୋ ବାବୁ ତୋତେ,
ଠିକ ରେ ଏ କଥା ଦେଖାଇ କହିଲୁ ରଖିଥିବୁ ହୃଦଗତେ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Bāraṅga bolai shuṇimā gosāiṃ kuha bhabiṣya bichāra,
ketebeḷe Kalkī swarūpa hoibe shuṇaiṃ mukhu tumbhara.
Shishu bolanti he shuṇimā Bāraṅga Kaḷaṅki swarūpa tuhi,
yugashandhi pāñcha sahasra baraṣa jebe jiba bhoga hoi.
Jeseneke nishi pāhile prabhāta yugashandhi ehā jāṇa,
jemanta samaye Kaḷaṅki swarūpa hoibe se Nārāyaṇa.
Samakṣara batā shuṇi āṇikari pramāṇa ehāku kara,
sabu ekaṭhābe mishāi kariṇa karibu pāñcha hajāra.
Ehi samayaku taḷe karithibu kahai ho bābu tote,
Ṭhika re e kathā dekhāi kahilu rakhithibu hṛudagate.

Tikanga:
E kī ana te akonga a Baranga Das ki a Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das—"E Guru Dev, kōrerotia mai ki ahau mō te anamata. E hiahia ana ahau ki te whakarongo mai i tōu waha i te wā ka puta Te Ariki i Tōna āhua Kalki."

I te rongonga i tēnei, e kōrero ana a Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das ki tana akonga matua a Baranga e ka puta te 'Yuga-Sandhi' (whakawhiti i waenga i ngā tau), kātahi anake ka mau Te Ariki i Tōna whakahokinga mai o Kalki. E whakamārama anō ana ia, e kī ana ia e puta tēnei Yuga-Sandhi i te wā kua pā a Kaliyuga mō te 5,000 tau. Pērā i te ata ka puta i muri i te pō, ka puta tētahi atu tau i muri i tētahi tau. Ko te wāhanga waenga i waenga i ngā tau e rua ka kīia ko Yuga-Sandhi. Ā, i roto i tēnei Yuga-Sandhi tonu, ka mau a Narayana i Tōna āhua Kalki. Ka puta tēnei Yuga-Sandhi i muri i te paunga o te 5,000 tau o Kaliyuga.

I a Achyutananda ji e kī ana i te tau o Kaliyuga i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Kaliyuga Malika’, e kī ana ia:

Odia Sloka:

ଚହଟିବ ଲୀଳା ତୁ ଚାରିରେ ମିଶା ଏକ,
ଚଢ଼ା ତିନି ଶୂନ ତହିଁ ଯେତେ ହେଲା ଠିକ।
ଚଳିଯିବ ଘୋର କଳି ଦଳି ଦେବେ ମିଳି,
ଚେତାଇ ଗୀତ ଛନ୍ଦଇ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ସେ ଭାଳି।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

chahaṭiba līḷā tu chārire mishā eka,
chaḍhā tini shūna tahiñ jete helā ṭhika.
Chalijiba ghora Kaḷi daḷi debe miḷi,
chetāi gīta chhandai Achyuta se bhāḷi.

Tikanga:
Ko te tikanga, tāpirihia te kotahi ki te tau whā (4+1=5), ā, waiho kia toru ngā kore ki te taha o te tapeke; ko te tau i whiwhi ai e tohu ana i te wā i muri iho ka mutu te wā o te Kaliyuga whakamataku. I roto i te irārangi i runga ake nei, e kōrero ana a Mahapurush i te tau o Kaliyuga mā te tohu. E kī ana ia 4+1=5, ā, ko te waiho i ngā kore e toru ki te taha o te 5 te tikanga e muri i te paunga o te 5,000 tau o Kaliyuga, ka mutu tērā tau.

I roto i tētahi atu tuhinga Malika ‘Bhavishya Chautisha’, e kī ana ia ki tana akonga a Ramachandra:

Odia Sloka:

ଠିକଣା ଅମରପୁର,
ଠାକୁର ତହିଁରୁ ହେବେ ବାହାର,
ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ହେ, ଠାରି ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଧର।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Ṭhikaṇā Amarapura,
Ṭhākura tahiñru hebe bāhāra, Rāmachandra he,
ṭhāri pāñcha sahasra dhara.

Tikanga:
Ko te tikanga, e te akonga a Ramachandra, ka puta te whakahokinga mai o Te Ariki Kalki i Amarapur (te rohe e kore e mate—e noho ana i mua noa atu i te Satya Yuga, arā, Jajpur), ā, ka puta tēnei i muri i te paunga o te 5,000 tau o Kaliyuga.

Kātahi, i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Kali Bayalisha’, e kōrero ana a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das mō te tau o Kaliyuga mā ngā tohu. E kī ana a Mahapurush:

Odia Sloka:

ଦଶରଥ ସୁତ ମୁଣ୍ଡରେ ତିନି ଅଙ୍କ ଚଢ଼ିବ,
ବଜ୍ର ଅଙ୍କ ଦେଇ ଏଗାର ଅଙ୍କ ତହୁଁ ଫେରିବ।
ଶେଷ ଡାହାଣ ଭାଗ ଯାହା ହେଲା ଗାଣ୍ଡିବ ଗୁଣ,
କଳି ଶେଷ ଭୋଗ ଏହିଟି କହିଲି ଅରଜୁନ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Dasharatha suta muṇḍare tini aṅka chaḍhiba,
bajra aṅka dei egāra aṅka tahuṃ pheriba.
Sheṣa ḍāhāṇa bhāga jāhā helā gāṇḍiba guṇa,
kaḷi sheṣa bhoga ehiṭi kahili Arajuna.

Tikanga:
Ko te tikanga, tuhia te tau 3 ki runga ake i te maha o ngā tama a Kingi Dasharatha (arā, 4^3 = 64). Kātahi ka kī a Mahapurush kia whakahurihia, ā, tangohia te 11 mai i tērā tau (Ko te whakahuri i te 64 ka hoatu 46, ā, 46 - 11 = 35). E kī ana a Mahapurush kia whakareatia te mati kei te taha matau o tēnei tau i whiwhi ai ki te 'Gandiva' (arā, 1000), ā, e tuhi ana ka mutu a Kaliyuga i te tau i whiwhi ai, i te 5,000 tau rānei.
Tuhipoka: I konei, mā te 'Gandiva', e tohu ana a Mahapurush ki tōna awhe patu, he 1,000 Yojanas.

E tuhi ana a Achyutananda Das ji i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Adi Sanhita’:

Odia Sloka:

ଏ ଯେ ସୁବାହୁ ଯୁଗ କଳି,
କ୍ଷୀଣ ଆୟୁଷ ମହାବଳି।
ପାପେ ସକଳ କ୍ଷୟ ଯିବ,
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଭୋଗ ହେବ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

E je subāhu yuga kaḷi,
kṣīṇa āyuṣa mahābaḷi.
pāpe sakaḷa kṣaya jiba,
pāñcha sahasra bhoga heba.

Tikanga:
Ko te tikanga, ko te tau 432,000 tau o Kaliyuga ka heke ki te 5,000 tau noa iho nā ngā hara.

E tuhi ana a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Kali Kalpa Gita’:

Odia Sloka:

କଳି ପାଞ୍ଚସହସ୍ର ବରଷ ଭୋଗ ହେବ,
ସେହି ଦିନୁ ସତ୍ୟକଳି ପ୍ରକାଶ ପାଇବ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Kaḷi pāñchasahasra baraṣa bhoga heba,
sehi dinu Satyakali prakāsha pāiba.

Tikanga:
Ko te tikanga, ko te wheako o Kaliyuga ka 5,000 tau te roa. I muri iho, ka puta a 'Satyakali'—te mārama o Adya Satya Yuga i roto i Kaliyuga.

E tuhi ana a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

କଳି ପାଞ୍ଚ ହଜାରରେ ଗଙ୍ଗାଦେବୀ ମେଳ କଟକ ଠାରେ,
ଭକ୍ତ ଦେଖିବେ ନେତ୍ରରେ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Kaḷi pāñchahajārare Gaṅgādebī meḷa Kaṭaka ṭhāre,
bhakta dekhibe netrare.

Tikanga:
Ko te tikanga, i muri i te paunga o te 5,000 tau o Kaliyuga, ka rere a Atua Ganga mā Cuttack; ko te tikanga, ka rukuhia ngā mea katoa mai i Kolkata ki Cuttack; ka puta he waipuke whakamataku.

I tuhi a Mahapurush i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Kaliyuga Bhavishya Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର କଳି ହଟେ ସତ୍ୟ ଉପୁଜିବ,
ମୋର ତଥ୍ୟମାନ ସେତେବେଳେଟି ହୋଇବ।
ପୂର୍ବ ପଶ୍ଚିମ ଉତ୍ତର ଆବର ଦକ୍ଷିଣ,
ପ୍ରକାଶ ହେବେ କଳଙ୍କି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Pāñcha sahasra Kaḷi hāte satya upujiba,
mora tathyamāna setebeḷeṭi hoiba.
pūrba pashchima uttara ābara dakṣiṇa,
prakāsha hebe Kaḷaṅki Achyuta bachana.

Tikanga:
Ko te tikanga, i muri i te paunga o te 5,000 tau o Kaliyuga, ka mutu a Kaliyuga, ā, ka tae mai te Pono (Satya). Kātahi, ka puta te mārama o Te Ariki Kalki i ngā ahunga katoa—Te Rāwhiti, Te Uru, Te Raki, me Te Tonga.

E tuhi anō ana a Hāto Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Kaliyuga Bhavishya Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ସପ୍ତ ବିଂଶ ସହସ୍ର,
କଳିଯୁଗ ଆୟୁଷ ହେବ ବିନାଶ।
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ରଟି ଭୋଗ ହୋଇବ,
ସତ୍ୟଯୁଗ ପୁଣି ଉଦୟା ହେବ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Chāri lakṣa sapta biṃsha sahasra,
Kaḷiyuga āyuṣa heba bināsha.
pāñcha sahasraṭi bhoga hoiba,
Satyayuga puṇi udayā heba.

Tikanga:
Ko te tikanga, ka whakangaromia te 427,000 tau mai i te tau 432,000 tau o Kaliyuga; e 5,000 tau anake ka pā, i muri iho ka whiti te Satya Yuga.

Ehara i a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anake, engari tētahi atu o ngā Panchasakhas, a Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das, i kōrero hoki mō te tau o Kaliyuga i roto i ana tuhinga Malika rerekē. I a ia e whakamārama ana ki tana akonga a Baranga i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Chumbaka Malika’, i tuhi ia:

Odia Sloka:

ଏବେ ପାଞ୍ଚ ଠିକ କହିବା ଶୁଣ,
ବାରଙ୍ଗ ବିଚାର ଚିତ୍ତରେ ଘେନ।
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଯେତେବେଳେ ହେବ,
ସମ୍ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ ଲୀଳା ପ୍ରକାଶ ହୋଇବ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Ebe pāñcha ṭhika kahibā shuṇa,
Bāraṅga bichāra chittare ghena.
pāñcha sahasra jetebeḷe heba,
sampūrṇa līḷā prakāsha hoiba.

Tikanga:
Ko te tikanga, whakarongo pū mai e te akonga a Baranga, puritia te tau 5. Ka pahemo te 5,000 tau, kātahi anake ka puta ngā mahi tapu katoa (Leelas) a Te Ariki.

I tuhi anō a Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Chumbaka Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

କଳିଯୁଗ ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଗଲେ,
ବିଷ୍ଣୁ ଜନମ ଯେ ହୋଇବେ ଭଲେ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Kaḷiyuga pāñcha sahasra gale,
Viṣṇu janama je hoibe bhale.

Tikanga:
Ko te tikanga, i muri i te paunga o te 5,000 tau o Kaliyuga, ka puta te whakahokinga mai o Te Ariki Vishnu.

E whakamārama ana e ka mutu a Kaliyuga i muri i te paunga o te rima mano tau, ā, ka puta ngā mahi a Te Ariki, i tuhi a Mahapurush Balarama Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Bhavishyata Parardha’:

Odia Sloka:

ହସ୍ତିନା କଟକ ଯେବଣ ଭୂଇଁ,
ବର୍ତ୍ତମାନ ଦିଲ୍ଲୀ ସହର ସେହି।
ପୂର୍ବର ଚରିତ କହିବା ଶୁଣ,
ଦେବାପି ଅଛନ୍ତି ତପସ୍ୟାରେଣ।
ଉତ୍ତର ଖଣ୍ଡେ ତପସ୍ୟା କରନ୍ତି,
ତପସ୍ୟା ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ ହେ ହୋଇ ନାହାନ୍ତି।
ପଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ରେ ବରଷ ପୁଣ,
ତପସ୍ୟା ତାଙ୍କର ହୋଇବ ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ।
ବାରୁଣା କୁଳେ ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କୁ ଭେଟିବେ,
ପ୍ରଭୁ ଶ୍ରୀହସ୍ତେ ସିରୀପା ବାନ୍ଧିବେ।
ଶ୍ରୀପଦ୍ମ ହସ୍ତ ତା ମୁଣ୍ଡେ ଲଦିବେ,
ହସ୍ତିନା ରାଜ୍ୟ ନଗ୍ରରେ ସ୍ଥାପିବେ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Hastinā kaṭaka jebaṇa bhūiṃ,
bartamāna Dillī sahara sehi.
Pūrbara charita kahibā shuṇa,
Debāpi achhanti tapasyāreṇa.
Uttara khaṇḍe tapasyā karanti,
tapasyā pūrṇa he hoi nāhānti.
Pañcha sahasre baraṣa puṇa,
tapasyā tāṅkara hoiba pūrṇa.
Bāruṇā kuḷe prabhuṅku bheṭibe,
Prabhu shrīhaste sirīpā bāndhibe.
Shrīpadma hasta tā muṇḍe ladibe,
Hastinā rājya nagrare sthāpibe.

Tikanga:
Ko te tikanga, ko te whenua i Hastinapur Cuttack ko te tāone o Delhi i tēnei wā. Whakarongo ki te kōrero o mua—Kei te mahi a Devaapi i te tūpato. Kei te mahi ia i te tūpato i te kāinga o Kalapa i te rohe o Uttarakhand i Inia, ka oti i muri i te paunga o te 5,000 tau o Kaliyuga. I muri i te otinga o te tūpato, ka tūtaki ia ki Te Ariki. Ka whakatakotoria e Te Ariki he karauna ki runga i a ia mā Ōna ringa rengarenga, ā, ka whakatau i a ia hei Kingi mō Hastinapur, mō Delhi rānei.

Ka tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Kali Kalpa Gita’:

Odia Sloka:

ଦାରୁ ଚଢ଼ି ଦାରୁରୂପ ତେଜିକରି କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପ ହୋଇବ,
ନିଷ୍ଠା ବଚନ ମୋହର ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର କଳିରେ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ଘଟିବ।
ଆହୁରି କଥାଏ ଶୁଣ ରାମଦାସ କଳି ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ରରେ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Dāru chaḍhi dārurūpa tejikari Kalkī rūpa hoiba,
niṣṭhā bachana mohara Rāmachandra Kaḷire nishche ghaṭiba.
āhuri kathāe shuṇa Rāmadāsa Kaḷi pāñcha sahasrare.

Tikanga:
Ko te tikanga, i muri i te paunga o ngā tau 5,000 o Kaliyuga, ka whakarere a Te Ariki Jagannatha i Tōna 'Daaru-rupa' rānei i te āhua o Jagannatha, ā, ka mau i te āhua o Kalki.

Mai i ngā irārangi katoa i runga ake nei, e mārama ana ko te tau i pā ki a Kaliyuga he 5,000 tau noa iho. Ehara i te 432,000 tau, i te 10,000 tau rānei.

Engari he pātai nui ka ara ake i konei— E mōhio ana tātou, kua pahemo kē ngā tau 5,000 o Kaliyuga, ā, kei te haere tonu te tau 5,127. E ai ki tēnei tātaitanga, kua mutu kē a Kaliyuga, ā, ko 'Adya Satya Yuga' te mea e rere ana! Engari kei te kitea tonu te mana o Kaliyuga i ngā wāhi katoa. Kei te horapa te hara me te hē i ngā wāhi katoa. He aha tēnei taupatupatu?

Ko te whakautu e whai ake nei— Tuatahi, ko te 5,000 tau he kōrero whānui, kāore i te tino tika. Ko te 5,000 tau te tikanga he tata ki te 5,000 tau. Koinei te take i tuhia ai te 5,050 tau ki ngā wāhi maha.

Tuarua, e ai ki te Malika, kua mutu kē te roanga o Kaliyuga, ā, kei te haere tonu te 'Sandhikala' (wā whakawhiti) o Kaliyuga me Satya Yuga. E ai ki te Malika, i roto i tēnei Sandhikala ka puta ngā kaupapa o te whānautanga o Te Ariki Kalki me te whakatūnga o te Dharma e Ia—arā, te whakangaro i te hunga kino me te whakaoranga o ngā ākonga tapu. Koinei te take kei te tihi te hara me te hē ahakoa kua mutu te roanga o Kaliyuga. Me mōhio, ka oti te whakatūnga o te Dharma, ka mutu rawa te mana o Kaliyuga, ā, ka whakatūhia anō te Pono, te Dharma, me te Tika.

Kua tuhia hoki e Achyutananda Das i roto i ana tuhinga Malika mō te wā ka puta tēnei whakatūnga o te Dharma i raro i te kaiārahi o Te Ariki Kalki.
