E mōhiotia whānuitia ana nō te Kingi o Puri, a Shri Dibyasingha Deb, tēnei tau 47, e taka ana i waenganui i ngā tau 2005 me 2008.

Ka tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Kalpatika’ –

Odia Sloka:

ନୀଳାଦ୍ରି ଛାଡି ପ୍ରଭୁ ପୀତବାସ,
ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟରେ ଜନ୍ମ ଲଭିବେ ଅବଶ୍ୟ।
ଗୁଣରେ ଭେଦି ଭେଦ ଅଙ୍କେ ଜାଣ,
ଦେଲେ ଯେତେ ସାଲ ହେଲା ପ୍ରମାଣ।
ଏହି ସାଲରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମିବେ,
ଅଙ୍କ ପ୍ରମାଣ ଯେ କହିଲୁ ଏବେ।
ବିଂଶ ରେବ ଅଙ୍କ ଯେବଣ ଦିନ,
ଇନ୍ଦୁ ପରେ ବିନ୍ଦୁ ଉଦେ ଗଗନ।
ଏମନ୍ତ ଲକ୍ଷଣ ଦେଖିବୁ ଯେବେ,
ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମ ହୋଇଥିବେ ତେବେ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Nīḷādri chhāḍi prabhu pītabāsa,
martyare janma labhibe abashya.
guṇare bhedi bheda aṅke jāṇa,
dele jete sāla helā pramāṇa.
ehi sālare prabhu janamibe,
aṅka pramāṇa je kahilu ebe.
biṃsha reba aṅka jebaṇa dina,
indu pare bindu ude gagana.
emanta lakṣaṇa dekhibu jebe,
prabhu janma hoithibe tebe.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka tino whai wairua te Mahaprabhu ki te whenua i muri i tana wehenga atu i tōna kāinga Jagannath. I a ia e kī ana i te tau whānau o te Mahaprabhu, ka kī te Mahapurush ka tāpiri koe i te rua tekau (20) ki te tau o te Revati Nakshatra (27), katahi ko te tau ka puta mai me mōhio ko te tau whānau o te Ariki. Arā, 20+27 = 47.

E mōhiotia whānuitia ana ko tēnei tau te tau rangatira (Anka) o te Kingi o Puri, a Dibyasingha Deb, e tohu ana ki tētahi o ngā tau i waenganui i te 2005 me te 2008.

I tuhi anō te Mahapurush i roto i tētahi o ana tuhinga Malika –

Odia Sloka:

ଏହି କଥାମାନ ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଘଟିବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ପଞ୍ଚଚାଳିଶ,
ଶୂନ୍ୟ ବାନା ଧରି ଆସିବେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି ଧରିଣ କଳକୀ ବେଶ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Ehi kathāmāna nishchaya ghaṭiba Dibyasiṃha pañchachāḷisha,
shūnya bānā dhari āsibe Shrī Hari dhariṇa Kaḷakī besha.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka tae ki te tau 45 o Maharaj Shri Dibyasingha Deb o Puri, katahi ka whai wairua te Ariki Shri Hari hei Kalki ka heke iho ki te whenua.

He mea nui kia mōhio ko te 45 o ngā tau o Maharaj Shri Dibyasingha Deb o Puri e tohu ana ki tētahi tau i waenganui i te 2005 me te 2008.

I tuhi anō a Achyutananda Das Ji i roto i tana tuhinga Malika –

Odia Sloka:

ଅତି ଅନ୍ୟାୟ ହୋଇବ ସଂସାର,
କାହାରି ନ ଥିବ କୁଳ ବିଚାର।
ବହୁତ ଉତ୍ପାତ ସଂସାରେ ହେବ,
ନିର୍ଭୟ ହୋଇ ଜଣେ କେ ନଥିବ।
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ପଞ୍ଚଚାଳିଶେ,
ଆସିବେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି କଳକୀ ବେଶେ।
ନାଶିବେ ଯାବତ୍ ଅବନୀଭାର,
ଏଇଠୁ ହୋଇବ ପୃଥବୀ ସଂହାର।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Ati anyāya hoiba saṃsāra,
kāhāri na thiba kuḷa bichāra.
bahuta utpāta saṃsāre heba,
nirbhaya hoi jaṇe ke nathiba.
Dibyasiṃha deba pañchachāliśe,
āsibe Śrī Hari Kaḷakī beśe.
nāśibe jābat abanībhāra,
eithu hoiba pṛuthabī saṃhāra.

Tikanga:
Arā, i te mutunga o Kaliyuga, ka nui te hē i te ao. Kāore he tangata e whakaaro ki te whānau, e whai rānei i te Dharma. Ka noho raruraru ngā wāhi katoa; kāore he tangata e noho rangimārie, e harikoa ana i ō rātou rā. I taua wā, i te 45 o ngā tau o te kingi ko Dibyasingha Deb te ingoa (i waenganui i te 2005-2008), ka heke iho te Ariki a Shri Hari ki tēnei ao matekore i roto i te āhua o Kalki. Ka tangohia e ia te taumahatanga o te hara i te whenua, ka whakangaro i te hunga kino, ā ka whakakore i ngā 'Mlechhas'.

Kua kīia e ngā Mahapurusha te whānautanga o te Ariki a Kalki i ngā wāhi maha i roto i ngā tuhinga maha.

I roto i tētahi atu o ana tuhinga Malika, ‘kalikalpa Gita’, i tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das –

Odia Sloka:

ବିରଜା ପୀଠ ବୈତରଣୀ ନଦୀ,
ଦଶାଶନୀ ଘାଟ ଉଦୟ ଭେଦି।
ସପତ ମାତୃକା ଟେକିଅଛି ଶିର,
ତାଙ୍କ ଅଧରୁ ବହିବ ରୁଧିର।
ସେହି ସଂକେତକୁ ଜାଣିଥାଅ ରାମ,
କଳଙ୍କି ଉଦୟ ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଜାଣ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Birajā pīṭha Baitaraṇī nadī,
Dashāshanī ghāṭa udaya bhedi.
Sapata mātṛkā ṭekiachhi shira,
tāṅka adharu bahiba rudhira.
Sehi saṅketaku jāṇithāa Rāma,
Kaḷaṅki udaya nishchaya jāṇa.

Tikanga:
Arā, e rere ana te Awa o Baitarani ki te taha o te Temepara o Biraja e tū ana i te rohe o Jajpur i Odisha. Ki te taha o te Dashashwamedha Ghat o taua Awa o Baitarani anō ko te temepara o te Saptamatruka, kei reira ngā Atua Whaea Tokowhitu—arā ko Chamunda, Varahi, Indrani, Vaishnavi, Brahmani, Kaumari, Maheshwari, me Narasimhi—e noho ana.

Ka tuhi te Mahapurush, ka rere te toto i ngā ngutu, arā, i ngā waha, o ngā Whaea Saptamatruka, he tohu tērā e mōhio ai ngā karakia kua whānau te Ariki a Kalki, kua tata rawa rānei tōna whānautanga.

E mōhiotia whānuitia ana kua puta kē tēnei kaupapa i te Temepara o Saptamatruka, e whakamana ana i te poropititanga a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das.

E ai ki ngā tohunga o te Temepara o Biraja, Jajpur, me te Temepara o Saptamatruka, me ngā tāngata whenua o reira, kua puta kē te rere o te toto i ngā waha o ngā Whaea Saptamatruka i te tau 2005.

I tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଯେ ଦିନ ବିରଜା ଆସ୍ଥାନ ଛୋଡ଼ିବ ଭୀମ ସିଂହନାଦ ହେବ,
ଗର୍ଭସ୍ଥିତ ଶିଶୁଭୂମିରେ ପଡ଼ିବେ ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରକୁ ଯିବ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Je dina Birajā āsthāna choḍiba bhīma siṃhanāda heba,
garbhasthita śiśubhūmire paḍibe Bhubaneśwaraku jiba.

Tikanga:
I konei, kei te whakaahua te Mahapurush i te wā o te whānautanga o te Mahaprabhu i roto i te reo tohu. Ka tuhi ia i te rā ka wehe a Maa Biraja i tōna nohoanga, i taua wā ka uru tētahi tamaiti (te Ariki a Kalki) ki te kōpū o tōna whaea, ka haere ki Bhubaneswar ā muri ake.

E mōhiotia whānuitia ana i wehe a Maa Biraja i tōna nohoanga i te tau 2004. I te marama o Hepetema 2004, ka whakatakoto mahere ētahi tāngata tōrangapū, ka tahaetia te whakapakoko o Maa Biraja (e whakanohohia ana ki te Temepara o Biraja, Jajpur). I muri i te 6 marama, i te marama o Maehe 2005, ka hoki te whakapakoko o te Whaea ki te temepara.

Mai i ngā irava e ono i runga ake nei, kua tino whakamatauria kua whānau a Mahaprabhu Kalki, ā ko tōna wā i te takiwa o ngā tau 2005-2008. Nō reira, kia mārama ngā kaipānui katoa kei te noho te avatar a Kalki i te Ao i tēnei wā.

8.1 Kei hea te Mahaprabhu i whānau ai?

E ai ki ngā Puranas, ko te wāhi whānau o te Ariki a Kalki ko te kāinga o Sambhal. E kī ana hoki te Bhavishya Malika ka whānau te Ariki a Kalki ki te kāinga o Sambhal. E whakaae ana te katoa ki tēnei take.

I tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Chakada Madana’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ କଷଣ ଦେଖି ପ୍ରଭୁ ଭଗବାନ,
କଳିରେ ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ରେ ଜନମ ହୋଇଣ।
ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ପିତାଙ୍କର ନାମଟି ଅଟଇ,
ମାତା ନାମ ସୁମତି ଯେ କହିଲୁ ବୁଝାଇ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Bhaktaṅka kaṣaṇa dekhi prabhu bhagabāna,
Kaḷire Sambaḷa nagre janama hoiṇa.
Biṣṇusharmā pitāṅkara nāmaṭi aṭai,
mātā nāma Sumati je kahilu bujhāi.

Tikanga:
Arā, i te kitenga i te mamae o ngā karakia i te tihi o te wā o Kali, ka whānau te Ariki a Kalki ki Sambal Nagar. Ko te ingoa o te pāpā o te Mahaprabhu ko Vishnusharma, ā ko te ingoa o tōna whaea ko Sumati.

I tuhi anō te Mahapurush i roto i te ‘Chakada Madana’ –

Odia Sloka:

ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ସେ ଗୁପ୍ତେ ହେବେ ଜନମ,
ଛତିଆ ବଟରେ ଲାଗିବ ଦିବାରାତ୍ରି ସଂଗ୍ରାମ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Sambaḷa nagare prabhu se gupte hebe janama,
Chhatiā baṭare lāgiba dibārātri saṅgrāma.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka whānau puku a Mahaprabhu Kalki ki Sambal Nagar. Ka pakaru mai he pakanga, he whawhai rānei i te ao, i te pō i Chhatia Bata.

I tuhi anō a Achyutananda Das Ji i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Chakada Madana’ –

Odia Sloka:

ମାରୁ ମହରଗ ରାଜକର ଆସି ଘରେ ଘରେ ପଶିଯିବ,
ବାରଣ ବେଳରେ ସମ୍ବଳନଗ୍ରେ କଳ୍କୀ ଉଦୟ ହୋଇବ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Māru maharaga rājakara āsi ghare ghare paśijiba,
bāraṇa beḷare Sambaḷanagre Kaḷkī udaya hoiba.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka uru te mate, te matekai, me ngā tāke ki ia kāinga, ā ka horapa te raruraru ki ngā wāhi katoa, ka puta mai te Ariki a Kalki ki Sambal Nagar i taua wā mutunga o Kaliyuga.

I tuhi anō te Mahapurush i roto i te ‘Chakada Madana’ –

Odia Sloka:

କହନ୍ତି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଶୁଣ ରାମଦାସ ଭକତ ମୋ ଗଳା ମାଳ,
ଭକତ ନିମନ୍ତେ ସମ୍ବଳନଗ୍ରେ ହେବି କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାର।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Kahanti Achyuta śuṇa Rāmadāsa bhakata mo gaḷā māḷa,
bhakata nimante Sambaḷanagre hebi Kaḷkī abatāra.

Ka tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i te ‘Tera Janma Sharana’ –

Odia Sloka:

କଳକୀର ସୀମା କାଳ ପୂରିଗଲେ କଳ୍କୀ ବିଷ୍ଣୁଯଶା ପୁରେ,
ସମ୍ବଳ ଗ୍ରାମରେ ଜାତ ହୋଇଥିବେ ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ସଂହାର କାଳରେ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Kaḷakīra sīmā kāḷa pūrigale Kaḷkī Biṣṇujashā pure,
Sambaḷa grāmare jāta hoithibe mlechha saṃhāra kāḷare.

Tikanga:
"Ka oti te wā o Kaliyuga, ka whānau a Kalki ki te whare o Vishnuyasha i te kāinga o Sambal mō te whakangaro i ngā Mlechhas."

Heoi anō, ko te wāhi e tū ana a 'Sambal' he take tautohetohe i roto i ngā hinengaro o te tini tāngata tae noa ki tēnei rā. I roto i tēnei tuhinga, ka whakatau mātou i tēnei.

8.2 He aha te kī a te Bhavishya Malika mō tēnei kaupapa?

Ka tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Jaiphula Malika’ –

Odia Sloka:

ସେହି ବେଳ କାଳ ଜାଣି,
ଓଡିଶାରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନ୍ମିବେ ପୁଣି ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
କେହି ତାଙ୍କ ମାୟାକୁ ନ ଚିହ୍ନି।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Sehi beḷa kāḷa jāṇi,
Oḍishāre prabhu janmibe puṇi lo jāiphula,
kehi tāṅka māyāku na chinhi.

Tikanga:
Arā, i taua wā ka eke te hara ki tōna tihi, ka whānau te Ariki a Kalki ki te kāwanatanga o Odisha. Ka tino kaha te pohehe o te Ariki, e kore e taea e tētahi te mōhio ki a ia.

I tuhi anō te Mahapurush i roto i tētahi o ana tuhinga Malika –

Odia Sloka:

ଉତ୍କଳ ଭୂଇଁରେ କଳ୍କୀର ଜନମ,
ଗ୍ରାମ ନାମ ରାଜହଂସ।
କଳ୍କୀ ଜନମି ଲୀଳା ପ୍ରକାଶିବ,
ଏହା କହିଲି ପ୍ରକାଶ୍ୟ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Utkala bhuiñre Kaḷkīra janama,
grāma nāma Rājahansa.
Kaḷkī janami līḷā prakāśiba,
ehā kahili prakāshya.

Tikanga:
"Ka whānau a Kalki ki te whenua o Utkala (Odisha), i tētahi kāinga ko Rajahansa te ingoa. I muri i tōna whānautanga, ka whakaatu a Kalki i āna mahi atua."

Nā, mō te wāhi i Odisha ka whānau te Ariki a Kalki, i tuhi anō a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tētahi atu tuhinga Malika, ‘Bhabishya Jatipataka Gita’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଓଡିଶା ଦେଶରେ ଗୁପ୍ତ ଗଙ୍ଗା ନଦୀ ତୀର,
ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ରେ ବିଷ୍ଣୁବିପ୍ର ଦ୍ୱିଜବର।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Oḍiśā deśare gupta Gaṅgā nadī tīra,
Sambaḷa nagre Biṣṇubipra dwijabara.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka tū te wāhi whānau o te Mahaprabhu ki te taha o ngā tahataha o te Gupta Ganga (Awa o Baitarani) i Sambal Nagar, Odisha. I taua wāhi tonu, i te whare o tētahi Brahmin pai e waiata ana i ngā whakamoemiti ki te Ariki a Vishnu, ka whānau a Mahaprabhu Kalki.

Mai i ngā irava e rua i runga ake nei, kua tino mārama, e ai ki te Bhavishya Malika, ka whānau te Ariki a Kalki ki Sambal, e tū ana i te kāwanatanga o Odisha i Inia.

8.3 Kei hea a Sambal i Odisha?

Ko tētahi o ngā Panchasakhas, a Mahapurush Jagannath Das, i tuhi i roto i te tuhinga Malika ‘Bhabishyat Parardha’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଯାଜପୁର ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ରରେ,
ଦୁଇ ଜଣ ଅଛନ୍ତି ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ଘରେ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Jājapura Sambaḷa nagrare,
dui jaṇa achhanti brāhmaṇa ghare.

Tikanga:
I roto i te irava i runga ake nei, ka tuhi te Mahapurush – “Jajapura Sambala nagrare”. Arā, “I te Sambal Nagar o Jajpur”. Kua mārama kei Jajpur a Sambal Nagar.

I tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Kalikalpa Gita’ –

Odia Sloka:

କ୍ଷୀର ନଦୀ ଦକ୍ଷିଣ ଯେ ବିରଜା ମଣ୍ଡଳ,
ଖ୍ୟାତ ଯେ କମଳପୁର ଗୁପ୍ତ ଯେ ସମ୍ବଳ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Kṣhīra nadī dakṣhiṇa je Birajā maṇḍala,
khyāta je Kamalapura gupta je Sambaḷa.

Tikanga:
Arā, ki te tonga o te awa o Khira me te raki o te Mahanadi ko te rohe temepara o Biraja. Ki te taha o tēnei rohe o Biraja he kāinga e mōhiotia ana ko Kamalapur. Koinei te mea e kīia puku ana ko te kāinga o Sambal.

Nā roto i tēnei irava, kua whakaatu te Mahapurush kei te rohe o Biraja, kei te rohe rānei o Jajpur i Odisha a Sambal.

Kia mārama tātou kei hea a Sambal i Odisha mā ētahi atu irava.

I tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Gurubhakti Gita’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଗୟାରେ ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ର ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ଘର,
ଜନମିଣ ତପସ୍ୟା କରିବେ ଚକ୍ରଧର।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Gayāre Sambaḷa nagra Biṣṇusharmā ghara,
janamiṇa tapasyā karibe Chakradhara.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka whānau a Chakradhara (Te Ariki Vishnu) ki te whare o tētahi tangata karakia e noho ana i Sambal Nagar, Gaya, ā, ka mahi i te tūpato i muri mai.

I tuhi anō a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Kalikalpa Gita’ –

Odia Sloka:

ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ଗୃହେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ହୋଇବେ ଜାତ,
ଗୟାରେ ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ର ହରିହର କ୍ଷେତ୍ର।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Biṣṇusharmā gruhe prabhu hoibe jāta,
Gayāre Sambaḷa nagra Harihara kṣhetra.

Tikanga:
"Ka whānau te Ariki ki te whare o Vishnusharma, i te rohe o Harihara o Sambal Nagar, Gaya."

E ai ki ngā irava e rua i runga ake nei, ka whānau te Ariki ki te Sambal Nagar o Gaya. Inaianei, ko tēhea Gaya tērā, ā, kei hea i Odisha i kōrerotia e Te Ariki Krishna tonu ki ana Gopas tekau mā rua, i whakahuatia e Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Biraja Mahatmya’. I tuhi te Mahapurush –

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ବାର ସୁତ ନିହାର ବଚନ ଏ ଅଟେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଠାର,
ନାଭିଗୟା ତୀର୍ଥେ ହରିହର କ୍ଷେତ୍ର ଗ୍ରାମଟି ସମ୍ବଳ ପୁର।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Śuṇa bāra suta nihāra bachana e aṭe Achyuta ṭhāra,
Nābhigayā tīrthe Harihara kṣhetra grāmaṭi Sambaḷa pura.

Tikanga:
"Whakarongo e ngā tama tekau mā rua, koinei te kupu pono mai i a Achyuta: kei te rohe o Harihara te kāinga o Sambalpur i te wāhi tapu o Nabhigaya."

Arā, kei te wāhi tapu o Nabhigaya te rohe o Harihara, e kīia nei ko Sambal.

I roto i ngā kōrero i runga ake nei, kua kite tātou i ngā wāhi maha i kīia e te Mahapurush te wāhi whānau o te Mahaprabhu ko te rohe o Biraja, ko te wāhi tapu rānei o Gaya (Nabhigaya). Inaianei, ka mōhio tātou kei hea tonu ēnei rohe, he aha ngā whakapono pūrākau e pā ana ki a rātou, ā, he aha tō rātou hiranga.

8.3.1 Te Rohe o Biraja

Ko te 'Temepara o Biraja' he temepara nui kei ngā tahataha o te Awa o Baitarani i Jajpur, Odisha, e karakiatia ana a Adi Shakti i te āhua o 'Biraja'. Inarā, i ngā wā o mua, nā Brahma Ji tonu i mahi he Yajna (whakahere) ki konei i te Brahmakunda, i muri mai ka puta mai a Whaea Parvati i te ahi whakahere, ā, ka tohutohu ki a Brahma Ji kia whakaturia ia ki te ingoa 'Biraja'. Ka kīia hoki te rohe ko te rohe o Biraja i muri i te ingoa o te Atua Wahine a Biraja.

I konei i taka ai te pito (Nabhi) o te Atua Wahine a Sati, ka waiho hei tētahi o ngā Shakti Peethas 51. Koinei anake te temepara e karakiatia ana te Atua Wahine i te āhua o Mahishasura Mardini me ngā ringa e rua.

He rongonui hoki tēnei wāhi ko Nabhigaya mō te Pinda Daan (ngā whakahere ki ngā tūpuna). Inarā, koinei anake te Shakti Peetha e mahia ana te Pinda Daan. E tata ana ki te Brahmakunda, he puna hōhonu e kīia nei ko Nabhikunda, mā tōna wai e mahi ai te tangata i te Pinda Daan mō ō rātou tūpuna. Kua whakahuatia hoki tēnei wāhi i roto i te Skanda Purana.

8.3.2 Nabhigaya

Kua whakahuatia i roto i ngā Puranas i te Satya Yuga, he rewera ko Gayasura tōna ingoa. Ahakoa i whānau ia ki roto i te hapū rewera, i whakaaweawea tōna ngākau e te Dharma me te tūpato. He tino karakia ia, he tino kaha, ā, he tinana nui tōna. I mahi tonu ia mō te oranga o te ao me te painga o ētahi atu. Kua whakahuatia i roto i ngā Puranas i mahi te rewera i te tūpato kaha, i pai ai Te Ariki Vishnu, ā, ka tono te Ariki ki a ia kia tono i tētahi manaakitanga. I tono a Gayasura i tētahi manaakitanga motuhake. I kī ia, “Kia tae ngā mea ora ki te rangi mā tōku pā noa iho.” I hoatu e Te Ariki Vishnu tēnei manaakitanga i tana kitenga i tana tūpato kaha. Engari nā tēnei, ka tīmata te Yamaloka (te ao o te hunga mate) ki te noho kau, nā te mea ko te tangata i pāngia e Gayasura ka haere tika ki te rangi. Ka noho tēnei hei raruraru nui mō ngā atua me Yamraj. I whakatata katoa ngā atua ki te Tokotoru Tapu (Brahma, Vishnu, Mahesh) ā, ka inoi kia kitea he otinga ki tēnei raruraru. I tono ngā atua ki te Tokotoru Tapu kia aukati i a Gayasura. I whakatau te Tokotoru Tapu ki te aukati i a Gayasura, nāna i whakararuraru i ngā ture o te orokohanga ahakoa he tika ia. I mau rātou tokotoru i te āhua o ngā Brahmins tokotoru, ka haere ki a Gayasura, ā, ka kī rātou e hiahia ana rātou ki te mahi i tētahi Yajna tonu mō ngā rā e whitu, ā, mō tēnei, i hiahia rātou ki tētahi wāhi nui, tapu hoki. I tono rātou ki a Gayasura kia hoatu tōna tinana mō te Yajna, engari kaua ia e neke mō ēnei rā e whitu. I whakaae a Gayasura ki tēnei. I horahia e Gayasura tōna upoko ki Gaya (Bihar) i noho ai Te Ariki Vishnu, i whakatakotoria tōna rohe pito ki Nabhigaya (Jajpur, Odisha) i noho ai a Brahma Ji, ā, i whakatakotoria ōna waewae ki Pithapuram (Andhra Pradesh) i noho ai Te Ariki Shiva, ā, nō reira ka tīmata te Yajna. I mahi te Tokotoru Tapu i te Yajna tonu mō ngā rā e whitu.

I pānui tonu a Gayasura i ngā rā mā te rongo i te tangi o te tikaokao i te ata. I te mōhio ki tēnei, i te rā whakamutunga, ka mau a Te Ariki Shiva i te āhua o te tikaokao, ā, ka tangi i mua i te mutunga o te pō. I te rongonga i te tangi o te tikaokao, ka whakaaro a Gayasura kua ata, ā, ka neke i tōna tinana i tana whakaaro kua mutu ngā rā e whitu. Ka puta tonu te Tokotoru Tapu ki mua i a ia, ā, ka kī kua takahia e Gayasura tana oati kia noho puku tōna tinana mō ngā rā e whitu. I kī rātou ko te whiu mō te takahi i te oati ko te mate. I whakaae a Gayasura tika ki tēnei me te kore amuamu. I pai te Tokotoru Tapu ki tana karakia, ā, ka tono ki a ia kia tono i tētahi manaakitanga. Nā reira ka tono a Gayasura ki a rātou kia maharatia tōna ingoa i te taha o te Tokotoru Tapu. I tono ia ki a rātou kia whakapaipai tonu i tōna tinana ki ngā kākahu o ngā Brahmins. I tono hoki ia i te manaakitanga mena ka hoatu he whakahere (Pinda Daan me Tarpan) ki ngā tūpuna o tētahi ki ēnei wāhi e toru, kaua rātou e whānau anō.

I te whakaae ki te hiahia o Gayasura, ka mau a Te Ariki Vishnu i te āhua o Gadadhar i Gaya, ka karakiatia a Brahma Ji i te āhua o Girija Devi i Nabhigaya, ā, ka karakiatia a Te Ariki Shiva i Pithapuram hei Kukkuteshwar (te Ariki nāna i mau te āhua o te tikaokao). Nā tēnei, ka rongonui ēnei wāhi e toru mō te Pinda Daan me Tarpan:

  • Shirogaya (Gaya, Bihar)
  • Nabhigaya (Jajpur, Odisha)
  • Padagaya (Pithapuram, Andhra Pradesh)

Ko te tohu kei Jajpur te wāhi tapu o Nabhigaya e kitea ana hoki i roto i ngā tuhinga Malika o ngā Panchasakha Mahapurushas.

I roto i te tuhinga Malika ‘Chakada Madana’, ka tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das –

Odia Sloka:

ଯାଜପୁରଠାରେ ନାଭିଗୟା ତୀର୍ଥ ଏକଥା ଜାଣ ସତ୍ୱର।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Jājapurathāre Nābhigayā tīrtha ekathā jāṇa satwara.

Tikanga:
"Kia mōhio ki tēnei me te tino māia, kei Jajpur te wāhi tapu o Nabhigaya."

I a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das e matapae ana i tōna whānautanga tekau i te 600 tau ki muri, i tuhi ia i roto i te tuhinga Malika ‘Kalikalpa Gita’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଦଶମ ଜନମ ମୋହର ହୋଇବ ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ଯାଜପୁର ନାଭିଗୟା ହରିହର ଖ୍ୟାତ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Daśama janama mohara hoiba prakāśa,
Jājapura Nābhigayā Harihara khyāta.

Tikanga:
"Ka puta tōku whānautanga tekau i te rohe rongonui o Harihara o Nabhigaya, Jajpur."

Mā tēnei irava hoki, e mārama ana kei Jajpur te wāhi tapu o Nabhigaya.

Ka tuhi te Mahapurush mō tēnei kaupapa anō i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Kalikalpa Gita’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଯାଜପୁର ପଞ୍ଚକୋଶ ବିରଜା ମଣ୍ଡଳ,
ନାଭିଗୟା ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ଘରେ ହେବ ଖେଳ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Jājapura pañchakośa Birajā maṇḍala,
Nābhigayā Biṣṇusharmā ghare heba kheḷa.

Tikanga:
Arā, kei roto i te rima-kosha te whānui o Jajpur te rohe o Biraja. Ka whānau Te Ariki Kalki ki te whare o tētahi tangata karakia o Vishnu e noho ana i taua rohe o Biraja, Nabhigaya.

Nō reira, mai i te kōrero i runga ake nei, he tino mārama kei roto tonu i te rohe o Jajpur o Odisha te Sambal Nagar, te wāhi tapu o Gaya, me te rohe o Biraja i matapae ai ngā Panchasakhas i te whānautanga o Te Ariki Kalki.

Kia mārama ake ai mō te wāhi whānau o Te Ariki Kalki, ka tirohia e tātou ētahi atu irava i whakahuatia i roto i te Malika.

I tuhi a Mahapurush Shishu Ananta Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Chumbaka Malika’ –

Odia Sloka:

କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାରେ ଓଡିଶା ମଣ୍ଡଳେ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ଜନମ ହୋଇବ,
ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ଜନ ନାଶି ଭକତଙ୍କୁ ଉଶ୍ୱାସୀ ଧର୍ମକୁ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ପାଳିବ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Kaḷkī abatāre Oḍiśā maṇḍaḷe tumbhe janama hoiba,
duṣhṭa jana nāśi bhaktaṅku uśwāsī dharmaku tumbhe pāḷiba.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka whānau te Kalki Avatar ki te kāwanatanga o Odisha. Ka whakangaro a Mahaprabhu Kalki i te hunga kino, ka whakaora i ngā tangata karakia, ā, ka whakatū anō i te Dharma ki te Ao.

I a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das e whakahua ana i te Ariki, ka tuhi ia i roto i tana tuhinga Malika –

Odia Sloka:

ଇନ୍ଦୁ ପରେ ବିନ୍ଦୁ ଦେଖିବ ଜାଣ,
ଆମ୍ଭ ଆବିର୍ଭାବ ଯାଜପୁରେଣ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Indu pare bindu dekhiba jāṇa,
āmbha ābirbhāba Jājapureṇa.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka kitea he ira, he whetū rānei i runga ake i te marama i te rangi, kia mōhio ka puta te whakaaturanga o Te Ariki Nui i Jajpur.

I a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Jai Birachita Chautisha’, ka tuhi i a ia e whakahoki anō ana i ngā kupu a te Ariki tonu –

Odia Sloka:

ଯେତେବେଳେ ନୀଳାଚଳ ଛାଡିକରି ଯିବୁ,
ଯାଜପୁର ବିପ୍ର ଘରେ ଯାଇଁ ଜନମିବୁ।
ଜଗିଥିବୁ ହେ। ଯେ ଯେତେ କହିଲେ ନ ଶୁଣିବୁ ହେ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Jetebeḷe Nīḷāchaḷa chhāḍikari jibu,
Jājapura bipra ghare jāiñ janamibu.
Jagithibu he. Je jete kahile na śuṇibu he.

Tikanga:
"Ka wehe koe i Nilachala (Puri), ka haere koe, ā, ka whānau ki te whare o tētahi Brahmin i Jajpur. Kia mataara; kaua e whakarongo ki ngā kōrero a tētahi."

I roto i te tuhinga Malika ‘Tatwabodhini’, ka tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das –

Odia Sloka:

ଯାଜନଗ୍ରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମ ହେବେ,
ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ଭାବେ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Jājanagre prabhu janama hebe,
janama hoibe brāhmaṇa bhābe.

Tikanga:
"Ka whānau te Ariki ki Jajanagar; Ka whānau ia hei Brahmin."

I tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ko ‘Bigyanakalpa’ te ingoa –

Odia Sloka:

ଯାଜନଗ୍ରେ ମହାପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ,
ଗରୁଡକୁ ସଙ୍ଗେ ଘେନି ଲୀଳା ଯେ କରିବେ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Jājanagre Mahāprabhu janama hoibe,
Garuḍaku saṅge gheni līḷā je karibe.

Tikanga:
"Ka whānau a Mahaprabhu ki Jajanagar; ka mau i a Garuda, ka mahi ia i tana takaro atua."

Mai i ngā irava i runga ake nei, kua kore he ruarua ka whānau a Mahaprabhu Kalki ki Sambal Nagar, te wāhi haereere o Nabhigaya, te rohe o Biraja, te takiwā o Jajpur, Odisha.

Ki te tirohia e tatou te Bhagavata Purana me te Mahabharata i tuhia i mua i te Bhavishya Malika, e tohu ana hoki rāua ki Jajpur, Odisha e pā ana ki te wāhi whānau o Lord Kalki.

I ngā wā o mua, i kī a Maharishi Vedavyas ko te wāhi whānau o Lord Kalki ko 'Sambhal village' i roto i te Shrimad Bhagavata Mahapurana. I tuhia e ia –

Sloka Sanskrit:

संभलग्राममुख्यस्य ब्राह्मणस्य महात्मनः ।
भवने विष्णुयशसः कल्किः प्रादुर्भविष्यति ।।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Śambhalagrāmamukhyasya brāhmaṇasya mahātmanaḥ |
bhavane viṣṇuyaśasaḥ kalkiḥ prādurbhaviṣyati ||

Tikanga:
"I roto i te whare o tētahi Brahmin wairua nui, te rangatira o te kāinga o Sambhal, ko Vishnuyasha te ingoa, ka puta a Lord Kalki."

I muri mai, i roto i te 'Vanaparva' o te Mahabharata, i whakatika paku a Maharishi Vyas i tēnei, ā, i tapaina e ia te wāhi whānau o Lord Kalki ko 'Sambhoota Sambhal Grama'. I tuhia e ia –

Sloka Sanskrit:

कल्कि विष्णुयशा नाम द्विजः कालप्रचोदितः ।
उत्पत्स्यते महावीर्यो महाबुद्धिपराक्रमः ।।
सम्भूतः सम्भलग्रामे ब्राह्मणावसथे शुभे ।
मनसा तस्य सर्वाणि वाहनान्यायुधानि च ।।
उपस्थास्यन्ति योधाश्च शस्त्राणि कवचानि च ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Kalki Vishnuyashā nāma dwijaḥ kālaprachoditaḥ |
utpatsyate mahāvīryo mahābuddhiparākramaḥ ||
sambhūtaḥ sambhalagrāme brāhmaṇāvasathe shubhe |
manasā tasya sarvāṇi vāhanānyāyudhāni cha ||
upasthāsyanti yodhāshcha shastrāṇi kavachāni cha |

Tikanga:
Mā ngā irava i runga ake nei, i kī a Maharishi Vyas ka whānau a Lord Kalki ki 'Sambhoota Sambhal Grama', arā, he kāinga Sambhal 'i whakatūria' rānei 'i whakapūmautia', he kāinga Sambhal i whakatūria hei koroni o ngā Brahmin mō te mahi Yajnas me te karakia. I tuhia e ia ka whānau a Lord Kalki ki te whare o tētahi Brahmin pai i noho ki tēnei kāinga Sambhal, ā, i waiata i ngā honore o Lord Vishnu.

Ināianei, ki te tirotirohia e tatou ngā whārangi o te hītori, ka kitea e tatou kei te takiwā o Jajpur, Odisha te nohoanga o ngā Brahmin i whakatūria mō te Yajna me te karakia.

Ko te ingoa o Jajpur i tapaina i te rautau 10 i muri i te taitara o te Kingi Somavamsi a Jajati (Yayati). I whakatūria e Kingi Jajati tēnei tāone, ā, i hanga e ia a Abhinav Jajatinagar hei whakapaipai mōna. I muri mai, i hurihia tōna ingoa, i hanga tuatahi hei Jajanagar, katahi ko Jajpur. I kawea mai e Kingi Jajati Keshari e 10,000 ngā Brahmin mai i Kannauj i Uttar Pradesh, ā, i whakataungia rātou ki Jajpur, Odisha, ki te mahi i ngā Ashwamedha Yajnas tekau (Dashashwamedha). Ahakoa i tēnei rā, ko te Dashashwamedha Ghat i ngā tahataha o te Awa Baitarani e whakamahara ana i a tatou ki tēnei kaupapa. Ahakoa he aha, i tohu a Maharishi Vyas ki tēnei koroni o ngā Brahmin i noho ki te taha rāwhiti o te temepara o Biraja i Jajpur mā te karanga i a ia ko 'Sambhoota Sambhal Grama' i roto i te 'Vanaparva' o te Mahabharata.

9 Khandagiri – Kei Bhubaneswar, kei tētahi atu wāhi rānei?

Kāore he ruarua o tētahi tangata e hiahia ana ki te Bhavishya Malika mō te wāhi ka whānau a Mahaprabhu Kalki. E mōhio pai ana rātou ka whānau te avatar Kalki ki te kāinga o Sambal kei Jajpur, Odisha.

Kāore hoki he ruarua o rātou ka noho te Ariki ki tētahi wāhi ko Khandagiri te ingoa i muri i tōna whānautanga ki Jajpur.

Ko tētahi o ngā Panchasakhas, a Mahapurush Shishu Ananta Das, i tuhi i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ko ‘Chumbaka Malika’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଏହି କଳିଯୁଗେ କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାରେ ହରି,
ଶ୍ରୀ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରିରେ ଥିବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ବିଜେ କରି।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Ehi kaliyuge Kalki abatare Hari,
Shri khaṇḍagirire thibe prabhu bije kari.

Tikanga:
Arā, i Kaliyuga, ka noho a Lord Hari ki Khandagiri i roto i tōna ahua Kalki.

E tuhi ana a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana ‘Agata Bhabishyata Malika’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଗୋପନେ ମୁକୁନ୍ଦ ଯେ ଆସି,
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରିରେ ଥିବେ ବସି।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Gopane Mukunda je asi,
Khandagirire thibe basi.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka noho puku te Ariki ki Khandagiri.

I tuhi anō a Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das i roto i tētahi o ana tuhinga Malika, 'Patta Madana' –

Odia Sloka:

ଭକତ ସୁମେଳ ହୋଇ ଏକ ଠୁଳ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ପାଶେ ଯିବେ,
ଅନନ୍ତ ମୂରତି ଦର୍ଶନ ସାରିଣ ସର୍ବେ ସର୍ବଙ୍କୁ ଚିହ୍ନିବେ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Bhakata sumeḷa hoi eka ṭhuḷa Khaṇḍagiri pāshe jibe,
ananta mūrati darshana sāriṇa sarbe sarbaṅku chinhibe.

Tikanga:
Arā, i tētahi wāhi ko Khandagiri te ingoa, ka huihui ngā karakia. I reira, ka kite ngā karakia i te darshan (tirohanga) o Lord Kalki, ā, ka mōhio tētahi ki tētahi.

Tae noa ki tēnei wā, e whakaae ana te katoa, engari ka puta te ruarua o te tangata mō te wāhi kei hea tēnei Khandagiri. I ēnei rā, mā ētahi ataata me ngā PDF, e kīia ana ngā momo kerēme mō Khandagiri. E ai ki ētahi tangata, kei Jajpur, Odisha tēnei wāhi ko Khandagiri te ingoa, ka noho te Ariki ki reira, engari e kī ana ētahi atu kei te takiwā o Dhenkanal, Odisha a Khandagiri.

9.1 Kei hea a Khandagiri?

Mā tēnei tuhinga, tukua ahau kia kī ko ngā ingoa katoa i whakahuatia i runga ake nei e pā ana ki Khandagiri (Jajpur, Dhenkanal), he hē rawa rāua.

E whakamārama ana i te wāhi kei hea a Khandagiri, ko tētahi o ngā Panchasakhas, a Mahapurush Yasovanta Das, i tuhi i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ko ‘Basanta Manjari’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ମହିମା କହିତ ନୁହଁଇ,
ଲକ୍ଷ ଲକ୍ଷ ଭକ୍ତ ତହିଁ ସେଠାରେ ଅଛଇ।
ଗୁପତ ଲୀଳା ହୋଇବ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଠାରେ,
ତପସ୍ୟାରେ ରହିଥିବେ ଋଷିଙ୍କ ସଙ୍ଗରେ।
ଗୁପ୍ତ ଗଙ୍ଗା ସେକାଳରେ ବାହାର ହୋଇବ,
ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରକୁ ସେହି ଭସାଇଣ ଦେବ।
ତ୍ରିଭୁବନ ସ୍ୱର କଥା କହିତ ନୁହଁଇ,
ବାରଭୁଜା ଦୁର୍ଗାମାତା ଜଗି ରହିଛଇଁ।
ଆହୁରି କଥାଏ ବାବୁ ଶୁଣ ମନଦେଇ,
ଭୂଆସୁଣୀ ଜଗିଅଛି ସେଠାବକୁ ତହିଁ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Khaṇḍagiri mahimā kahita nuhañi,
lakṣha lakṣha bhakta tahin seṭhāre achhai.
Gupata līḷā hoiba Khaṇḍagiri ṭhāre,
tapasyāre rahithibe ruṣhiṅka saṅgare.
Gupta Gaṅgā sekāḷare bāhara hoiba,
Bhubaneśwaraku sehi bhasāiṇa deba.
Tribhubana swara kathā kahita nuhañi,
bārabhuja Durgāmātā jagi rahichhaiṃ.
Āhuri kathāe bābu śuṇa manadei,
Bhūāsuṇī jagiachhi seṭhābaku tahiñ.

Tikanga:
Arā, kāore e taea te kōrero te korōria o Khandagiri. He maha ngā karakia kei reira. Ka tū ngā mahi atua ngaro (Leela) ki Khandagiri. He maha ngā tohunga me ngā mōnehi e mahi ana i te penance ki reira. He awa huna (Gupta Ganga) e rere ana i reira, ka puta he whakangaro ki Bhubaneswar i te wā o te mahi whakangaro a Kalki. He nui hoki te korōria o taua Bhubaneswar. Kei reira a Whaea Barabhuji me Whaea Bhuasuni, mā rāua e mahi te mahi whakangaro i te mutunga o Kali Yuga.

I roto i te shloka i runga ake nei, i whakamārama te Mahapurush kei Bhubaneswar a Khandagiri.

E whakaahua ana i te wāhi o Khandagiri, i tuhi hoki a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ko ‘Chakada Madana’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରରେ ଲାଗିବ ଗୋଳ,
ଏକାମ୍ର ବନ ଯେ ଅଛି ସେଠାର।
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ସିଦ୍ଧ ସାଧୁ ଅଛନ୍ତି,
କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପକୁ ଧ୍ୟାନ କରନ୍ତି।
କ୍ଷୀର ଧାର ବହିଯାଏ ଗୁପ୍ତର,
ଗୁମ୍ଫାରେ ଭଜନ ହୁଏ ସତ୍ୱର।
ଘଟଣା ସ୍ଥାନ ଯେ ଅଟଇ ସେହି,
କଳିର ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ସେଠାହିଁ।
ଉଦୟଗିରି ଯେ ଅପୋଡା ସ୍ଥାନ,
ସେଠାବରେ ବହୁ ସାଧୁ ଆଶ୍ରମ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Bhubaneśwarare lāgiba goḷa, ekāmra bana je achhi seṭhāra.
Khaṇḍagiri siddha sādhu achhanti, Kaḷkī rūpaku dhyāna karanti.
Kṣhīra dhāra bahijāe guptara, gumphāre bhajana hue satwara.
Ghaṭaṇā sthāna je aṭai sehi, Kaḷira bhārata yuddha seṭhāhiñ.
Udayagiri je apoḍā sthāna, seṭhābare bahu sādhu āśrama.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka tū he pakanga ki Bhubaneswar, ā, kei reira te ngahere o Ekamra. Katahi ka kī te Mahapurush kei reira hoki a Khandagiri, ka noho ngā tohunga me ngā tapu maha i reira, ā, ka waiata tonu i te ingoa o Kalki, ka whakaaroaro hoki ki tōna āhua. I roto i aua ana o Khandagiri, ka tū ngā bhajans me ngā kirtans i te ao me te pō. Kei reira hoki tētahi awa huna (Kshira Nadi) e rere ana. Ko taua Khandagiri te "Wāhi o te Takahanga" (Ghatana Sthana), ka tū te pakanga e toe ana o te Mahabharata ki reira. Kei te tata ko Udayagiri, kei reira ngā ashrams o ngā tapu maha i whakatūria.

Mā te shloka i runga ake nei, i kī mārama te Mahapurush kei Bhubaneswar a Khandagiri, te wāhi ka noho ngā tapu maha, ka haere tonu te waiata ngaro o te ingoa o Kalki i roto i ngā ana, ā, ka tū te pakanga e toe ana o Mahabharata, kei reira hoki te ngahere o Ekamra.

I konei e hiahia ana ahau ki te kukume i te aro o ngā kaipānui ki tētahi meka nui. I roto i ngā tuhinga Malika, ka karangatia a Bhubaneswar he Ekamra Kshetra, he Ekamra Van, he Ekamra Kanan rānei. Inaa hoki, e ai ki te Ekamra Purana o te rautau 13, kotahi anake te rākau mango i tata ki te wāhi o te atua rangatira a Lingaraj. Nō reira, ka karangatia hoki te rohe o Lingaraj he Ekamra Kshetra (te rohe me te rākau mango kotahi).

Koinei te take i karanga ai ngā Mahapurushas i a Bhubaneswar he Lingaraj Kshetra (te rohe o te temepara o Lingaraj) he Ekamra Van Kshetra (te rohe o te rākau mango kotahi) i ngā wā maha.

Ehara i te mea anake, nā te wāhi o Bindu Sarovar i Bhubaneswar, kua kōrero hoki ngā Panchasakhas i roto i ā rātou tuhinga Malika ki a Bhubaneswar hei rohe o Bindu Sarovar.

I tuhi te Mahapurush e whakamārama ana i roto i te 'Garuda Gita' –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ଯେ ଏକାମ୍ର କାନନ ଏ ସବୁ ମୋ ଗୁପ୍ତ ସ୍ଥାନ,
ଆଉ ପୃଥ୍ବୀରେ ଯେତେ ସିଦ୍ଧ ସ୍ଥାନ ପୁରାଣେ ଅଛି ବର୍ଣ୍ଣନ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Bhubaneśwara je ekāmra kānana e sabu mo gupta sthāna,
āu pruthwīre jete siddha sthāna purāṇe achhi barṇana.

Tikanga:
E kī ana a Lord Krishna ki tana karakia, a Garuda, ko te Ekamra Kanan kei Bhubaneswar he wāhi ngaro, he wāhi Siddha hoki.

I tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika 'Chhayalisha Patala' –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ଏକାମ୍ରରେ,
ରହିବେ ଭକ୍ତ ତିନିଘରେ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Bhubaneśwara Ekāmrare, rahibe bhakta tini ghare.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka noho ngā karakia o te Ariki ki te rohe o Bhubaneswar Ekamra.

I tuhi a Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika 'Chumbaka Malika' –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ସୀମା ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରିରେ,
ପଞ୍ଚବିଂଶ ଭକ୍ତ ଥିବେ ତଟରେ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Bhubaneśwara sīmā Khaṇḍagirire, pañchabiṃśha bhakta thibe taṭare.

Tikanga:
Arā, kei te taitapa o Bhubaneswar a Khandagiri, ka uru ngā karakia 25 ki te takaro atua.

E tuhi anō te Mahapurush i roto i te 'Chakada Madana' –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରରେ ଗୋଳ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଠାର,
ଭାବି କହଇ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ନିଜ ନାମ ସ୍ମର।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Bhubaneśwarare goḷa Khaṇḍagiri ṭhāra,
bhābi kahai Achyutī nija nāma smara.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka tū he pakanga ki te rohe o Khandagiri i Bhubaneswar.

Tētahi o ngā Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Yasovanta Das, ka tuhi i roto i tana tuhinga Malika –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ଠାରେ ଜାଣ,
ବିନ୍ଦୁ ସେ ସରୋବର ପୁଣ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Bhubaneśwara ṭhāre jāṇa,
Bindu se sarobara puṇa.

Tikanga:
Arā, kia mōhio kei Bhubaneswar te Bindu Sarovar.

Ka tuhi a Mahapurush Yasovanta Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Bhavishyata Parardha’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ଲିଙ୍ଗରାଜ ସ୍ଥଳୀ,
ଦୁଇ ପୁରୁଷ ଏକଜଣ ସ୍ତିରୀ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Bhubaneśwara Liṅgarāja sthaḷī,
dui puruṣha ekajaṇa stirī.

Tikanga:
Arā, ko Bhubaneswar te nohoanga o Lingaraj, arā, kei Bhubaneswar te temepara o Lingaraj.

Mēnā ka whakamahi tātou i a Google, ahakoa rā, ka māmā noa te mōhio ahakoa ko Khandagiri, ko Ekamra Kanan, ko Bindu Sarovar, ko te temepara o Lingaraj rānei, kei Bhubaneswar tonu ēnei katoa.

Ko Khandagiri, te kāinga noho o Te Ariki, kei reira ngā wairua tapu e karakia ana i te ingoa o Kalki—hei whakamārama kei Bhubaneswar (Ekamra Kshetra) tēnei Khandagiri, ka tuhi anō a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i te ‘Chakada Madana’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ପାଦଦେଶେ ଭରତ ପାଦୁକା,
ଛଦ୍ମବେଶୀ ସାଧୁ ପୂଜା କରୁଅଛି ଏକା।
ଗୁପ୍ତଗଙ୍ଗା ବହିଅଛି ଗୁପ୍ତ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି,
ସିଦ୍ଧ ସାଧୁ ଗୁପ୍ତ ବେଶେ ରହିଛନ୍ତି ପୁରି।
ଏକାମ୍ର ବନ ଅଟଇ ଶିବ ଯୋଗ ସ୍ଥାନ,
ଶୁକପକ୍ଷୀ ପାଇଲା ଯେଉଁଠି ବ୍ରହ୍ମଜ୍ଞାନ।
ଭୂଆସୁଣୀ ଦେବୀ ଅଛି ଜାଣିଥା ସତ୍ୱର,
ସେ ଦେବୀ ଘାତରେ ପୃଥବୀ ହେବ କମ୍ପମାନ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Khaṇḍagiri pādadeśe Bharata pādukā, chhadmabeśī sādhu pūjā karuachhi ekā.
Guptagaṅgā bahiachhi gupta Khaṇḍagiri, siddha sādhu gupta beśe rahichhanti puri.
Ekāmra bana aṭai Śiba yoga sthāna, śukapakṣhī pāilā jeuṇṭhi brahmajñāna.
Bhūāsuṇୀ debī achhi jāṇithā satwara, se debī ghātare pruthibī heba kampamāna.

Tikanga:
Arā, kei ngā rekereke o te maunga o Khandagiri te Bharata Paduka, e karakiatia ana e tētahi wairua tapu huna. I taua Khandagiri anō, e rere huna ana te awa o Gupta Ganga. Kei reira (i Khandagiri), he maha ngā wairua tapu tino pai e noho huna ana. Kei reira te ngahere o Ekamra, koia nei te wāhi Yoga o Te Ariki Shiva. Kei reira, i tētahi wā ka whiwhi te manu Shuka i te mātauranga Brahma. E tata ana ko te Atua Wahine Bhuasuni, māna e mahi te mahi whakangaro i te Ao i te mutunga o Kali Yuga.

E hiahia ana ahau ki te kōrero ki ngā kaipānui i konei, i roto i tētahi o ngā irārangi i runga ake nei, i kī te Mahapurush kei te rohe ngahere o Ekamra a Khandagiri, ā, he maha ngā wairua tapu e mahi tūpato ana i Khandagiri. E rere huna ana hoki tētahi awa ki reira. I roto i tēnei irārangi o nāianei, kei te whakahoki anō te Mahapurush i taua kōrero anō.

Ka tuhi anō a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana ‘Tera Janma Sharana’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଲୀଳା ପ୍ରକାଶିବ ଲୀଳାମୟଙ୍କର ସତ୍ୟ ଯେ ଏକାମ୍ର ବନ,
ଲୀଳା କରୁଥିବେ ଅନନ୍ତ ମାଧବ ସର୍ବେ ଆନନ୍ଦ ହୋଇଣ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Līḷā prakāśiba līḷāmayankara satya je Ekāmra bana,
līḷā karuthibe Ananta Mādhaba sarbe ānanda hoiṇa.

Tikanga:
I konei ka kī anō te Mahapurush ko Bhubaneswar te kāinga noho o Te Ariki. Engari kāore ia i whakahua i te ingoa o Bhubaneswar, o Khandagiri rānei. I kī te Mahapurush ki taua wāhi anō ko ‘Ekamra Van’. I tuhi te Mahapurush ka puta ngā mahi atua a Te Ariki (Leelamaya) mai i te rohe o Ekamra Van. I taua rohe o Ekamra Van anō, ka mahi a Te Ariki Ananta Madhava, arā, a Kalki, i ngā mahi atua, mā te kite me te rongo i ēnei, e kore e taea te whakatau i te koa o ngā ākonga tapu.

Ka tuhi anō a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana ‘Aagata Bhavishyata Malika’ –

Odia Sloka:

କଳା ଧଳା ଅଶ୍ୱ ଉପରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ହେବେ ବାହାର,
ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରରେ ରହିବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ରଙ୍ଗା ଅଧର।
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ସିଦ୍ଧ ସ୍ଥାନରେ ଯେତେ ଅଛନ୍ତି ଋଷି,
ସକଳେ ବାହାର ହୋଇବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ନାମକୁ ଘୋଷି।
ସକଳେ ସେବେଳେ ଠୁଳ ଯେ ହେବେ ବିନ୍ଦୁ ସାଗର,
କପିଳେଶ୍ୱରରେ ଭେଟିଣ ହୋଇବେ ସୂପକାର।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Kaḷā dhaḷā ashwa upare prabhu hebe bāhāra,
Bhubaneshwarare rahibe prabhu raṅgā adhara.
Khaṇḍagiri siddha sthānare jete achhanti ruṣi,
sakaḷe bāhāra hoibe prabhu nāmaku ghoṣi.
Sakaḷe sebeḷe ṭhuḷa je hebe Bindu Sāgara,
Kapileshwarare bheṭiṇa hoibe sūpakāra.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka haere Te Ariki ki te whakangaro i runga i tētahi hōiho “Kala-Dhala” rānei, hōiho pango-mā rānei. I mua i tērā, ka noho Te Ariki ki Bhubaneswar. Ka puta mai ngā tohunga me ngā mōnehi katoa e mahi tūpato ana i te wāhi tino pai o Khandagiri i ngā ana i taua wā, e karanga ana i te ingoa o Te Ariki, ā, ka huihui ki Bindu Sarovar kei Bhubaneswar. Ā muri iho, ka tūtaki ngā kaiwhakahaere kai ki a rātou katoa i te temepara o Kapileshwar e tata ana.

I roto i te irārangi i runga ake nei hoki, e kīia ana ko Khandagiri he wāhi tino pai koia nei te kāinga noho o ngā tohunga me ngā mōnehi maha. Ā, e kīia ana hoki ahakoa ko Khandagiri, ko Bindu Sarovar, ko te Temepara o Kapileshwar rānei, kei Bhubaneswar ēnei katoa, ā, e tata ana tētahi ki tētahi.

Tētahi o ngā Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das, i te pātainga a tana ākonga a Baranga Das kei hea a Te Ariki Kalki e whānau ai, ā, kei hea ia e noho ai, ka whakamārama i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Chumbaka Malika’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ହେ ବାରଙ୍ଗ କହିବା ସେ ରଙ୍ଗ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଅବତାର ସ୍ଥାନ,
ଶ୍ରୀ ବିରଜା କ୍ଷେତ୍ରେ ଜନମ ଲଭିବେ ଅନନ୍ତ ମିଶ୍ର ଗୃହେଣ।
ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ ଗୃହକୁ ଛାଡିବେ ତପସ୍ୟା କରିବେ ଯାଇଁ,
ସିଦ୍ଧଗିରି ସ୍ଥାନ ସିଦ୍ଧଙ୍କ ସଦନ ରହିବେ ସେ ଭାବଗ୍ରାହୀ।
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ସ୍ଥାନ ଏକାମ୍ର କାନନ ସୁବର୍ଣ୍ଣଚ୍ୟୁତ ଗିରିରେ,
ସେହି ସ୍ଥାନେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ବିଜେ କରିଥିବେ ଭକ୍ତ ଜନ ସଙ୍ଗତରେ।
ଶ୍ରୀ ଅନନ୍ତ ବାସୁଦେବ ବିପ୍ରଲିଙ୍ଗ ଭେଟ ସେ ଯହିଁ ଆଗରେ,
ବିନ୍ଦୁସାଗରେ ସ୍ନାନ ବିଧି ସାରିଣ ସିଦ୍ଧ କେଦାର ମଧ୍ୟରେ।
ଶ୍ରୀ ବିନ୍ଦୁ ସାଗରେ ସ୍ନାନ ସାରିବେ ସମସ୍ତ ଭକତ ମିଳି,
ନିଜ କଳା ଭକ୍ତି କଳା ପ୍ରକାଶିବେ ଛାଡିବେ କଳିର ମଳି।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Shuṇa he bāraṅga kahibā se raṅga prabhu abatāra sthāna,
Shrī Birajā kṣetre janama labhibe Ananta Mishra gruheṇa.
Janama hoibe gruhaku chhāḍibe tapasyā karibe jāiñ,
Siddhagiri sthāna siddhaṅka sadana rahibe se bhābagrāhī.
Khaṇḍagiri sthāna Ekāmra kānana Subarṇachyuta girire,
sehi sthāne prabhu bije karithibe bhakta jana saṅgatare.
Shrī Ananta Bāsudeba bipraliṅga bheṭa se jahiñ āgare,
Bindusāgare snāna bidhi sāriṇa siddha Kedāra madhyare.
Shrī Bindu Sāgare snāna sāribe samasta bhakata miḷi,
nija kaḷā bhakti kaḷā prakāshibe chhāḍibe kaḷira maḷi.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka whānau Te Ariki ki te whare o tētahi ākonga tapu ko Ananta Mishra te ingoa i te Shri Viraja Kshetra (Jajpur). I muri i te whānautanga, ka wehe Te Ariki i tōna kāinga, ka haere ki tētahi wāhi o ngā wairua tapu tino pai ki te mahi tūpato. Kāore te Mahapurush i mutu noa ki te kī “wāhi tino pai,” i kī anō ia ko te ingoa o taua wāhi ko Khandagiri, ka noho ki Ekamra Kanan. I taua rohe o Khandagiri o Ekamra Kanan anō, ka noho Te Ariki me ōna ākonga tapu. I kī te Mahapurush ka noho te temepara o Shri Ananta Vasudev ki reira, ā, ka noho hoki te Linga, arā, te temepara o Lingaraj ki reira. Ka kaukau Te Ariki i Bindu Sagar, ka toro hoki ki te temepara o Siddha Kedara (Kedareshwar).

I kī anō te Mahapurush ka kaukau ngā ākonga tapu katoa i taua Bindu Sarovar anō, i muri iho ka puta te māramatanga atua ki ngā ākonga tapu, ā, ka whakawāteatia rātou katoa i te paru o Kali Yuga.

Mā te irārangi i runga ake nei, i whakamārama a Sishu Ananta Dasji kei hea te temepara o Shri Ananta Vasudev, kei hea te temepara o Kedareshwar, kei hea a Lingaraj e noho ana, i taua Bhubaneswar anō (rohe o Ekamra Van), kei reira a Khandagiri. Ko tēnei Khandagiri te rohe o ngā wairua tapu tino pai, ā, ka noho Te Ariki ki konei.

He whakaahuatanga o Achyutananda Dasji, Hāto Hadi Das, ka tuhi i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Kali Chautisha’ –

Odia Sloka:

କର୍ଣ୍ଣ ଦେଇ ଶୁଣ ସୁଜନେ କଳିଯୁଗ ବିଚାର,
କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପରେ ଜନମ ହେବେ ଶ୍ରୀ ଚକ୍ରଧର।
କ୍ଷୀର ଧାର ବହି ଯାଉଛି ସ୍ଥାନ ଅମରାବତୀ,
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ସ୍ଥାନ ଗୁପତେ ସିଦ୍ଧ ସାଧୁ ଅଛନ୍ତି।
ଗୁମ୍ଫାରେ ଭଜନ ଲାଗିଛି ରାତ୍ର ଦିନ ମଧ୍ୟରେ,
ଗୁପତେ ଖେଳ ସେ ଖେଳିବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ସେହି ଠାବରେ।
ଝାଡଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଗୁମ୍ଫା ମଧ୍ୟରେ ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କର ସେ ଠାବ,
ଝଲମଲ ଜ୍ୟୋତି ବାହାରେ ଲୀଳା ଚହଟ ହେବ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Karṇa dei shuṇa sujane kaḷiyuga bichāra,
Kaḷkī rūpare janama hebe Shrī Chakradhara.
Kṣīra dhāra bahi jāuchhi sthāna Amarābatī,
Khaṇḍagiri sthāna gupate siddha sādhu achhanti.
Gumphāre bhajana lāgichhi rātra dina madhyare,
gupate kheḷa se kheḷibe prabhu sehi ṭhābare.
Jhāḍakhaṇḍagiri gumphā madhyare prabhuṅkara se ṭhāba,
jhalamala jyoti bāhāre līḷā chahaṭa heba.

Tikanga:
Arā, i te mutunga o Kali Yuga, ka whānau a Te Ariki Maha Vishnu ki te Ao i roto i te whakaahuatanga Kalki. Ka tū ana mahi atua tuatahi ki te rohe o te awa o Kshira (Vaitarani), Amaravati, arā, ki Jajpur. Muri iho ka tū ana mahi atua ki Khandagiri kei reira ngā wairua tapu tino pai e rumaki ana i te bhajan-kirtan i te ao, i te pō i roto i ngā ana huna. I kī te Mahapurush ka noho Te Ariki, ka mahi hoki i ana mahi atua i taua rohe o Khandagiri, o Jhadakhandagiri rānei.

Ka tuhi a Hāto Hadi Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Kali Chautisha’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଘଟଣା ପ୍ରତିମା ପୁରୁଷ ଲିଙ୍ଗରାଜଙ୍କ ପୁର,
ଘୋର କଳି ମହାଭାରତ ହେବ ସେହି ଠାବର।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Ghaṭaṇā pratimā puruṣha Liṅgarājaṅka pura,
ghora kaḷi mahābhārata heba sehi ṭhābara.

Tikanga:
Ka tuhi te Mahapurush ko te “Wāhi o te Takahanga” ko te rohe o Lingaraj, ko Bhubaneswar rānei, ā, ka tū te pakanga toe o Mahabharata ki reira. E mōhiotia ana i kite tātou i tētahi irārangi i mua i tuhi ai a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das – “Ghatana sthana je atai sehi, kalira bharata yuddha sethahin”. I tuhi a Achyutananda Dasji i tēnei irārangi mō Khandagiri i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Chakada Madana’, ā, i kī ko Khandagiri te “Ghatana Sthana,” kei reira ka tū te pakanga toe o Mahabharata.

Muri iho, ka tuhi a Hadi Das – Ko Bhubaneswar, ko te rohe o Lingaraj rānei, te “Ghatana Sthana” ā, ka tū te pakanga o Kali Mahabharata ki reira.

Ko te tikanga o tēnei, ko ngā kōrero a Achyutananda Das mō Khandagiri i roto i te ‘Chakada Madana’ he ōrite tonu ki ngā kōrero a tōna whakaahuatanga, a Hadi Das, mō te rohe o Lingaraj (Bhubaneswar) i roto i te ‘Kali Chautisha’. E whakamana ana tēnei e tohu ana ngā wāhi e rua, arā, a Khandagiri me Bhubaneswar, ki te wāhi kotahi. Hei poto, kei Bhubaneswar a Khandagiri.

Tuhipānui – I roto i ngā irārangi maha i runga ake nei, kua ako tātou ko Khandagiri i Bhubaneswar te rohe o ngā wairua tapu tino pai, tapu hoki. Kei te rumaki aua wairua tapu i te bhajan-kirtan o Te Ariki Kalki i te ao, i te pō i roto i ngā ana o Khandagiri. Ināianei ka ako tātou ko ēhea ngā tohunga matua me ngā mōnehi ka mahi tūpato mō Te Ariki Kalki i taua Khandagiri, ā, e hia katoa ngā tohunga ka mahi bhajan-kirtan i roto i ngā ana.

Ka tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana ‘Aagata Bhavishya Prabandha Malika’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରିକି ଯେ ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର କେତେକ ଦୂର,
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ମଧ୍ୟେ ଅଛନ୍ତି ସାଧୁସନ୍ଥ ନିକର।
ନାରଦ ମାର୍କଣ୍ଡ ଶମ୍ଭୁ ଯେ ଅଗସ୍ତି ଉପଲସ୍ତି,
ଦୁର୍ବାସା କଶ୍ୟପ ଚ୍ୟବନ କୁମ୍ଭଋଷି ସନ୍ତତି।
ପରାଶର ବଶିଷ୍ଠାଦି ହୋ ବିଶ୍ୱାମିତ୍ରଙ୍କୁ ଘେନି,
ଷାଠିଏ ସହସ୍ର ଋଷି ହୋ ଗିରି ମଧ୍ୟରେ ପୁଣି।
ଦେବଋଷି ବ୍ରହ୍ମଋଷି ଯେ ସର୍ବେ ମେଳି ହୋଇଣ,
ଗୁମ୍ଫାରେ ଭଜନ କରନ୍ତି କଳ୍କୀ ନାମ ପୁଣ।
ଦେବତାମାନେ ଯେ ସେଠାରେ ରାତ୍ରେ ଗୋଷ୍ଠୀ କରନ୍ତି,
କଳ୍କୀର ଆଦ୍ୟ ମନ୍ତ୍ରକୁ ନିତ୍ୟେ ଜପୁଛନ୍ତି।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Khaṇḍagiriki je Bhubaneshwara keteka dūra,
Khaṇḍagiri madhye achhanti sādhusantha nikara.
Nārada Mārkaṇḍa Shambhu je Agasti Upalasti,
Durbāsā Kashyapa Chyabana Kumbharuṣi santati.
Parāshara Bashiṣṭhādi ho Bishwāmitraṅku gheni,
ṣāṭhie sahasra ruṣi ho giri madhyare puṇi.
Debaruṣi Brahmaruṣi je sarbe meḷi hoiṇa,
gumphāre bhajana karanti Kaḷkī nāma puṇa.
Debatāmāne je seṭhāre rātre goṣṭhī karanti,
Kaḷkīra ādya mantraku nitye japuchhanti.

Tikanga:
I whakahua te Mahapurush i te kōrero a Krishna-Garuda i roto i tana tuhinga Malika. E kī ana a Garuda kāore te rohe o Khandagiri i tawhiti atu i Bhubaneswar (e tino tata ana). He maha ngā wairua tapu i Khandagiri. Kei reira, ko Narada, Markandeya, Shambhu, Agastya, Upalasti, Durvasa, Kashyapa, Chyavana, “Kumbharushi Santati” arā ko Dridhasyu, Parashara, Va-shish-tha, Vishwamitra, me ērā atu, e tekau mā rua katoa ngā tohunga, ā, tae atu ki a rātou e 60,000 katoa ngā tohunga, ngā Devarishis, me ngā Brahmarishis e rumaki ana i te ingoa-bhajan o Te Ariki Kalki i roto i ngā ana. Ka tū ngā atua i ngā hui i roto i aua ana i te pō, ā, ka karakia i te mantra o Te Ariki Kalki i ia rā.

I roto i tēnei irārangi, kāore a Garudaji i kōrero noa mō ngā tohunga e mahi tūpato ana i roto i ngā ana o Khandagiri, engari i kī anō ia e kore te ana o Khandagiri e tawhiti atu i Bhubaneswar, arā, ka tino tata.

Mai i ngā irārangi 15 i runga ake nei, kua mārama pea ki ngā kaipānui e ai ki a Bhavishya Malika, ko Khandagiri—kei reira a Kalki e noho ai, kei reira ngā wairua tapu tino pai e noho ana, kei reira te bhajan-kirtan o te ingoa o Kalki e haere tonu ana i roto i ngā ana, kei reira tētahi awa e rere huna ana, kei reira ka tū te pakanga toe o Mahabharata—kei Bhubaneswar anake, kei te rohe o Lingaraj rānei, kei te rohe o Ekamra Van rānei, ā, kāore i wāhi kē atu.

10 Te Kaupapa o te Whakaarikitanga o Kalki

E mōhio katoa ana tātou, ka tango Te Ariki i ngā whakaahuatanga tekau ki tēnei Ao hei whakapūmau i te tika (Dharma). Ahakoa ko Satya Yuga, ko Treta Yuga, ko Dvapara Yuga rānei, ka noho tonu te kaupapa o te whakaahuatanga o Te Ariki ki te Ao he ōrite—te whakapūmau i te Dharma.

I roto i te Shrimad Bhagavad Gita, ka kī a Te Ariki Krishna ki a Arjuna –

Sloka Sanskrit:

परित्राणाय साधूनां विनाशाय च दुष्कृताम् ।
धर्मसंस्थापनार्थाय संभवामि युगे युगे ।।

Tikanga:
Hei tiaki i te hunga tika (ngā ākonga tapu), hei whakangaro i te hunga kino, ā, hei whakapūmau i te tika, ka puta ahau i ia tau, i ia tau.

I roto i ngā tuhinga Bhavishya Malika hoki, e kīia ana hei whakangaro i te hunga kino, hei tiaki i ngā ākonga tapu me ngā wairua tapu, ā, hei whakapūmau anō i te Dharma, ka heke a Te Ariki Maha Vishnu ki te Ao i roto i tōna whakaahuatanga tekau, whakamutunga hoki, a Kalki.

Ko te kōrero i waenganui i Te Ariki Krishna me tōna hoa a Arjuna i Dvapara Yuga i whakahua i te 600 tau ki muri i Kali Yuga e Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika 'Chausathi Patala'. Ka tuhi te Mahapurush –

Odia Sloka:

ପାପ ଭାରା ପୃଥ୍ବୀ ସହି ନପାରି,
ମୋ ଆଗରେ କଲା ବେଗେ ଗୁହାରି।
କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାର ମୁହିଁ ହୋଇବି,
ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ପ୍ରାଣୀମାନଙ୍କୁ ବିନାଶିବି।
ବସି ନରପତି ହୃଦ ଦେଶରେ
ପ୍ରଜା ପାଳିବି ଉତ୍ତମ ଭାବରେ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Pāpa bhārā pṛthwī sahi napāri,
mo āgare kalā bege guhāri.
Kaḷkī abatāra muhiñ hoibi,
duṣṭa prāṇīmānaṅku bināshibi.
Basi narapati hṛda deshare,
prajā pāḷibi uttama bhābare.

Tikanga:
Arā, ko te Ao, i te kore e kaha ki te kawe i te taumaha o ngā hara i te mutunga o Kali Yuga, ka inoi mai ki ahau. Ā muri iho ka tango ahau i te whakaahuatanga Kalki, ka whakangaro i te hunga kino, ka whakaora hoki i ngā ākonga tapu. Ka noho ahau ki Inia, ka whakahaere i te kāwanatanga o te ao katoa, ā, ka rangatira i ngā tāngata i runga i te tika me te pai.

Ka tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tētahi atu tuhinga Malika –

Odia Sloka:

ମହାଭୟଙ୍କର ଉତ୍ପାତ ହେବ ଧରିତ୍ରୀ ବକ୍ଷରେ ଜାଣ,
କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପରେ ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ସଂହାରି ଭାରା ଉଶ୍ୱାସିବେ ପୁଣ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Mahābhayaṅkara utpāta heba dharitrī bakṣhare jāṇa,
Kaḷkī rūpare mlechha saṃhāri bhārā uśwāsibe puṇa.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka puta he hurihanga whakamataku ki te Ao. Mā Kalki tonu e whakangaro te hunga kino, e whakaora hoki i te taumaha hara o te Ao.

Mā te whakahua i te kōrero a Krishna-Arjuna, ka tuhi anō te Mahapurush i roto i tana tuhinga Malika –

Odia Sloka:

କଳି ଶେଷ ବେଳେ ମୁହିଁ କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପ ହେବି,
ସନ୍ଥଙ୍କୁ ରଖିଣ ଦୁଷ୍ଟଜନମାନଙ୍କୁ ନାଶିବି।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Kaḷi śeṣha beḷe muñhi Kaḷkī rūpa hebi,
santhaṅku rakhiṇa duṣhṭajanamānanku nāśhibi.

Tikanga:
E kī ana a Shri Krishna, i te mutunga o Kali Yuga, ka tango ahau i te whakaahuatanga Kalki. Mā te tiaki i ngā wairua tapu, i ngā tāngata tapu, me ngā tāngata ngākau mā, ka whakangaro ahau i te hunga kino me ngā kairara katoa.

Mā te whakahua anō i ngā kupu a Te Ariki, ka tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika 'Chausathi Patala' –

Odia Sloka:

ବେଶୀ ପାପ ଯେତେବେଳେ ହେବ କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପେ ମୁଁ ଉଦୟ ହେବି,
ଓଡିଶା ମଧ୍ୟରେ ସ୍ଥାନେ ସ୍ଥାନେ ମନ ପରୀକ୍ଷା ଯାଇ କରିବି।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Beśī pāpa jetebeḷe heba Kaḷkī rūpe mu udaya hebi,
Oḍiśā madhyare sthāne sthāne mana parīkṣhā jāi karibi.

Tikanga:
E kī ana te Ariki, i te Kali Yuga, ka eke te hara ki tōna tihi, ka ara ake ahau i tōku āhua Kalki. Ka whakamātau ahau i ngā tāngata karakia i ngā wāhi rerekē o Odisha, ka whakatau i waenga i te tika me te hē, ā, ka mahi i runga i tērā.

E kōrero ana i ngā kupu a te Ariki, tētahi o ngā Panchasakhas, a Mahapurush Balaram Das, i tuhi i roto i tana tuhinga Malika 'Kali Aagata Bhavishyanta' –

Odia Sloka:

ଚଣ୍ଡୀ ଯେ ଚାମୁଣ୍ଡା ମାତିବେ,
ଖପର କରେ ଘେନିଥିବେ।
ରୋଧିର ବୃଷ୍ଟି ଯେ ହୋଇବ,
କେ ଅବା କାହିଁ ନାଶ ଯିବ।
ଯାହାକୁ ରଖିବିଟି ଟିକିଏ ମୁହିଁ,
ସେ ପ୍ରାଣୀମାନେ ଥିବେ ରହି।
ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ଲୋକ ଯେ ନାଶ ହେବେ,
ସନ୍ଥ ଯେ ଅବଶ୍ୟ ରହିବେ।
ଅନେକ ଉତ୍ପାତ ଲାଗିବ,
ଶୂନ୍ୟରେ ଚହଳ ପଡିବ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Chaṇḍī je Chāmuṇḍā mātibe,
khapara kare ghenithibe.
Rodhira bṛṣṭi je hoiba,
ke abā kāhiñ nāsha jiba.
Jāhāku rakhibiṭi ṭikie muhiñ,
se prāṇīmāne thibe rahi.
Duṣṭa loka je nāsha hebe,
santha je abashya rahibe.
Aneka utpāta lāgiba,
shūnyare chahaḷa paḍiba.

Tikanga:
E kī ana te Ariki, i te wā o te whakatūnga o te Dharma i te mutunga o te Kali Yuga, ka haere a Whaea Ashta Chandis me ētahi atu Yoginis ki te whakangaro. Ka heke te ua toto i te rangi. Ka maha ngā raruraru, ā, ka puta mai ngā aituā i te kore, arā, i te wāhiatea. Ko te hunga anake e tiakina ana e ahau ka noho haumaru. Ka whakangaroa te hunga kino, ā, ka whakaorangia ngā tāngata tapu me ngā tāngata karakia.

E tuhi ana a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika 'Kali Kalpa Gita' –

Odia Sloka:

କଳ୍କୀ ରାମ ରୂପ ଧରି ହରି ଦେଶ ଭ୍ରମଣ କରିବେ,
କ୍ଷତ୍ରୀମାନଙ୍କୁ ନିକ୍ଷତ୍ରୀ କରିଣ ବସୁଧାକୁ ଉଦ୍ଧାରିବେ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Kaḷkī Rāma rūpa dhari Hari desha bhramaṇa karibe,
kṣhatrīmānanku nikṣhatrī kariṇa basudhāku uddhāribe.

Tikanga:
Arā, i te mutunga o te Kali Yuga, ka mau te Ariki i tōna āhua Kalki, ā, ka haereere i te ao katoa, ā, ka whakaora i a Whaea Papa mā te whakangaro i te hunga kino.

I roto i te 'Kali Kalpa Gita' anō, e tuhi anō ana a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das –

Odia Sloka:

ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ନିଧନ ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ସଂହାରଣ,
କରିବେ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ଦେବ କଳ୍କୀ ରାମ।
ଏମନ୍ତ ମହୀ ଯେ ହେବ ଉଶ୍ୱାସ,
କଳିକଳ୍ପ କହେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦାସ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Mlechha nidhana duṣhṭa saṃharaṇa,
karibe niśhche deba Kaḷkī Rāma.
Emanta mahī je heba uśwāsa,
Kaḷikalpa kahe Achyuta dāsa.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka tino whakaora a Ariki Kalki i te Papa mā te whakangaro i ngā Mlechhas (tāngata nanakia/kino) me te whakakore i te hunga kino.

10.1 Te Taenga Mai o Ariki Kalki

Tētahi o ngā Panchasakhas, te Wairua Nui a Sishu Ananta Das, i tuhi mō tēnei kaupapa i roto i tana tuhinga Malika 'Chumbaka Malika':

Odia Sloka:

କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାର ଓଡ଼ିଶା ମଣ୍ଡଳେ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ଜନମ ହୋଇବ,
ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ଜନ ନାଶି ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କୁ ଆଶ୍ୱାସି ଧର୍ମକୁ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ପାଳିବ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Kaḷkī abatāra Oḍiśā maṇḍaḷe tumbhe janama hoiba,
duṣhṭa jana nāśhi bhaktaṅku āśwāsi dharmaku tumbhe pāliba.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka whānau te Ariki Nui a Kalki ki Odisha. I muri i tērā, ka whakangaro te Ariki i te hunga kino, ā, ka tiaki i ngā tāngata karakia kia whakatū anō i te Dharma.

11 Ngā Mahi Whakangaro: He Timatanga me te Whakamutunga Tino?

E mōhio ana tātou i ngā tohutoro o Bhabishya Malika, i roto i tēnei wā whakawhiti i waenga i te Kali Yuga me te Satya Yuga, ka puta ngā mahi whakangaro kāore anō kia kitea i te ao katoa i raro i te kaiārahi o Ariki Kalki, mā reira ka whakangaroa ngā Mlecchas katoa (tāngata nanakia), ngā kaikino, te hunga kino, me te hunga hē. E homai ana hoki a Bhabishya Malika i te whakapūmautanga ka noho haumaru ngā tāngata karakia katoa i roto i ēnei mahi, ā, kāore rātou e hiahia ki te mataku.

E kōrero ana a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Chhayalisha Patala’, i ngā kupu a te Ariki Nui e homai ana te Ariki i ngā kōrero mō te whakakorenga wāhanga e haere mai ana (Khanda Pralaya).

E tuhi ana te Mahapurush:

Odia Sloka:

କାଳଭାବେ ମହାଶକ୍ତି ଖଣ୍ଡ ପ୍ରଳୟରେ,
ସୃଷ୍ଟି ନାଶ କରି ଲୀଳା କରେ ପାପ ଭାରେ।
ମାତ୍ର ମହାପ୍ରଳୟକୁ ରହିଥିବୁ ଟାକି,
ସେ କାଳରେ ଚରାଚର ନ ରହିବେ ବାକି।
ଚଣ୍ଡୀ ଚାମୁଣ୍ଡା ସହିତ ସଂହାରିଣୀ ଶକ୍ତି,
ଖଣ୍ଡା ଖପର ହାତରେ ବୁଲି ଦିବାରାତ୍ରି।
ରୋଗ ଶୋକ ଦୁଃଖ ଜାଲ ସଂସାରେ ପାତିବେ,
ପର ଆପଣାର ଭେଦ କିଛି ନ ରଖିବେ।
ଅଣଚାଶ ପବନ ଯେ ରୁଦ୍ର ମୂର୍ତ୍ତି ଧରି,
ଉପାଡ଼ିବ ସୁମେରୁ ସହିତ ଯେତେ ଗିରି।
ଚାରିମେଘ ଚାରିଦିଗେ ଆଚ୍ଛନ୍ନ କରିବେ,
ମହାଘୋର ବିପତ୍ତିକୁ ଆଣି ଘଟାଇବେ।
ଅନ୍ଧାର ହୋଇବ ପୃଥ୍ବୀ ବଢ଼ିବ ଉତ୍ପାତ,
ନକ୍ଷତ୍ର ଉଲକା ଛିଣ୍ଡିବ ନାହିଁ ଆଦି ଅନ୍ତ।
ଭୈରବୀ ରଡ଼ିବେ ପୃଥ୍ବୀ କାନ୍ଦିବ ସକଳ,
ପାପମାତ୍ରା ପ୍ରବଳରେ ହେବ ଏହି ଫଳ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Kālabhābe mahāshakti khaṇḍa praḷayare,
Sṛṣṭi nāsha kari līḷā kare pāpa bhāre.
Mātra mahāpraḷayaku rahithibu ṭāki,
Se kāḷare charāchara na rahibe bāki.
Chaṇḍī Chāmuṇḍā sahita saṃhāriṇī shakti,
Khaṇḍā khapara hātare buli dibārātri.
Roga shoka duḥkha jāla saṃsāre pātibe,
Para āpaṇāra bheda kichhi na rakhibe.
Aṇachāsha pabana je rudra mūrtti dhari,
Upāḍiba Sumeru sahita jete giri.
Chārimegha chāridige āchhanna karibe,
Mahāghora bipattiku āṇi ghaṭāibe.
Andhāra hoiba pṛthwī baḍhiba utpāta,
Nakṣatra ulakā chhiṇḍiba nāhiñ ādi anta.
Bhairabī raḍibe pṛthwī kāndiba sakaḷa,
Pāpamātrā prabaḷare heba ehi phaḷa.

Tikanga:
Arā, ko te Mana Nui i te āhua o te Wā (Kala) ka kawe mai i tētahi whakakorenga wāhanga, ā, ka whakangaro i te ao. I te wā o te whakakorenga, ka pāngia ngā mea ora katoa. Ka haereere a Chandi-Chamunda me ngā ope whakangaro i te ao me te pō e mau ana i ngā hoari (Khanda) me ngā angaanga (Khapar). Ka horapa te mamae, te pōuri, te mate, me te aue ki te ao katoa. Ka ngaro ngā rerekētanga katoa i waenga i 'ōna ake' me 'ngā tauhou'. Ko ngā hau e whā tekau mā iwa, e mau ana i te āhua nanakia o Rudra, ka hutia ngā pae maunga katoa tae atu ki te Sumeru nui. Ko ngā kapua e whā, arā ko Avarta, Samvarta, Pushkara, me Drona, ka karapoti i ngā taha e whā, ā, ka hanga i ngā aituā whakamataku. Ka taupokina te Papa e te pōuri hōhonu; e kore te rā me te marama e whiti. I taua wā, ka heke mai ngā whetū-hāuata i te rangi, ā, ka waiho hei take whakangaro. I roto i taua pōuri, ka ngunguru a Whaea Bhairavi me te whakangaro i ngā kaikino maha.

E tuhi ana a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, e kōrero ana i ngā kupu a te Ariki, i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Agata Bhabishyata Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

କାହିଁ ଜଳ ରୂପେ ମୁହିଁ ଜଗତ ନାଶିବି,
କାହିଁ ପବନ ରୂପରେ ସକଳ ଗ୍ରାସିବି।
କାହିଁ ଯୋଗମାୟା ନେଇ ସଂହାର କରିବି,
କାହିଁ ନିଜେ ବିଶ୍ଵରୂପ ଧାରଣ କରିବି।
କାହିଁ ମଉନ ବ୍ରତ ମୁଁ ଧାରଣ କରିବି,
କାହିଁ କାଳରୂପେ ମୁହିଁ ପାପୀଙ୍କୁ ନାଶିବି।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Kāhiñ jala rūpe muhiñ jagata nāshibi,
kāhiñ pabana rūpare sakaḷa grāsibi.
kāhiñ yogamāyā nei saṃhāra karibi,
kāhiñ nije bishwarūpa dhāraṇa karibi.
kāhiñ mauna brata muñ dhāraṇa karibi,
kāhiñ kāḷarūpe muhiñ pāpīṅku nāshibi.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka kawe mai a Ariki Kalki i te whakangaro nui ki tēnei ao i roto i ōna āhua rerekē. I ētahi wāhi, ka mau ia i te āhua wai, ā, ka ruku i ngā mea katoa; i ētahi atu, ka pūhia e ia ngā mea katoa i roto i te whakakorenga hau. I ētahi wā, ka kawe mai ia i te whakangaro i a ia e pupuri ana i te oati wahangū, ā, i ētahi atu, ka patu ia i te hunga kino i te āhua o te Wā. I ētahi wāhi, ka mau ia i tōna Āhua Ao Whānui, ā, i ētahi atu, ka kawe mai ia i te whakangaro mā tōna mana Yogamaya.

E ai ki a Bhabishya Malika, ka kawe mai a Ariki Kalki i tēnei whakangaro mā ngā waipuke wai nanakia, ngā rū whenua nunui, ngā āwhā hau whakangaro, ngā waipuke ahi, ngā aituā wāhiatea, ngā mate urutā mōrearea, me tētahi pakanga ao karihi.

11.1 Ngā Rū Whenua

I tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ହିମାଳୟ ଏଭରେଷ୍ଟ ଟଳି ଯେ ପଡିବ,
ଯେବେ ଏହି ଧରା ପରେ ଭୂମିକମ୍ପ ହେବ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Himālaya Everest ṭaḷi je paḍiba,
Jebe ehi dharā pare bhūmikampa heba.

Tikanga:
Arā, i ngā mahi whakangaro a Kalki i te mutunga o te Kali Yuga, ka pērā te kaha o ngā rū whenua ka pā ki te Papa ka pakaru noa atu ngā 'Himalayas' me ōna tihi pērā i a 'Everest'. Ka noho haumaru ngā tāngata karakia, engari ka whakangaroa ngā kaikino.

E tuhi anō ana a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଭୂମିକମ୍ପେ ପ୍ରକମ୍ପିତ ହୋଇବ ଧରଣୀ,
ମାଟି ତଳେ ଲୁଚିଯିବ କୋଠାବାଡି ପୁଣି।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Bhūmikampe prakampita hoiba dharaṇī,
māṭi taḷe luchijiba koṭhābāḍi puṇi.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka wiri te Papa katoa i ngā rū whenua, ā, nā aua rū whenua, ka tanumia ngā whare nunui ki raro i te oneone.

I tuhi te Mahapurush i roto i tētahi atu o ana tuhinga Malika:

Odia Sloka:

ପାତାଳେ ବାସୁକୀ ଟେକିବ ମୁଣ୍ଡ,
ତିନିଥର ଯେ କମ୍ପିବ ବ୍ରହ୍ମାଣ୍ଡ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Pātāḷe Bāsukī ṭekiba muṇḍa,
tini thara je kampiba brahmāṇḍa.

Tikanga:
Arā, nā te taumaha rawa o ngā mahi hara i te mutunga o te Kali Yuga, ka rūrū te nakahi a Vasuki, e mau ana i te Papa i runga i tōna māhunga, i tōna māhunga i ngā wā katoa. Nā tēnei, ka puta ngā rū whenua nunui e toru ki tēnei Papa.

Tētahi tāngata tapu nui i whānau ki Odisha i te rautau 20, a Thakur Shri Abhiram Paramahansa, i tuhi i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Baishnaba Gita’:

Odia Sloka:

ଭୂମିକମ୍ପ ହେବ ଅପାର,
ନାଶ ହୋଇବେ ନାରୀ ନର।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Bhūmikampa heba apāra,
nāśha hoibe nārī nara.

Tikanga:
Arā, i te wāhanga whakamutunga o te Kali Yuga, ka puta ngā rū whenua kāore e taea te tatau, ka mate ngā tāne me ngā wāhine maha.

11.2 Waipuke Wai (Jala Pralaya)

I a ia e kōrero ana i ngā whakamataku o te waipuke wai, e tuhi ana a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଭାରତକୁ ଛାଡି ଦେଲେ ସବୁ ରାଷ୍ଟ୍ର ଯିବ, ମହାସାଗର ଜଳରେ ଡୁବି ମରୁଥିବ।
ଧରଣୀ ଦେହରେ ଜଳ ମାରିବ ଲହରୀ, ଜଳାର୍ଣ୍ଣବ ହୋଇଯିବ ଚଉଦିଗ ଭାରି।
ଅଛଇ ବରଫଗଡ଼ ଉତ୍ତର ମେରୁରେ, ତରଳିବ ଧୀରେ ଧୀରେ ସୂର୍ଯ୍ୟଙ୍କ ରଶ୍ମିରେ।
ରୌଦ୍ରତାପେ ଯେତେବେଳେ ମେଦିନୀ କମ୍ପିବ, ସେତିକିବେଳେ ବରଫଗଡ଼ ତରଳିବ।
ବହିବ ପାଣିର ସୁଅ ସମୁଦ୍ରକୁ ପୁଣ, ଆଟଲାଣ୍ଟିକ ବଢ଼ିବ କାହିଁ କେତେ ଜାଣ।
ସମୁଦ୍ରର ପାଣି ଧୀରେ ବଢ଼ି ବଢ଼ି ଯିବ, ସାଗର ମାତିବ ଶେଷେ ଆଉ ଯାହା ଥିବ।
ଅନାଉ ଅନାଉ କାହିଁ ଜଳ ମାଡିଯିବ, ମାଟିର ଲକ୍ଷେ ଘନ ଫୁଟ ଉପରେ ରହିବ।
ଭୂଗୋଳର ସ୍ଥିତି ବଡ଼ ବିଚିତ୍ର ତୁ ଜାଣ, ଭାରତ ରହିଛି ସର୍ବ ଉର୍ଦ୍ଧ୍ଵରେ ତ ପୁଣ।
ସୃଷ୍ଟିର କର୍ତ୍ତା ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜଗତପତି ଜାଣ, ଭାରତ ଭୂମିରେ କରିବେ ବୈକୁଣ୍ଠ ସ୍ଥାପନ।
ପାଣିର ଘେରରେ ଆଉ ସବୁ ମହାଦେଶ, ଧୀରେ ଜାଣ ବୁଡିଯିବ ସେ ମହାବିନାଶ।
ଦେଖିବ ଏ ଦୁଇ ଆଖି ସମୟ ଆସିଛି, ସାମନା କରି ପାରିଲେ ସମୟକୁ ଇଛି।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Bhāratku chāḍidele sabu rāṣṭra jiba, Mahāsāgara jalare ḍubi maruthiba.
Dharaṇī dehare jala māriba laharī, Jalārṇaba hoijiba chaudiga bhāri.
Achai baraphagaḍa uttara merure, taraḷiba dhīre dhīre sūryāṅka raśmire.
Raudratāpe jetebeḷe medinī kampiba, setikibeḷe baraphagaḍa taraḷiba.
Bahiba pāṇira sua samudruku puṇa, Āṭlāṇṭika baḍhiba kāhiñ kete jāṇa.
Samudrara pāṇi dhīre baḍhi baḍhi jiba, sāgara mātiba śeṣe āu jāhā thiba.
Anāu anāu kāhiñ jala māḍijiba, māṭira lakṣhe ghana phuṭa upare rahiba.
Bhūgoḷara sthiti baḍa bichitra tu jāṇa, Bhārata rahichi sarba urdhware ta puṇa.
Sruṣṭira kartā prabhu jagatpati jāṇa, Bhārata bhūmire karibe Baikuṇṭha sthāpana.
Pāṇira gherare āu sabu mahādeśa, dhīre jāṇa buḍijiba se mahābināśa.
Dekhiba e dui ākhi samaya āsichi, sāmnā karipārile samayaku ichhi.

Tikanga:
Arā, haunga a Inia, ka ruku te nuinga o ngā iwi ki ngā wai o te moana. Ka puta he waipuke wai ki te Papa, ā, ka ruku ngā taha e whā katoa. Nā te pikinga o te wera o te rā, ka tīmata te rewa o ngā papa hukapapa i te Pou Raki, ka tino pāngia te Moana Atarangi. Ka piki tonu te taumata wai i ngā moana, ka ruku ngā tāone takutai. I ētahi wāhi, ka ara ake ngā ngaru tsunami teitei pērā i te maunga. Ka rerekē te matawhenua katoa. Engari ka tiaki te Ariki Nui i a Inia, ā, ka iti ake te pānga o tēnei waipuke wai ki Inia. Haunga a Inia, ka hinga ngā whenua nui katoa ki te waipuke wai.

11.3 Waipuke Hau (Pavan Pralaya)

Tētahi o ngā Panchasakhas, a Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das, e tuhi ana i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Patta Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଧର୍ମହୀନ ହେବେ ସକଳ ଏ ଜନ ଧର୍ମାଧର୍ମ ନ ରହିବ,
ଅଣଚାଶ ବାୟୁ ବହିବ ଏମନ୍ତ ପୃଥିବୀ ଟଳଟଳ ଧ୍ରୁବ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Dharmahīna hebe sakaḷa e jana dharmādharma na rahiba,
aṇachāśha bāyu bahiba emanta pruthibī ṭaḷaṭaḷa dhruba.

Tikanga:
Arā, i te mutunga o te Kali Yuga, ka hē katoa ngā tāngata; kāore he karakia/mahi e toe ana ki tētahi. Koinei te take ka whakatū a Ariki Nui Kalki i te Dharma. Ka pūhia ngā hau e whā tekau mā iwa i tētahi āhua ka wiri te Papa.

E tuhi ana te Mahapurush i roto i tētahi atu o ana tuhinga Malika:

Odia Sloka:

ପବନ ବହି ଅଣଚାଶ,
କ୍ଷିତିକି କରିଦେବ ଧ୍ଵଂସ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Pabana bahi aṇachāśha,
kṣhitiki karideba dhwaṃsa.

Tikanga:
Arā, i ngā mahi whakangaro a Ariki Kalki, ka pūhia ngā hau e whā tekau mā iwa, ā, ka kawe mai i te whakangaro ki te Papa.

Te tāngata tapu matapō i whānau ki Odisha i te rautau 19, a Bhima Bhoi, i tuhi hoki mō tēnei kaupapa i roto i tana tuhinga Malika. E tuhi ana te Mahapurush:

Odia Sloka:

ଅଣଚାଶ ଖଣ୍ଡ ପବନ ବହିବ ପର୍ବତ ଦୋହଲିଯିବ,
ଚାରିମେଘ ମିଳି ବର୍ଷିବେ ଚାରିଦିଗ ଅନ୍ଧାର ଘୋଟିବ।
ମୋ ଭାଇ, ଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ସୂର୍ଯ୍ୟ ଦେଖାଯିବ ନାହିଁ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Aṇachāśha khaṇḍa pabana bahiba parbata dohalijiba,
chārimegha miḷi barṣhibe chāridiga andhāra ghoṭiba.
Mo bhāi, chandra sūrya dekhājiba nāhiñ.

Tikanga:
Arā, i te mutunga o te Kali Yuga, ka tae mai tētahi wā ka pūhia ngā hau e whā tekau mā iwa, ā, ka wiri noa atu ngā maunga me ngā puke. Ka ua ngātahi ngā kapua e whā ko Avarta, Samvarta, Pushkara, me Drona. E kore te rā me te marama e kitea i te rangi, ā, ka taupokina ngā taha e whā e te pōuri hōhonu.

I tuhi anō a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das:

Odia Sloka:

ଟଳଟଳ ହେଉଥିବ ସପ୍ତଦ୍ଵୀପ ମହୀ,
ସେ ବେଳେ ଥଳକୂଳ ରହିବଟି ନାହିଁ।
ଅଣଚାଶ ଖଣ୍ଡ ହୋଇ ପବନ ବହିବ,
ସରକାର ବୁଦ୍ଧିବିଦ୍ୟା ଉଭେଇ ଯିବ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Ṭaḷaṭaḷa heuthibā saptadwīpa mahī,
se beḷe thaḷakūḷa rahibāṭi nāhiñ.
aṇachāśha khaṇଡa hoi pabana bahiba,
sarakāra buddhibidyā ubhei jiba.

Tikanga:
Arā, i te wā o te whakatūnga o te Dharma i te mutunga o te Kali Yuga, ka puta te hepohepo ki te Papa katoa tae atu ki ngā whenua nui e whitu. E pūhia ana me te tino riri nui, ka whakangaro ngā hau e whā tekau mā iwa i ngā hanganga katoa. I taua wā, e kore tētahi e haumaru. E kore te mana o te kāwanatanga me tōna mātauranga e whai hua. Ko te hunga anake e whai ana i te ara o te Dharma, ā, e tino tuku ana ki te Ariki Nui ka whiwhi tiaki.

Odia Sloka:

କର ପତ୍ର ଯୋଡି କହଇ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ଗୁରୁପାଦେ ଶିର ଦେଇ,
ପ୍ରଳୟ କାଳର ବାରଣ କହିବା କଳି ଶେଷ ହେବ ଯହିଁ।
ଶୁଣରେ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ଏମନ୍ତ କାଳ ଯେ ଦିନେ ଆସି ପହଞ୍ଚିବ,
ଦଶ ଗ୍ରାମ ମଧ୍ୟେ ଜଣେକ ରହିବେ ଦେବତାଙ୍କ ଆବିର୍ଭାବ।
ସେ କାଳେ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ଅଣଚାଶ ବାୟୁ ଭାଦ୍ରବ ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣିମା ଦିନ,
ଏମନ୍ତ ପବନ ବହିବ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ସକଳ ହୋଇବ ଲୀନ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Kara patra joḍi kahai Bāraṅga gurupāde śira dei,
praḷaya kāḷara bāraṇa kahibā kaḷi śeṣha heba jahiñ.
Śuṇare Bāraṅga emanta kāḷa je dine āsi pahañchiba,
daśha grāma madhye jaṇeka rahibe debatāṅka ābirbhāba.
Se kāḷe Bāraṅga aṇachāśha bāyu Bhādraba pūrṇimā dina,
emanta pabana bahiba Bāraṅga sakaḷa hoiba līna.

Tikanga:
I te pātainga a te ākonga a Baranga mō ngā mahi whakangaro a Kalki e puta ana i te mutunga o te Kali Yuga, i tuhi a Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ka kite te tangata i tētahi rā ka ora kotahi anake te tangata, pērā i te atua, i roto i ngā kāinga 10. I taua wā, ka pūhia ngā hau e whā tekau mā iwa me te tino tere, ā, ka whakangaro i ngā hanganga katoa. Ko tērā te rā o te marama kī katoa o te marama o Bhadra.

11.4 Waipuke Ahi (Agni Pralaya)

I tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika 'Gupta Kheda Malika':

Odia Sloka:

ଧନ, ସମ୍ପତ୍ତି ଓ ଟଙ୍କା ଯାହା ଅଛି ଜମା,
ସବୁଯିବ ପୋଡି ଭାଇ ନିଆଁର ଗଞ୍ଜମା।
ଚଉଦିଗେ ନିଆଁ କାହୁଁ ଦଗ୍ଧି ଏ ଧରଣୀ,
ଓଦା କଞ୍ଚା ନ ମାନିବ ସବୁ ଖାଏ ଅଗ୍ନି।
ଅଗ୍ନିର କରାଳ ରୂପ ଜୀବାତ୍ମା ଦେଖିବ,
ହାଁ ହାଁ ହୋଇ ତାର ପ୍ରାଣ ଛାଡିଯିବ।
ସମଗ୍ର ବିଶ୍ଵରେ ଧ୍ଵଂସ ଲୀଳୋ ଯେ ଚାଲିବ,
କେଉଁଠି କମ୍ ବେଶୀ ଜାଣେ ତା ଦଇବ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Dhana, sampatti o ṭaṅkā jāhā achi jamā,
sabujiba poḍi bhāi niāñra gañjamā.
Chaudige niāñ kāhuñ dagdhi e dharaṇī,
odā kañchā na māniba sabu khāe agni.
Agnira karāḷarūpa jībātmā dekhiba,
hāñ hāñ hoi tāra prāṇa chāḍijiba.
Samagra biśware dhwaṃsa līḷā je chāliba,
keuṇṭhi kam beśī jāne tā daiba.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka kitea te kanikani o te ahi i ngā taha e whā. Ka ngiha te Ao. He maha ngā tāngata ka mate. Ka ngiha ngā taonga, ngā rawa, me te moni hei pungarehu. Ka horapa ēnei mahi whakangaro ki te ao katoa, ka iti ake i ētahi wāhi, ka nui ake i ētahi atu.

11.5 Aituā Wāhi/Āhuarangi

Odia Sloka:

ନାନା ଉତ୍ପାତ ଯାକ ଅନ୍ତରୀକ୍ଷେ ହେବ,
ଭକ୍ତ ବିନା ଅନ୍ୟ କେହି ଜାଣି ନ ପାରିବ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

nānā utpāta jāka antarikhye heba,
bhakta binā anya kehi jāni na pāriba.

Tikanga:
I tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika 'Chakada Madana' e, i te wā mutunga o Kali Yuga, i te wā e haere tonu ana te whakatūnga o Dharma, ka maha ngā raruraru i te wāhi. Ka maha ngā panonitanga i ngā aorangi me ngā whetūrangi, tae atu ki ō rātou tūnga me ō rātou tere. I tuhi te Mahapurush e, haunga ngā kaimahi tapu, e kore e taea e te iwi whānui te mōhio ki ēnei mea.

I tuhi anō te Mahapurush mō tēnei kaupapa i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Amara Jumara Samhita’:

Odia Sloka:

କଳିଯୁଗ ଶେଷବେଳେ ପୃଥ୍ବୀ ଉତ୍ପାତ,
ରଣ ଗୋଳ ସେ କାଳେ ହୋଇବ ବହୁତ।
ଚାରିଦିଗମାନଙ୍କରେ ଲାଞ୍ଜାମାନ ପଡି,
ସେ କାଳେ ବହୁତ ଶବ୍ଦ ହେବ ଘଡ଼ଘଡ଼ି।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Kaḷiyuga śheṣhabeḷe pruthbī utpāta,
raṇa goḷa se kāḷe hoiba bahuta.
chāridigamānaṅkare lāñjāmāna paḍi,
se kāḷe bahuta śhabda heba ghaḍaghaḍi.

Tikanga:
Arā, i te wā mutunga o Kali Yuga, ka maha ngā raruraru me ngā pakanga nanakia i te Ao. Ka hinga ngā meteoro/asteroids i ngā taha e whā, ā, ka puta ngā oro i te wāhi kore.

11.6 Mate me te Urutā

I tuhi ngā Mahapurush i roto i ngā tuhinga Malika maha e, i te mutunga o Kali Yuga, ka maha ngā mate me ngā urutā e whakapiko i te hapori tangata ki ōna turi. I kī mārama te Malika e kore e angitu ngā tākuta ki te tātaritanga me te rongoā o aua mate me ngā urutā, ā, ka kore e whai hua ngā rongoā i hoatu e rātou.

I kī anō ngā Mahapurush e, i roto i te mooni, ka kawea mai ēnei mate me ngā urutā e ngā mana whaea pērā i a Yogamaya, Ashta Chandis, 64 Yoginis, Dakinis, Pisachinis, me ērā atu. Ka hīkoi ēnei mana whaea i te ao katoa i roto i ngā āhua ngāwari hei mate, ā, ka uru ki ngā tinana o te tangata.

Ka tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Bigyanakalpa’:

Odia Sloka:

ଦୁର୍ଭିକ୍ଷ ପଡିବ ଲୋକେ ଯିବେ ବହୁ ମରି,
ଚଉଷଠି ରୋଗ ଭ୍ରମୁଥିବେ ଫେରି ଫେରି।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Durbhikṣha paḍiba loke jibe bahu mari,
chauṣhaṭhi roga bhramuthibe pheri pheri.

Tikanga:
Arā, i te wā e whakatū ana i a Dharma i te mutunga o Kali Yuga, ka puta te matekai me te tauraki, ka mate ai te tini tangata. E ono tekau mā whā ngā momo mate ka horapa ki te ao katoa.

Ka tuhi anō a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i te tuhinga Malika ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ବାଡି ବେମାରୀରେ ଅନେକ ମରିବେ କହେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Bāḍi bemārīre aneka maribe kahe dīna Achyuta.

Tikanga:
Arā, i te mutunga o Kali Yuga, ka maha ngā mate ka waiho hei take mate mō te tini tangata.

I tuhi anō te Mahapurush i roto i tana ‘Jaiphula Malika’ me ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଅଚିହ୍ନା ରୋଗ ଘୋଟିବ,
ବଇଦର ବୁଦ୍ଧି ବଣା ହୋଇବ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ମହୌଷଧି କାମ ନ କରିବ।
ଅଚିହ୍ନା ବ୍ୟାଧି ଯେ ଜଗତ୍ରେ ଘୋଟିବ ବଇଦ ବଣା ହୋଇବେ,
ରୋଗ ନ ଚିହ୍ନିଣ ବଣା ହେଉଥିବେ ଅନେକ ପ୍ରାଣୀ ମରିବେ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Achinhā roga ghoṭiba,
baidara buddhi baṇā hoiba lo jāiphula,
mahauṣadhi kāma na kariba.
Achinhā byādhi je jagatre ghoṭiba baida baṇā hoibe,
roga na chinhiṇa baṇā heuthibe aneka prāṇī maribe.

Tikanga:
Arā, i te mutunga o Kali Yuga, ka puta mai ngā mate kāore i mōhiotia, ka kore e whai hua te mātauranga o ngā tākuta. E kore ēnei mate e tautohutia, ā, ka kore e whai hua ngā rongoā ki te rongoā i a rātou, nō reira ka mate te tini tangata.

Ka tuhi te Mahapurush i roto i tētahi atu o ana tuhinga Malika:

Odia Sloka:

ରୋଗ ରୂପେ ଯୋଗମାୟା ଶରୀରେ ଖେଳିବ,
କାହାର ଆୟତ ପୁଣି ସେଠାରେ ନ ଥିବ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Roga rūpe Yogamāyā sharīre kheḷiba,
kāhāra āyata puṇi seṭhāre na thiba.

Tikanga:
Arā, i te mutunga o Kali Yuga, ka uru a Whaea Yogamaya ki ngā tinana o te tangata i roto i te āhua o te mate.

Ka tuhi anō te Mahapurush:

Odia Sloka:

ଗଦ ମହୌଷଧି କରିବନି କାର୍ଯ୍ୟ ମାଡି ଆସିବଟି ରୋଗ,
ସେ କାଳେ ଜାଣିବୁ ଅଳ୍ପ ଦିନେ ପୃଥ୍ବୀକୁ ପ୍ରଳୟ ଯୋଗ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Gada mahauṣhadhi karibani kārjya māḍi āsibaṭi roga,
Se kāḷe jānibu alpa dine pruthbīku praḷaya yoga.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka puta mai ngā mate-urutā pērā e kore e whai hua ngā rongoā ki te rongoā i a rātou, katahi ka mōhiotia kua tata te wā o te whakakorenga i te Ao.

Ka tuhi anō te Mahapurush:

Odia Sloka:

ବ୍ୟାଧି ହୋଇ ପିଶାଚୀ ଆସିବେ,
ପ୍ରାଣୀମାନଙ୍କୁ ଗୁପ୍ତେ ନାଶିବେ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Byādhi hoi piśhāchī āsibe,
prāṇīmānanku gupte nāśhibe.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka uru puku ngā mana whaea ki ngā tinana i roto i te āhua o te mate, ā, ka whakangaro i te tini tangata. Engari i te taha o tēnei, i kōrero anō te Mahapurush i roto i tana tuhinga Malika i te huarahi e mawhiti ai te hapori tangata i ēnei mate me ngā urutā.

Ka tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Chausathi Patala’:

Odia Sloka:

ସତ୍ୟ ଶାନ୍ତି ଦୟା କ୍ଷମାରେ ଯେଉଁ ପ୍ରାଣୀ ରହିବେ,
ରୋଗ କବଳରେ କେବେହିଁ ସେମାନେ ନ ପଡିବେ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Satya śhānti dayā kṣhamāre jeuñ prāṇī rahibe,
roga kabaḷare kebehiñ semāne na paḍibe.

Tikanga:
Arā, ko aua tāngata e whai ana i ngā tikanga rangatira pērā i te pono, te rangimārie, te atawhai, me te murunga hara i roto i ō rātou whanonga, ka noho wātea i ēnei mate.

11.7 Pakanga o te Ao

I tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Jaiphula Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଲାଗିବ ମହାସମର,
ଦେଶ ଦେଶ ମଧ୍ୟେ ଅତି ପ୍ରବଳ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ପୃଥିବୀ ହୋଇଯିବ ନାରଖାର।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Lāgiba mahāsamara,
deśha deśha madhye ati prabala lo jāiphula,
pruthibī hoijiba nārakhāra.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka puta he pakanga nui i te Ao. Ka puta he pakanga nanakia i waenganui i ngā iwi, ka whakangaro i te whenua.

I tuhi anō a Achyutananda Das ji i roto i ‘Jaiphula Malika’ tonu:

Odia Sloka:

ଦେଶ ଦେଶ ମଧ୍ୟେ ପୁଣ,
ଭୟଙ୍କର ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହେବ ପ୍ରମାଣ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ଏହା କଳି ଶେଷ ବୋଲି ଜାଣ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Deśha deśha madhye puṇa,
bhayaṅkara juddha heba pramāṇa lo jāiphula,
ehā kaḷi śeṣha boli jāṇa.

Tikanga:
Arā, i te wā mutunga o Kali Yuga, ka pakaru mai ngā pakanga tino nanakia i waenganui i ngā whenua rerekē. Arā, ka tīmata he pakanga whakamataku i waenganui i ngā iwi, me mōhio kua tata te mutunga o Kali Yuga.

Ka tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଅଣୁ ପରମାଣୁ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଭାରି ଭୟଙ୍କର,
ଗୋଟିଏ ଅଣୁ ବୋମାର କରାମତି ସାର।
ଲକ୍ଷ ଲକ୍ଷ ଅଣୁବୋମା ଗର୍ଜିବ ମହୀରେ,
କାନ୍ଦିବ ଧରଣୀ ରାଣୀ ହା ହୁତାଶରେ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Aṇu paramāṇu juddha bhāri bhayaṅkara,
goṭie aṇu bomāra karāmati sāra.
lakṣha lakṣha aṇubomā garjiba mahīre,
kāndiba dharaṇī rāṇī hā hutāśhare.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka puta he pakanga karihi-ngota i te Ao, he tino whakamataku. Ka wiri te Ao katoa i te whakamahinga o ngā poma karihi kore e taea te tatau. Ka tangi a Whaea Papa.

E kī ana a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das e, i tēnei wā, ka puta he pakanga nanakia i waenganui i Inia me Pakistan. E ai ki te Mahapurush, ka huri tēnei pakanga i waenganui i Inia me Pakistan hei pakanga o te ao, nā te mea he maha ngā whenua ka tautoko i a Pakistan i roto i tēnei pakanga, engari ko ētahi whenua ka whawhai mō Inia. I roto i tēnei pakanga, ka wehea ngā iwi nui o te ao ki ngā puni e rua.

Ka tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଚୀନ ପାକିସ୍ଥାନ ପୁଣି ଆମେରିକା ଜାଣ,
ଋଷିଆ ଭାରତ ପୁଣି ଜର୍ମାନ ଜାପାନ।
ଭାରତ ସପକ୍ଷ ହେବେ ମହାଶକ୍ତି ଜାଣ,
ଇରାକ ଯେ ସମର୍ଥନ ଦେବ ପାକିସ୍ଥାନ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Chīna Pākisthāna puṇi Āmerikā jāṇa,
Ruṣhiā Bhārata puṇi Jarmāna Jāpāna.
Bhārata sapakṣha hebe mahāśhakti jāṇa,
Irāka je samarthana deba Pākisthāna.

Tikanga:
Arā, i roto i tēnei pakanga, ka tautoko ngā whenua pērā i Haina, Amerika, me Iraq i a Pakistan, engari ko ngā mana pērā i Ruhia, Tiamana, me Hapani ka whawhai mō Inia.

Ka tuhi te Mahapurush i roto i te tuhinga Malika ko ‘Bhabishyat Malika’ te ingoa:

Odia Sloka:

ଏହି ଭାରତ ସଙ୍ଗେ ପାକିସ୍ଥାନଟି ରଙ୍ଗେ,
ମହାସମର କରିବ ଜାଣିଥା ବେଗେ।
ପୁଣି ଚୀନ୍ ସଇନୁ ତୁର୍କୀ ଇରାନ ଜାଣ,
ବ୍ରିଟିଶ ଯେ ସଇନ୍ୟ ସବେ ମିଳିଣ।
ପାକସ୍ଥିାନକୁ ବଳ ଅସ୍ତ୍ର ସର୍ବେ ଆବର,
ପାକିସ୍ଥାନକୁ ଦେବେ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଲାଗିବ ଘୋର।
ଏହା ଦେଖି ରୁଷିଆ ଆସି ହୋଇବ ଠିଆ,
ଭାରତ ସମରକ୍ଷେତ୍ରେ ସେ ଏକୁଟିଆ।
ଅସ୍ତ୍ର ଶସ୍ତ୍ର ସଇନ୍ୟ ଯୋଗାଇ ଦେବ ଜାଣ,
ଭାରତ ସହିତ ମିଶି କରିବ ରଣ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Ehi Bhārata saṅge Pākisthānṭi raṅge,
mahāsamara kariba jāṇithā bege.
Puṇi Chīna sainu Turkī Irāna jāṇa,
Briṭisha je sainya sabe miḷiṇa.
Pākisthānku baḷa astra sarbe ābara,
Pākisthānku debe juddha lāgiba ghora.
Ehā dekhi Ruṣiā āsi hoiba ṭhiā,
Bhārata samarakṣetre se ekuṭiā.
Astra shastra sainya jogāi deba jāṇa,
Bhārata sahita mishi kariba raṇa.

Tikanga:
I tuhi te Mahapurush e, ka whawhai nanakia tētahi whenua ko Pakistan te ingoa ki a Inia. I roto i tēnei pakanga, ka āwhina ngā whenua pērā i Haina, Turkey, Iran, Ingarangi, me Amerika i a Pakistan, ā, ka hoatu tautoko hōia. Ka kite i ēnei mea katoa, ka tae tuatahi mai, ko Ruhia anake, te hoa pai rawa o Inia, ki te āwhina i a Inia. Ka hoatu ki a Inia ngā momo patu katoa, te mahi tahi ohaoha, me te tautoko hōia, ā, ka whawhai nanakia ki ngā iwi hoariri mō Inia.

I tuhi te Mahapurush e, ehara i te mea ko Ruhia anake, engari ka whawhai anō a Hapani me Tiamana mō Inia i roto i tēnei pakanga. I roto i ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’ tonu, ka tuhi anō ia:

Odia Sloka:

ଜାପାନର ରାଗ ଅଛି ହିରୋଶିମା କଥା,
ପକାଇଥିଲା ବୋମା ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ଜାଣିଥା।
ସେହି କ୍ରୋଧ ଲିଭିନାହିଁ ଜାପାନର ଜାଣ,
ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ଭାରତ ପଟ ନେବେ ସେହି ପୁଣ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Jāpānara rāga achi Hirośhimā kathā,
pakāithilā bomā juddhe jāṇithā.
Sehi krodha libhināhiñ Jāpānara jāṇa,
juddhe Bhārata paṭa nebe sehi puṇa.

Tikanga:
Arā, e kore e wareware i a Hapani te whakataka o te poma karihi ki runga i a ia e Amerika. E kore e marino te riri o Hapani ki a Amerika. Ka ngiha tonu te mura o te ngaki i roto i tōna ngākau, nō reira ka whawhai a Hapani ki a Amerika i te taha o Inia i te wā o te pakanga o te ao tuatoru. I runga i te tono a Hapani, ka whawhai anō a Tiamana i te taha o Inia.

12 Te Pakanga e Toe ana o Mahabharata i Kali Yuga

I tuhi ngā Panchasakhas i roto i ngā tuhinga Bhabishya Malika e, ko te Pakanga o te Ao e puta ana i te mutunga o Kali Yuga, ko te pakanga toenga o te Mahabharata. I whakamārama ngā Mahapurush e, kāore i oti katoa te pakanga 18-rā o Mahabharata i te mutunga o Dwapara Yuga, nā te mea, i taua wā, i whakatau a Ariki Krishna kia kaua e whakahaere i te wāhanga whakamutunga o te pakanga i reira, engari kia oti i te mutunga o Kali Yuga.

Ehara i te mea ko tēnei anake, kua tuhia e ngā Mahapurushas i roto i ngā taipitopito mō tēnei pakanga i roto i ā rātou tuhinga Malika, ā, kua whakaaturia e rātou ngā mea maha, pērā i te wā ka tū, e hia ngā tāngata ka mate i roto i taua pakanga, he aha te roanga o te wā, he aha te āhua ka mau, nā wai te kaiārahi ka tū, ko wai ka whai wāhi mai i te taha o Inia, kei hea ka mutu, me he aha ka tū i muri i tēnei pakanga, me ētahi atu.

12.1 Āhea tū ai tēnei pakanga?

Odia Sloka:

ଯୁଗଶେଷ କଳି ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ ଘୋର,
ଯମ ମାଆ ସାତ ଭଉଣୀ ଧରିଥିବେ ଖପର।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Yugaśheṣha kaḷi Bhārata juddha hoiba ghora,
Yama māā sāta bhauṇī dharithibe khapara.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka oti te pakanga e toe ana o Mahabharata i te mutunga o Kali Yuga. I roto i tēnei pakanga, ka whakangaro a "Yama Maa Sata Bhauni" rānei ngā Atua Whaea Tokowhitu (Saptamatruka) e noho ana i te Biraja Khetra (Chamunda, Varahi, Indrani, Vaishnavi, Brahmani, Kaumari, Maheshwari, me Narasimhi) i ngā tangata hara.

I te whakamārama i te wā ka tū tēnei pakanga i te mutunga o Kali Yuga, i tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଆସି ଦେଉଥିବ ଘାଣ୍ଟି ଭାରତ ଲଢ଼ାଇ ହେବ,
ଦଶ ଅଣା ଯିବ ଛଅ ଅଣା ରହିବ ମୋ ବଚନ ଅଟେ ଧ୍ରୁବ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Mīna Shani āsi deuthibā ghāṇṭi Bhārata laḍhāi heba,
Dasha aṇā jiba chha-a aṇā rahiba mo bachana aṭe dhruba.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka tīmata te whawhai e toe ana o Inia (Mahabharata) i te wā o te hononga Meena-Shani (Pisces-Saturn), ka mate te tini o ngā tāngata i reira.

12.2 He aha te āhua ka tū ai te pakanga, ā, kei hea ka whawhaitia i te wāhanga whakamutunga?

Ka tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika:

Odia Sloka:

ମହାଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଓଳିଏ ବାକି ଅଛି ଜାଣ,
କଳିଯୁଗରେ ବିଶ୍ୱଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇ ପୂରଣ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Mahābhārata juddha oḷie bāki achi jāṇa,
Kaḷiyugare biśhwajuddha hoi pūraṇa.

Tikanga:
Arā, kia mōhio kei te toe tonu tētahi wāhanga o te pakanga Mahabharata, ka oti hei Pakanga o te Ao i Kali Yuga.

Ka tuhi anō te Mahapurush i roto i tana ‘Jaiphula Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ,
ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଦେଶରେ ପୁଣି ହୋଇବ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
କହେ ଯବନ ବାହି ଆସିବ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Oḷie Bhārata juddha,
Oḍiśhā deśhare puṇi hoiba lo jāiphula,
kahe jabana bāhi āsiba.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka tū te pakanga e toe ana o Mahabharata ki Odisha, ā, i taua wā, he maha ngā hōia o ngā iwi hoariri ka uru ki roto o Inia.

Ka tuhi a Saint Achyutananda Das i roto i tētahi atu o ana tuhinga Malika:

Odia Sloka:

ଏ କଳିଯୁଗରେ ମହାଭାରତଟି ଉତ୍କଳ ଭୂଇଁ ପ୍ରମାଣ,
ଉତ୍କଳରୁ ସବୁ ଉତ୍ପତ୍ତି ହୋଇବ ଠିକେ ଠିକେ ଏହା ଶୁଣ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

E kaḷiyugare Mahābhārataṭi Utkaḷa bhūiñ pramāṇa,
Utkaḷaru sabu utpatti hoiba ṭhike ṭhike ehā śhuṇa.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka whawhaitia te pakanga e toe ana o Mahabharata i Kali Yuga, ka tū ki te whenua o Utkala, o Odisha rānei.

12.3 He aha te roanga o tēnei pakanga?

I matapae anō a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Amara Jumara Samhita’ ka roa tēnei pakanga e toe ana o Mahabharata mō te 13 marama katoa.

I tuhi te Mahapurush:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ଯେ ରହିଥିବ ଶୁଣ ହେତୁ ଦ୍ୱାରେ,
ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ ସେତିକି ବେଳରେ।
ସେହି ଓଳିକରେ ନାଥ କଳି ଶେଷ ହେବ,
ସେ ଓଳିକେ ତେର ମାସ ଭୋଗ ଯେ ହୋଇବ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Olie je rahithiba shuṇa hetu dwāre,
Bhārata juddha hoiba setiki xelare.
Sehi olikare nātha kali shesha hexa,
Se olike tera māsa bhoga je hoiba.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka roa te pakanga e toe ana o Mahabharata mō te tekau mā toru marama katoa, ā, ka oti taua pakanga, ka mutu rawa te mana o Kali Yuga i runga i tēnei Ao.

12.4 Ko wai ka whai wāhi mai i te taha o Inia i te wāhanga whakamutunga?

I tuhi ngā Mahapurushas i te wāhanga whakamutunga o tēnei pakanga, i tētahi taha ka noho ngā ope taua nunui o ngā iwi hoariri, i tētahi atu taha, ka noho ngā toa toa katoa o te wā Mahabharata me ngā mano tini o ngā wairua tapu, ngā tohunga, me ngā matakite i raro i te kaiārahi o te Ariki Nui a Kalki tonu.

I tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana ‘Bhabishyat Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଏହି ବେଳେ ଭାରତ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ହାରିଯିବ ତ,
ହାହାକାର ପଡ଼ିଯିବ ଚାରିଆଡ଼େ ତ।
ଭାରତ ନରନାରୀ ସର୍ବେ ଡାକିବେ ହରି,
ରକ୍ଷାକର ପ୍ରଭୁ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ଗଲୁଟି ସରି।
ଆହେ କୁଞ୍ଜବିହାରୀ ବାରେ ଶୁଣ ଗୁହାରି,
ବଇକୁଣ୍ଠ ତେଜି ଥରେ ଆସ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି।
ଦେଖ ନେତ୍ର ଫେଡ଼ିତ ପୁଣ୍ୟଭୂମି ଭାରତ,
ବିଦେଶୀଙ୍କ ହସ୍ତେ ହୁଏ କି ହନ୍ତସନ୍ତ।
ଭାରତର କାଳର ଶୁଣି ସେ ଆଦିମୂଳ,
ବଇକୁଣ୍ଠ ପୁର ତେଜି ହେବେ ବାହାର।
କଳ୍କୀ ଦେବ ନାମ ବହି ସେ ନାରାୟଣ,
ଉତ୍କଳ ଦେଶ କଟକରେ ଦେବେ ଦର୍ଶନ।
ସେହି ବେଳରେ ଜାଣ ଶ୍ରୀକୃଷ୍ଣ ବଳରାମ,
ଭୀମ ସହ ବାହାରିବେ ବୀର ଅର୍ଜୁନ।
ଭୀଷ୍ମ ଦ୍ରୋଣ ଆବର ଅଶ୍ୱତ୍ଥାମା ଯେ ବୀର,
କର୍ଣ୍ଣ ଶଲ୍ୟ ଜୟଦ୍ରଥ ହେବେ ବାହାର।
ବେଲାଲସେନ ବୀର ପୁଣି ହେବ ବାହାର,
ବାଳବୀର ଅଭିମନ୍ୟୁ ଘେନି ସଙ୍ଗର।
ବୀର ବବ୍ରୁବାହନ ବାହାରିବଟି ଜାଣ,
ଏକଲବ୍ୟ ଘଟୋତ୍କଚ ସଙ୍ଗେ ଘେନିଣ।
ସର୍ବେ ହୋଇଣ ମେଳି ବିଦେଶୀୟଙ୍କୁ ଦଳି,
ଦେବେ ମୁହୂର୍ତ୍ତକ ମଧ୍ଯେ ଲଗାଇ କଳି।
ମନ୍ତ୍ର ଶକତି ବଳେ ଅପଲକ ନେତ୍ରରେ,
ଚାହିଁ ବିଜ୍ଞାନ ଶକ୍ତିକି ଜିଣିବେ ହେଳେ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Ehibeḷe Bhārata nishche hārijiba ta,
hāhākāra paḍijiba chāriāḍe ta.
Bhārata naranārī sarbe dākibe Hari,
rakhākara prabhu nishche galuṭi sari.
Āhe Kuñjabihārī bāre shuṇa guhāରି,
Baikuṇṭha teji thare āsa Shrī Hari.
Dekha netra pheḍita puṇyabhūmi Bhārata,
bideshīṅka haste hue ki hantasanta.
Bhāratara kāḷara shuṇi se ādimūḷa,
Baikuṇṭhapura teji hebe bāhāra.
Kalkī deba nāma bahi se Nārāyaṇa,
Utkaḷa desha Kaṭakare debe darshana.
Sehibeḷere jāṇa Shrī Kṛṣṇa Baḷarāma,
Bhīma saha bāhāribe bīra Arjuna.
Bhīṣma Droṇa ābara Ashwatthāmā je bīra,
Karṇa Shalya Jayadratha hebe bāhāra.
Belāḷasena bīra puṇi hebe bāhāra,
bāḷabīra Abhimanyu gheni saṅgara.
Bīra Babhrubāhana bāhāribaṭi jāṇa,
Ekalabya Ghaṭotkacha saṅge gheniṇa.
Sarbe hoiṇa meḷi bideshīyaṅku daḷi,
debe muhūrttaka madhye lagāi kaḷi.
Mantra shakti baḷe apalaka netrare,
chāhi bijñāna shaktiki jiṇibe heḷe.

Tikanga:
Arā, i te wā o te Pakanga Tuatoru o te Ao, i te kitenga i ngā whakaeke whā-taha ki Inia e ngā hoariri o tāwāhi, ka mataku ngā tāngata o Inia, ā, ka tangi i roto i te pōuri. I te kore kaha, ka mahara rātou katoa ki te Ariki, ā, ka inoi mō te tiaki. Ka kī rātou—E te Ariki, whakarongo mai ki tā mātou inoi, whakaorangia mātou, waiho a Vaikuntha ka haere mai ki te Ao ki te tiaki i a mātou.

I te oho i tēnei karanga aroha o ngā ākonga tapu, me te kitenga i te whakaekenga o Inia e ngā hoariri o tāwāhi, ka puta mai te Brahman Nui, te Ariki Narayana (Maha Vishnu) tonu, i roto i tana whakaahua Kalki, ki Cuttack, Odisha, ā, ka hoatu he darshan ki ngā ākonga tapu.

I tuhi te Mahapurush, i te taha o te Ariki Kalki i taua wā anō, ka whawhai a Bhima, Arjuna, Bhishma, Drona, Ashwatthama, Karna, Shalya, Jayadratha, Barbarika, Abhimanyu, Babhruvahana, Ekalavya, me Ghatotkacha, kua whānau kē, i te taha o Inia, ā, ka tino hinga i ngā iwi hoariri. Ka kaha ake te mana mantra atua o ēnei toa toa i te mana pūtaiao hou o ngā hoariri.

Mō tēnei kaupapa anō, i tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ପ୍ରସ୍ତାବିତ କଳି ଭାରତ ସରିବ ହେବ ଭାରତ ସମର।
ତିନିଯୁଗ ଯୋଦ୍ଧା ଏକ ଯୁଗରେ ମହାସମର ଲାଗିବ,
କହଇ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଭକ୍ତ ହେବେ ମୁକ୍ତ ମନୋସ୍କାମନା ପୂରିବ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Prastābita kaḷi Bhārata sariba heba Bhārata samara.
Tinijuga joddhā eka jugare mahāsamara lāgiba,
