kahai Achyuta bhakta hebe mukta manoskāmanā pūriba.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka whawhaitia te pakanga o Mahabharata i Kali Yuga, ka whai wāhi mai ngā toa toa o ngā tau e toru o mua.

Ka tuhi anō a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i te ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ଭାରତ ସମର ହେବ ଦେଲି ଚେତାଇ,
ସ୍ୱୟଂ ପରଂବ୍ରହ୍ମ ମୂରତି ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜଗତସାଇଁ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Oḷie Bhārata samara heba deli chetāi,
swayaṃ Parambrahma mūrati prabhu Jagatasāiñ.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka tū te pakanga e toe ana o Mahabharata i raro i te kaiārahi o te Brahman Nui, te Ariki Narayana (whakaahua Kalki) tonu.

Mō tēnei kaupapa, i tuhi anō tētahi o ngā Panchasakhas, a Mahapurush Yashovanta Das, i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Nidagha Nighanta’:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ରହି ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହେବ,
ଆଦେଶ ଦେବେଟି ମାଧବ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Oḷie rahi juddha heba,
ādeśha debeṭi Mādhaba.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka oti te pakanga e toe ana o Mahabharata, ā, ka hoatu e Madhava tonu, te Ariki Nui a Kalki, te whakahau kia tīmata tēnei pakanga.

I tuhi anō tētahi wairua tapu nui o Odisha i whānau i te rautau 20, a Thakur Shri Abhiram Paramahansa, mō tēnei kaupapa i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Baishnaba Gita’:

Odia Sloka:

ତୋପର ତପର ସମର,
ହୋଇବ ଅତି ଭୟଙ୍କର।
ତପକୁ ତୋପ ନ ପାରିବ,
ତପର ବଳ ବେଶୀ ହେବ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Topara tapara samara,
hoiba ati bhayaṅkara.
tapaku topa na pāriba,
tapara baḷa beśhī heba.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka tū he pakanga kaha i waenganui i te mana pū o ngā iwi hoariri o Inia me te mana tapu (Tapasya) o ngā wairua tapu me ngā tohunga maha o Inia, ka kore e whai hua te mana pere hou o ngā hoariri i mua i te kaha tapu o Inia, ā, i te mutunga ka puta a Inia hei toa i roto i tēnei pakanga nui.

12.5 Kei hea ka oti te pakanga i Inia?

I tuhi ngā Mahapurushas i roto i ngā tuhinga Malika, ka tae ngā ope taua o tāwāhi ki Odisha, ka whawhai rātou ki ngā wāhi rerekē o Odisha, pērā i te nuinga o te wā ki Birat Gadi o Cuttack, Choudwar, Jobra, Barabati, me Kaliaboda; ki Puri; ki Jajpur; ki Gohira Tikiri o Bhadrak; ki Paradip; ki Balasore, me ētahi atu.

Ahakoa ka tū tēnei pakanga ki ēnei wāhi katoa i Odisha, i kī mārama a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ka oti katoa ki Khandagiri i Bhubaneswar i te wā ka whakangaro te Ariki Nui tonu i ngā hōia katoa o ngā iwi hoariri ki tana Sudarshana Chakra.

I tuhi te Mahapurush i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Kalpatika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଦେଶରେ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଠାରେ ବହୁତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ,
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ରୂପ ଧରି ପ୍ରଭୁ ଚକ୍ର ଧରି ବିନାଶିବ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Oḍiśhā deśhare Khaṇḍagiri ṭhāre bahuta juddha hoiba,
Ananta Keśharī rūpa dhari prabhu chakra dhari bināśhiba.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka tū he pakanga tino kaha ki Khandagiri i Odisha. I taua wāhi anō, ka whakangaro te Ariki Nui a Kalki i ngā hōia maha ki tana Sudarshana Chakra.

12.6 He aha ka tū i roto i tēnei pakanga, ā, he aha ka tū i muri iho?

I roto i te ‘Amara Jumara Samhita’, i tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i tētahi irava ko tōna wāhanga tuatahi e whai ake nei:

Odia Sloka:

ସେହି ଓଳିକରେ ନାଥ କଳି ଶେଷ ହେବ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Sehi oḷikare nātha kaḷi śheṣha heba.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka oti noa tēnei pakanga o Mahabharata, ka mutu te mana o Kali Yuga.

Nā, ka tuhi anō a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i tētahi irava i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Chakada Madana’, ko tōna rārangi tuatahi e kī ana:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଯେ ସରିବ ପୃଥିବୀ ଶାନ୍ତ ହୋଇବ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Oḷie Bhārata juddha je sariba pruthibī śhānta hoiba.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka oti te pakanga e toe ana o Mahabharata, ka noho rangimārie te Ao. Nā, ka mutu katoa ngā raruraru i runga i te Ao.

12.7 Whakaahuatanga o te āhua o ngā mana nui pērā i Amerika me Uropi i te Pakanga o te Ao

I tuhia e ngā Panchasakha Mahapurushas i roto i ā rātou tuhinga Malika ka tino kaha te Pakanga Tuatoru o te Ao, ka pā ki ngā iwi katoa. Ko ngā whenua katoa e kiia nei i ēnei rā ko ngā mana tino kaha o te ao, ka waiho e tēnei pakanga mutunga o Kali Yuga rātou kia ngaro.

E whakaahua ana i ngā āhuatanga o Amerika i tēnei pakanga, i tuhia e Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଆମେରିକା ରାଷ୍ଟ୍ର ଭାଇ ମୂଳୁ ଧ୍ୱଂସ ଯିବ,
ଗର୍ବରେ ଜଳୁଛି ରାଜା ବିଶ୍ୱ ମୁଖ୍ୟ ହେବ।
ଧରାକୁ ମଣୁଛି ସରା କହେ ମୁହିଁ ଶ୍ରେଷ୍ଠ,
ଉଡ଼ାଇବି ପୃଥିବୀକୁ କରେ ମୁଁ ଶପଥ।
କାହାରି ଗର୍ବ ସହି ନ ପାରିବେ ଧାତା,
ଜଳାଇ ଦେବେ ତାହାକୁ ଉଡ଼ାଇବେ ମଥା।
ନ୍ୟୁୟର୍କ ସହର ଧ୍ୱଂସ ପଦା ହୋଇଯିବ,
ହାହାକାର ପଡ଼ିଯିବ ଅଥର୍ବ ନିର୍ବେଦ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Āmerikā rāṣhṭra bhāi mūḷu dhwaṅsa jiba,
garbare jaḷuchi rājā biśhwa mukhya heba.
dharāku maṇuchi sarā kahe muiñ śhreṣhṭha,
uḍāibi pruthibīku kare muñ śhapatha.
kāhāri garba sahi na pāribe dhātā,
jaḷāi debe tāhāku uḍāibe mathā.
New York sahara dhwaṅsa padā hoijiba,
hāhākāra paḍijiba atharba nirbeda.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka whakaaro tētahi iwi ko Amerika te kaiārahi o te ao. Ka ruku ki te whakapehapeha me te whakakake, ka kī ia ko ia te mea pai rawa atu, ā, ka whakatuma ki te pupuhi i ētahi atu whenua i te pakanga. I kī anō te Mahapurush e kore te Ariki Nui e whakaae ki te whakapehapeha o tētahi, nō reira ka pakaru hoki i a ia te whakakake o Amerika. Ka ngaro te Tāone o New York o Amerika, ā, ka tūpono taua iwi ki te ngaronga katoa hei kaiwhakaatu mō te whakangaro kino.

I roto i te ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’ tonu, ka tuhi anō a Saint Achyutananda Das:

Odia Sloka:

ଆମେରିକା ଇଉରୋପ ମହାଦେଶ ଜାଣ,
ସ୍ଥିତି ହରାଇବ ତାର ବିଷାକ୍ତ ପବନ।
ସବୁରି ଧ୍ୱଂସର କାରଣ ଆମେରିକା ଜାଣ,
ନିଶ୍ଚିହ୍ନ ହୋଇବ ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ଗର୍ବି ଅଟେ ପୁଣ।
ନିଜର ଇଜ୍ଜତ ପୃଥିବୀ ପରେ ଦେଖାଇଲା,
ଯୁଦ୍ଧକୁ ତ ଜୀବନରେ ସିଦ୍ଧହସ୍ତ କଲା।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Āmerikā Iuropa mahādesha jāṇa,
Sthiti harāiba tāra biṣākta pabana.
Saburi dhwaṃsara kāraṇa Āmerikā jāṇa,
Nishchinha hoiba juddhe garbi aṭe puṇa.
Nijara ijjata pṛthwī pare dekhāilā,
Juddhaku ta jībanare siddhahasta kalā.

Tikanga:
Arā, ko te take o te Pakanga Tuatoru o te Ao he iwi ko ‘Amerika’ te ingoa. Ka whakatū taua iwi i tōna ake mana me tōna whakakake ki runga i te Ao katoa, ā, ka riro i a ia te mātanga ki te whawhai. I tuhi anō te Mahapurush nā tēnei whakakake, ka ngaro tōna ingoa me tōna tohu i te Pakanga Tuatoru o te Ao. I muri i te pakanga, ka paitini te āhuarangi i reira, ā, nā tērā hoki, ka mate te tini tangata i ngā whenua o Amerika me Uropi.

Ka tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana ‘Bhabishyat Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଶେଷେ ଇଉରୋପ ଦେଶ ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ହୋଇବ ଧ୍ୱଂସ,
ବଞ୍ଚି ରହିବେ କେବଳ ଭକ୍ତ ଅଂଶ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Śheṣhe Iuropa deśha juddhe hoiba dhwaṅsa,
bañchi rahibe kebaḷa bhakta aṅśha.

Tikanga:
Arā, i tēnei pakanga, ka tino ngaro te whenua o Uropi. Haunga ngā kaingākau o te Ariki, e kore tētahi e ora i aua iwi.

I roto i te ‘Jaiphula Malika’, ka tuhi a Saint Achyutananda Das:

Odia Sloka:

ପଶ୍ଚିମ ଉତ୍ତର ଦେଶ,
ହାଡ଼ ମାଳ ହୋଇଯିବ ବିଶେଷ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ମହୀ ଦୁଲୁ ଦୁଲୁ ହେବ ଧ୍ୱଂସ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Paśhchima uttara deśha,
hāḍa māḷa hoijiba biśheṣha lo jāiphula,
mahī dulu dulu heba dhwaṅsa.

Tikanga:
Arā, ko ngā whenua kei te Hauāuru me te Raki ka ngaro i te Pakanga o te Ao.

Ka tuhi anō a Achyutananda Das ji i roto i te ‘Jaiphula Malika’ tonu:

Odia Sloka:

ଜର୍ମାନୀ ରୁଷିଆ ଆଦି,
ଇଟାଲୀ ଜାପାନ ତୁରସ୍କ ଗାଦି ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ସେହି ସରବେ ହୋଇବେ ରଦ୍ଦି।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Jarmānī Ruṣhiā ādi,
Itālī Jāpāna Turuska gādi lo jāiphula,
sehi sarabe hoibe raddi.

Tikanga:
Arā, ko Tiamana, Ruhia, Itari, Hapani, me Turkey, me ērā atu iwi, ka tino ngaro i te Pakanga o te Ao.

Ka rite ki tā mātou i kī ai i mua, ko ngā kaupapa katoa i runga ake nei ka tū i te wā o Meena-Shani yoga, arā, i waenga i ngā tau 2025 me 2028.

I te 29 o Maehe, 2025, ka uru a Saturn ki te tohu zodiac o Pisces (Meena). I ētahi wā ka neke whakamuri (Vakri) rānei, i ētahi wā ka neke tika (Margi) rānei, ka noho a Saturn ki te tohu zodiac o Pisces tae noa ki te 23 o Pepuere, 2028. I taua wā, mai i te 3 o Hune, 2027, ki te 20 o Oketopa, 2027, ka tū tētahi whakawhitinga poto o Saturn ki te tohu zodiac o Aries (Mesha).

Ināianei ka ara ake tētahi pātai nui i konei. Kua tata ki te 10 marama mai i te tīmatanga o te Meena-Shani yoga, engari kāore anō kia tīmata ēnei kaupapa whakangaro. He aha ai? Ae rā, kei te tū ngā aituā tūturu puta noa i te ao, kei te haere tonu hoki ngā pakanga i waenga i ētahi whenua, engari kāore i te tū i te taumata nui i matapaehia ai e ngā Panchasakhas i roto i ngā tuhinga Malika! Ka ara ake te pātai i roto i ngā hinengaro o te tini o tātou, ā, āhea ka kaha ake te taumata o ēnei kaupapa!

Āhea tonu ka tīmata te Whakangaro Nui? He aha te kī a Bhabishya Malika mō tēnei kaupapa? E kī ana a Malika ka tū ngā mahi whakangaro i Meena-Shani, engari e whakaahua ana a Malika i te tīmatanga o ēnei mahi whakangaro hei wāhanga whakamutunga o te tau 2026, kaua ki te tau 2025.

Ko tētahi o ngā Panchasakhas, a Mahapurush Yashovanta Das, i tuhi mō ngā raruraru kāore anō kia kitea ka tū puta noa i te ao i te wā o Meena-Shani i roto i āna tuhinga Malika, ā, i hoatu kōrero mō tō rātou wā tika. I roto i tētahi o āna tuhinga Malika, i tuhi ia:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଯୋଗରେ ଯେ ଦ୍ୱିତୀୟ ଚରଣ,
ଅକାଳେ ମଡ଼କ ପଡ଼ିବ ସେହି ସମୟରେ ଜାଣ।
ଭୂମିକମ୍ପରେ କମ୍ପିବ ସକଳ ମେଦିନୀ,
କାଳ ଭୈରବୀ ଛାଡ଼ିବେ ଶୂନ୍ୟରେ ଯେ ରଡ଼ି।
ମୀନ ଶନି ଯୋଗରେ ଯେ ତୃତୀୟ ଚରଣ,
ଜଳ ପ୍ରଳୟ ହୋଇବ ଭଲେ ତୁହି ଜାଣ।
ହୁତୁ ହୁତୁ ହୋଇ ଜଳୁଥିବେ ହିଙ୍ଗୁଳା ମାତିବେ,
ବଡ଼ ବଡ଼ କୋଠା ଘର ଜାଳି ଯେ ଦେବେ।
ଚୋର ଟାଉଟର ସଂଖ୍ୟା ପ୍ରବଳ ବଢ଼ିବ,
ସେଥିପାଇଁ ଦେବୀଙ୍କର କୋପେ ନାଶ ଯିବ।
ନ ଦେଖିଲା ନେତ୍ର ପୁଣି ସେ ସମୟେ ଦେଖିବ,
ତ୍ରାହି ତ୍ରାହି ବୋଲି ପୁଣି ଡାକ ଛାଡୁଥିବ।
ହରି ଆଶ୍ରା କର ତୁହି ନିସ୍ତାର ପାଇବୁ,
ଦାସ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ କହେ ବିହନ ହୋଇବୁ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Mīna śhani yogare je dwitīya charaṇa,
akāḷe maḍaka paḍiba sehi samayare jāṇa.
bhūmikampare kampiba sakaḷa medinī,
kāḷa bhairabī chāḍibe śhūnyare je raḍi.
mīna śhani yogare je tṛutīya charaṇa,
jaḷa pralaya hoiba bhale tuhi jāṇa.
hutu hutu hoi jaḷuthibe hiṅguḷā mātibe,
baḍa baḍa koṭhā ghara jāḷi je debe.
chora ṭāuṭara saṅkhyā prabala baḍhiba,
sethipāiñ debīṅkara kope nāśha jiba.
na dekhilā netra puṇi se samaye dekhiba,
trāhi trāhi boli puṇi ḍāka chāḍuthiba.
Hari āśhrā kara tuhi nistāra pāibu,
dāsa Yaśhobanta kahe bihana hoibu.

Tikanga:
Arā, i roto i ngā tau e rua me te haurua o Meena-Shani yoga, i te wāhanga tuarua (ngā tau 2026-2027) me te wāhanga tuatoru (ngā tau 2027-2028), ka tū ngā mate ohorere i roto i te tini, ā, ka rangatira te hepohepo ki ngā wāhi katoa. Ka tū ngā rū whenua kaha, ka wiri te Ao katoa. Ka tū ngā tangi Bhairavi, arā, ka ohorere te Whaea Bhairavi ki te hamama whakamataku mai i te kore. Ka kitea ngā whakamataku o te waipuke ki te Ao. Nā te riri o te Whaea Hingula, ka tino wera ngā whare nunui, ngā whare rangatira, me ērā atu. I tuhi te Mahapurush ka riro ngā tangata kino katoa hei patunga o te riri o ngā Atua Whaea. I taua wā, ka kite ngā tāngata i ngā mea kāore anō i kitea e ō rātou kanohi i mua, arā, ka tū ngā mea katoa i roto i tētahi āhua kāore anō kia kitea. Ka tangi ngā tāngata katoa mō te atawhai. Ko te hunga e tuku ana ki ngā waewae o te Ariki Shri Hari, ā, e rapu ana i tōna piringa, i kī te Mahapurush ko rātou anake ka kite i te whakaoranga mai i ēnei mea katoa, ā, ka riro hei kākano o Satya Yuga.

I roto i te irava i runga ake nei, i kī a Mahapurush Yashovanta Das ka tīmata ēnei mahi whakangaro mai i te wāhanga tuarua o Meena-Shani yoga, arā, ngā tau 2026-2027.

I roto i tēnei Kali Yuga, he maha ngā Mahapurushas i whānau ki Odisha i ia wā. I te rautau 20, i whakaatu tētahi Mahapurush i whānau ki Odisha i ngā matapae i kōrerohia e te Ariki Nui tonu, e te Whaea Yogamaya, me ngā atua me ngā atua wahine mō te painga o ngā kaingākau. I tuhi te Mahapurush:

Odia Sloka:

ବିଂଶ ଛବିଶ ସାଲ ସରିବ,
ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି ଆଦେଶ ଦେବୀଙ୍କୁ ହେବ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Binśha chabisha sāla sariba,
Śhrī Hari ādeśha debīṅku heba.

Tikanga:
Arā, i muri i te taenga mai o te wāhanga whakamutunga o te tau 2026, i te mutunga rānei o te tau 2026, ka hoatu e te Ariki Kalki te whakahau ki ngā mana atua katoa (Whaea Ashta Chandis, 64 Yoginis, iwa crore Katyayanis) kia tīmata te whakangaro.

Kātahi ka tuhi anō te Mahapurush:

Odia Sloka:

ସମୟ ବିଂଶ ଛବିଶ ସାଲ ସରିବ ଯେବେ,
ସବୁ ଦେବା ଦେବୀ ବାହାରେ ବିଶ୍ୱଯୁଦ୍ଧ କରିବେ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Samaya binśha chabisha sāla sariba jebe,
sabu debā debī bāhāre biśhwajuddha karibe.

Tikanga:
Arā, i muri i te tau 2026, ka whakatangihia e ngā atua me ngā atua wahine katoa te pūoro whakatūpato mō te pakanga Deva-Asura. Ka tīmata te pakanga (Pakanga o te Ao) ki te ao.

He mea nui kia mōhio, i roto i ngā irava e rua i runga ake nei, i tuhi mārama te Mahapurush “Tau 2026”.

Ka tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଛଅ ସାତ ମିଶାଇଣ ତେର ଯୋଗ କର,
ଏହିଠାରୁ ଘୋର ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଭାରତ ବକ୍ଷର।
ସେ ବେଳେ ଖଣ୍ଡ ପ୍ରଳୟଟି ହେବ ଜାଣ,
ଛିନ୍ନଛତ୍ର ଏ ପୃଥିବୀ କହିଲି ପ୍ରମାଣ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Chha-a sāta mishaiṇa tera yoga kara,
ehi thāru ghora juddha bhārata bakṣhara.
se beḷe khaṇḍa pralayaṭi heba jāṇa,
chhinnachhatra e pruthibī kahili pramāṇa.

Tikanga:
Arā, honoa te ono (6) ki te whitu (7), kātahi ka tāpiri i te tekau mā toru (13) ki te tapeke, arā, 6+7+13=26, mai i reira ka tīmata te pakanga kaha i Inia. I muri i taua wā tonu, ka tū ngā kaupapa o te whakangaro wāhanga (Khanda Pralaya), ā, ka tīmata te Ao ki te ngaro.

He mea nui kia mōhio, i konei kāore a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, i rerekē ki ngā irava e rua i runga ake nei, i tuhi “Tau 2026” engari i tuhi noa 26. Tērā pea i konei, mā te 26, e tohu ana te Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ki te tau 2026 anake.

Ka tuhi anō te Mahapurush i whānau i te rautau 20:

Odia Sloka:

ବିଂଶ ଛବିଶ ପରେ ତ କରିବୁ ବିଚାର,
ମନେ ନ କରିବୁ ଯଦି ଯିବୁ ଯମପୁର।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Binśha chabisha pare ta karibu bichāra,
mane na karibu jadi jibu Yamapura.

Tikanga:
I roto i te irava i runga ake nei, e kī ana te Whaea Yogamaya i muri i te tau 2026, ka whakawāhia ngā tangata katoa. Ko te hunga katoa kāore e whakapono, he kino, he hara, me ērā atu, ka ngaro katoa.

Nō reira, me mārama te mōhio ka tū ngā mahi whakangaro a Kalki i whakaahuatia e Malika i te wā o Meena-Shani yoga tonu, engari e kore ēnei e tū i te tau 2025 engari ka tīmata mai i te wāhanga whakamutunga o te tau 2026.

E hia tau katoa ka roa ēnei mahi whakangaro, ka tuhi a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das mō tēnei kaupapa i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Kaliyuga Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଚବିଶ ଠାବରୁ ଅଣତିରିଶ ଯାଏ ପ୍ରମାଦ ଅଛି ଅପାର,
ଅଣତିରିଶ ଅଙ୍କରେଟି ହୋ ଗରୁଡ଼ ମହାକଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାର।
ଅଣତିରିଶ ଭିତରେଟି ଗରୁଡ଼ ସବୁ ଉତ୍ପାତ ସରିବ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Chabiśha ṭhābaru aṇatiriśha jāe pramāda achi apāra,
aṇatiriśha aṅkareṭi ho Garuḍa mahā Kaḷkī abatāra.
aṇatiriśha bhitareṭi Garuḍa sabu utpāta sariba.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka tīmata ngā mahi whakangaro mai i te tau 24, ā, ka roa tae noa ki te tau 29. Ka mutu ngā mahi whakangaro katoa i te tau 29.

Kia mōhio, i roto i te irava i runga ake nei, i kōrero te Mahapurush mō te tau 24, kāore i te tau 2024. Engari kua tino mārama te Mahapurush ka haere tonu ēnei mahi whakangaro, mai i te tau 24 tae noa ki te tau 29, mō te katoa o ngā tau e 6. Kāore te Mahapurush i whakamārama mai i tēhea tau ka tīmata, ā, i tēhea tau ka mutu, engari i kī ia mai i te tau 24 ki te tau 29, arā, mō te katoa o ngā tau e 6, ka haere tonu ēnei mahi whakangaro.

Nā te mea mai i ngā irava i runga ake nei kua mōhio kē tātou ka tīmata ēnei mahi whakangaro mai i te wāhanga whakamutunga o te tau 2026, ka taea e tātou te whakatau ngāwari ko te tau 24 o ‘Kaliyuga Malika’ e tohu ana ki te tau 2026-2027. Nō reira, ko te tau 29 o tēnei raupapa o tēnei tuhinga Malika e tohu ana ki te tau 2031-2032.

I whakamārama anō a Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, mā tana Malika Pothi, –

Odia Sloka:

ଏହା ତ ହେଉଛି କାଳର ଭୟ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ତହିଁ,
ଏହା ତ କଳ୍କୀ ଖେଳଟି ଦାସ ଦେଲି ବୁଝାଇ।
କଳ୍କୀ ଖେଳ କରୁଅଛନ୍ତି ହେତୁ ପ୍ରମାଣ,
ଆଉ ବା ଏକତିରିଶ ସେ ତ ହେତୁରେ ଜାଣ।
ମହାମାରୀ ସେ ତ ଆସିବ କହେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ଆସ,
ତେତିଶି ମସିହା ସୁଦ୍ଧାଟି କହେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।
ଠା ଠିକଣା ଯେ ରହିବ ନାହିଁ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ କହି,
ଏଭଳି ସମୟ ଆସିବ ଦାସ ଦେଲି ବୁଝାଇ।
କାହାରି ପତା ରହିବ ନାହିଁ ହେତୁରେ ଜାଣ,
ବୈଜ୍ଞାନୀମାନେ ବାଟଟି ପାଇବେ ନାହିଁରେ ଜାଣ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Ehā ta heuchhi kāḷara bhaya Achyutī tahiñ,
ehā ta Kaḷkī kheḷaṭi dāsa deli bujhāi.
Kaḷkī kheḷa karuachhanti hetu pramāṇa,
āu bā ekatiriśa se ta heture jāṇa.
Mahāmārī se ta āsiba kahe Achyutī āsa,
Tetiriśhi masihā suddhāṭi kahe dīna Achyuta.
Ṭhā ṭhikaṇā je rahiba nāhiñ Achyutī kahi,
ebhaḷi samaya āsiba dāsa deli bujhāi.
Kāhāri patā rahiba nāhiñ heture jāṇa,
Baijñānīmāne bāṭaṭi pāibe nāhiñ re jāṇa.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka mahi te Ariki Kalki i tana mahi (ōna mahi whakangaro). Ka haere tonu ēnei mahi whakangaro tae noa ki te tau 2033. I taua wā, he maha ngā mate urutā ka puta, ka raru ai te hapori tangata. Ka tae mai tētahi wā ka hepohepo ngā wāhi katoa. Ka noho ngā tāngata katoa i roto i te āhua pōuri. Ahakoa te mātauranga o ngā pūtaiao e kore e whai hua.

Heoi anō, i kī anō a Achyutananda Das ji i roto i tana ‘Kaliyuga Malika’ ka mutu ngā mahi whakangaro matua i te tau 27.

I tuhi te Mahapurush –

Odia Sloka:

ସତାଇଶ ଅଙ୍କେ ଦକ୍ଷିଣ ଦିଗରୁ ସମୁଦ୍ର ଆସିବ ମାଡ଼ି,
ଉତ୍ତରରୁ ଗଙ୍ଗା ଉଛୁଳି ପଡ଼ିଣ ତହିଁ ସଙ୍ଗେ ଦେବ ଧାଡ଼ି।
ପଶ୍ଚିମ ଦିଗରୁ ଜଳ ସ୍ରୋତ ଏକ ଆସି ତହିଁ ଯିବ ମିଶି,
ଖଣ୍ଡ ପ୍ରଳୟର ସୃଜନା କରିବ ପାପୀ ତହିଁ ଯିବେ ଭାସି।
ପୁଣ୍ୟାତ୍ମାମାନଙ୍କୁ ସ୍ୱୟଂ ବଳଦେବ ଉଦ୍ଧାର କରି ରଖିବେ,
ଭାରତର ଗିରି ପର୍ବତ ପାହାଡ଼େ ନେଇ ଯତନେ ପାଳିବେ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Satāisha aṅke dakhina digaru samudra āsiba māḍi,
uttararu Gaṅgā uchhuḷi paḍiṇa tahiñ saṅge deba dhāḍi.
Pashchima digaru jala srota eka āsi tahiñ jiba mishi,
khaṇḍa praḷayara sṛujanā kariba pāpī tahiñ jibe bhāsi.
Puṇyātmāmānaṅku swayaṃ Baḷadeba uddhāra kari rakhibe,
Bhāratara giri parbata pāhāḍe nei jatane pāḷibe.

Tikanga:
Arā, i te tau 27, ka puta mai ngā ngaru moana mai i te Tonga, ā, ka tūtaki ki te awa Ganges e waipuke ana mai i te Raki. I taua wā anō, ka puta mai hoki ngā ngaru kaha o te tsunami mai i te Hauāuru, ā, ka whakapōrearea. I roto i tēnei whakangaro wāhanga, ka mate te tini o ngā tangata hara. Ko te hunga tapu me ngā wairua pai, ka whakaora a Baladev tonu i a rātou, ā, ka tiaki i a rātou i runga i ngā maunga me ngā pukepuke o Inia.

E ai ki te Mahapurush, ko tēnei te aituā whakamutunga i roto i ngā aituā nunui ka tū i te tau 27, arā, i te tau 2029-2030 e ai ki te tātaitanga i whakahuatia i runga ake nei.

Inaianei, me ngana tātou ki te mārama ki tēnei rārangi wā me ētahi atu irarangi. Ka tuhia e Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i te ‘Bhabishyata Malika’ –

Odia Sloka:

ପାଞ୍ଚ ବର୍ଷ କାଳ ସତୁରି ଯାଏ ଠୁଳ,
ଲାଗି ରହିବ ତୃତୀୟ ମହାସମର।
ପାଞ୍ଚ ବର୍ଷ କାଳ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ ଘୋର,
ଚମକ ପଡିବ ସ୍ଵର୍ଗ ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟ ପାତାଳ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Pāñcha barṣha kāḷa saturi jāe ṭhuḷa,
lāgi rahiba tṛutīya mahāsamara.
pāñcha barṣha kāḷa juddha hoiba ghora,
chamaka paḍiba swarga martya pātāḷa.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka roa te Pakanga Tuatoru o te Ao mō ngā tau e rima. Ka puta he pakanga nanakia mō ngā tau e rima; ka wiri te Rangi, te Ao, me te Ao Raro.

Ka tuhia e Achyutananda Das ji i roto i tētahi atu o ana tuhinga Malika –

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି କହନ୍ତି ବାସବ ଶୁଣ,
ଚବିଶ ଠାରୁ ସତେଇଶ ଜାଣ।
ଏଥି ଭିତରେ ଯେ ହୋଇବ ଗୋଳ,
କାହାରି ନ ରହିବ ଥଳକୁଳ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Śhrī Hari kahanti Bāsaba śhuṇa,
chabiśha ṭhāru sataiśha jāṇa.
ethi bhitare je hoiba goḷa,
kāhāri na rahiba thaḷakuḷa.

Tikanga:
I roto i te irarangi i runga ake nei, ka kī a Te Ariki Shri Krishna, i a ia e kōrero ana ki a Indra, ka puta he pakanga nanakia mai i te tau 24 ki te tau 27.

He mārama mai i ngā irarangi e rua i tuhia i runga ake nei, ka roa te pakanga matua, te Pakanga Tuatoru rānei o te Ao, mō te 4-5 tau. Arā, ka tīmata i te 2026-2027 ka mutu i te 2029-2030.

Nō reira, ko te whakangaromanga matua whakamutunga i whakahuatia e te Mahapurush i roto i te ‘Kaliyuga Malika’ i te tau 27, i te tau 2029-2030 rānei, e ōrite pū ana ki ngā irarangi e rua i hoatu i runga ake nei.

Mai i ngā kōrero i runga ake nei, ki te whakatau poto:

Te tau 2026 – 2027: Ka tīmata te wā o te whakangaro.
Te tau 2029 – 2030: Ka mutu ngā kaupapa tino taumaha me te kino rawa.
Te tau 2031 – 2032: Ka mutu ngā kaupapa whakangaro katoa.

Tuhipoka: Ko ngā mahi whakangaro matua ka mutu i te tau 2029, kei roto katoa ngā mea e kōrerohia ana, pērā i – ngā rū whenua nunui, te waipuke kaha, ngā aituā hau, ngā aituā ahi, te hinganga o ngā meteorites, ngā mate-urutā, te pakanga o Inia ki ngā whenua hoariri, te Pakanga o te Ao, te pakanga Kali-Mahabharata, te pakaru o Hirakud me ētahi atu papuni, te pō mō ngā rā e whitu me ngā pō e whitu, te Bhairavi Dak, me ētahi atu.

13 Te tīmatanga o Satyayuga

Kia uru ki Satyayuga, he mea nui kia haere te tangata i te ara o Dharma. Me kōrero tonu ia i te pono, me whai i te pono anake; kaua ia e whai i te ara o te porangi, engari ko te ara o te rangimārie. Me whai ia i te aroha, te atawhai, me te ngākau aroha ki ngā mea ora katoa. Kaua ia e whakapōuri i tētahi mea ora mā tōna hinengaro, tōna reo, me tōna whanonga. Me noho ia hei kaiwhaiwhai pai mō ngā mea ora katoa me te kore take, me muru hara tōna whanonga ki ētahi atu, me noho ia hei tangata ngāwari, tika hoki, kaua e mauahara ki tētahi, me mōhio hoki ki te whakahaere i ōna pūmanawa. Me noho ia hei kaumātua o te Atua, me whakatika tonu tōna hinengaro ki te Atua, me whakapau wā ki te waiata himene, ki te karakia i ngā ingoa o te Atua, ki te uru atu ki ngā hui wairua (Satsang), ki te mahi i ngā karakia o ia rā e toru (Trisandhya), ki te whai i ngā karaipiture, me te pānui i te Shrimad Bhagavat Mahapurana (e mōhiotia ana ko te Mahapurana i roto i ngā Puranas 18) ia rā. Me noho ia hei tangata e pānui ana, e whakarongo ana rānei ki ngā mahi a te Atua. Me kai ia i ngā kai mā, Sattvic (kaihuawhenua). I te taha o te mahi i ōna mahi o te ao, me mahi hoki ia i te Yoga, te karakia ingoa, me ētahi atu. Me whakaute tonu ia ki ōna mātua me ngā kaumātua, me whakaatu aroha ki ngā taiohi, me whakatū i te ngākau o te whakahoahoa ki ngā hoa.

E hiahia ana ia tangata o tātou ki te uru ki Satyayuga mā te mawhiti i ngā mahi whakangaro e haere mai ana, ngā aituā taiao, me te Pakanga o te Ao, me te whiwhi i te atawhai o Te Ariki Kalki. Engari he māmā noa te uru ki Satyayuga? Kāore. Ko te uru ki Satyayuga ehara i te mea pērā i te hanga o te otinga ki roto i te karaihe ka inu, kia whiwhi ai te tangata e inu ana i tēnei ara ki te urunga ki Satyayuga!

Kia uru ki Satyayuga, me kawe mai e ia tangata ngā panonitanga taketake ki tōna ao. He mea nui kia, i te taha o te mahi i ngā mahi o te ao, ka tīmata ia ki te noho i te ao wairua.

E whakamārama ana i te āhua o te wā e haere mai ana mō ngā kaumātua, ka tuhia e Mahapurush Achyutananda Das i roto i tana tuhinga Malika ‘Chakada Madana’ –

Odia Sloka:

କଳିଯୁଗ ହଟି ଯାଇ ସତ୍ୟର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ଭକତମାନେ ରହିବେ ଅତ୍ୟନ୍ତ ଉଲ୍ଲାସ।
ବହୁ କଷ୍ଟ ପାଇଛନ୍ତି ଭକତ ମୋହର,
ଅଳ୍ପ ଦିନରେ ଦୁଃଖ କଷ୍ଟ ଯିବ ଦୂର।
ଏ କଳି ହଟିବ ଯୁଗ ଅନନ୍ତ ହୋଇବ,
ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ କାମନା ପୂରଣ ହୋଇବ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Kaḷiyuga haṭi jāi satyara prakāśha,
bhakatamāne rahibe atyanta ullāsa.
bahu kaṣhṭa pāichhanti bhakata mohara,
alpa dinare duḥkha kaṣhṭa jiba dūra.
e kaḷi haṭiba juga ananta hoiba,
dīna Achyutī kāmanā pūraṇa hoiba.

Tikanga:
Arā, he nui te mamae i pā ki ngā kaumātua i Kaliyuga, engari i muri i te wā poto ka mutu a Kaliyuga ka puta te mārama o te wā mutunga kore (Ananta Yuga), ka tino koa ngā kaumātua. Katahi ka ngaro katoa ō rātou mamae me ō rātou pōuri.

Ka tuhia e Saint Bhima Bhoi i roto i tētahi o ana tuhinga Malika ‘Stuti Chintamani’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ନିବାରିବେ ସନ୍ଥଙ୍କୁ ରଖିବେ ପାଳିବେ ଜନ ପରଜା,
କଳିଯୁଗ ଶେଷେ ସତ୍ୟ ଉପୁଜିବ ଅନାଦି ହୋଇବେ ରାଜା।
ହରଷ ହୋଇବେ ଭକତ ସକଳ ହରି ହରିବେ ଯେ ଦୁଃଖ,
ହରିବୋଲ ହୁଳହୁଳି ଶବଦରେ ପୂରିବ ଏହି ତ୍ରୈଲୋକ୍ୟ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Duṣṭa nibāribe santhaṅku rakhibe pāḷibe jana parajā,
Kaḷiyuga sheṣe satya upujiba Anādi hoibe rājā.
Haraṣa hoibe bhakata sakaḷa hari haribe je duḥkha,
Haribola huḷuhuḷi shabadare pūriba ehi traiḷokya.

Tikanga:
Arā, ka whakangaro Te Ariki Nui i te hunga kino, ka tiaki i ngā hunga tapu me ngā kaumātua, ka tiaki hoki i ngā iwi i runga i te Ao. Ka mutu a Kaliyuga ka tae mai a Satyayuga, ā, ko Te Ariki Kalki Tonu te kaiwhakahaere o te ao katoa. Ka tangohia e Te Ariki ngā mamae o ngā kaumātua katoa, ka pupuri i a rātou kia koa. Ka harikoa ngā kaumātua, ā, ka tangi ngā ao e toru i te karakia o te ingoa 'Hari'.

13.1 Āhea tīmata ai a Satyayuga?

E kī ana a Bhabishya Malika ka tīmata a Ananta Yuga, a Adi Satyayuga rānei, i te tau 2033. Ka tuhia e te Mahapurush i roto i tana tuhinga Malika –

Odia Sloka:

ଦୁଇ ଡାହାଣରେ ଶୂନ ଏକ ତିନି ରଖ,
ସମ୍ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ ସତ୍ୟ ପ୍ରକାଶ ହୋଇବ ପ୍ରତ୍ୟକ୍ଷ।
ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ଯବନ ଗଣ ବିନାଶଟି ଧ୍ରୁବ,
ଦୁଇ ଶୂନ ଏକ ତିନି ଅଙ୍କରୁ ଆରମ୍ଭ।
ଅନ୍ୟାୟ ଅସତ୍ୟ ମାନ ହଟି ଯାଉଥିବ,
ଅଧର୍ମର ନାଶେ ଧର୍ମ ପ୍ରଭାବୀ ହୋଇବ।
ତେତିଶିରେ ଚାରି ପାଦ ଧର୍ମ ପ୍ରକାଶିବ,
ସତ୍ୟ ଶାନ୍ତି ଦୟା କ୍ଷମା ତହୁଁ ବିକଶିବ।
ସରଳ ସଜ୍ଜନ ସାଧୁ ସନ୍ଥ ଜନ ମେଳ,
ହେବା ପାଇଁ ଏ ଧରା ରେ ଆସିଗଲା ବେଳ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Dui ḍāhāṇare shūna eka tini rakha,
sampūrṇṇa satya prakāsha hoiba pratyakṣa.
Mlechha jabana gaṇa bināshaṭi dhruba,
dui shūna eka tini aṅkaru ārambha.
Anyāya asatya māna haṭi jāuthiba,
adharmara nāshe dharma prabhābhī hoiba.
Tetishire chāri pāda dharma prakāshiba,
satya shānti dayā kṣamā tahuñ bikaśhiba.
Saraḷa sajjana sādhu santha jana meḷa,
hebā pāiñ e dharā re āsigalā beḷa.

Tikanga:
Ka tuhia e te Mahapurush mai i te tau 2013, ka tīmata āta te whakangaro o Kaliyuga me te mārama o te Pono. Mai i taua wā, ka tīmata te whakangaro o te hunga kino me te hunga hara. Ka heke te kaha o te teka, te hē, me te hē, ā, ka tīmata te piki haere o te mana o Dharma. Ka tuhia anō e te Mahapurush, mā te haere tonu pēnei, i te tau 2033, ka puta te mārama o ngā pou e whā o Dharma, arā, ka tū te whakaturanga katoa o Dharma. Mai i taua wā, ka tīmata te whanake katoa o ngā tikanga o te Pono, te Rangimārie, te Atawhai, me te Murunga hara. Ka tū he kotahitanga o ngā tangata rangatira, atawhai, me te hunga tapu, ngā wairua tapu, ngā tohunga.

Ka tuhia anō e te Mahapurush –

Odia Sloka:

ଅତ୍ୟନ୍ତ ଗୁପତୁ ଗୁପତ, ତେତିଶି ଅଙ୍କେ ହେବ ଖ୍ୟାତ।
ଭକତମାନେ ଯେ ଜାଣିବେ, ସମସ୍ତେ ହରଷ ହୋଇବେ।
ସଂସାର ଉଶ୍ଵାସ ହୋଇବ, କାଳର ଭୟ ଯେ ଛାଡିବ।
ଅନ୍ନ ବସ୍ତ୍ରରେ ସୁଖୀ ହେବେ, ଧର୍ମ ପଥକୁ ଆଚୋରିବେ।
ଶସ୍ୟ ଯେ ବହୁତ ହୋଇବ, ଲକ୍ଷ୍ମୀ କୃପାରୁ ସବୁ ହେବ।
ସତ୍ୟରେ ସର୍ବେ ଆତଜାତ, ସତ୍ୟ କଳି ଯେ ହେବ ଖ୍ୟାତ।
ପୁରାଣ ଭାଗବତ ମାନ, କରିବେ ଗାନ ସଂକୀର୍ତ୍ତନ।
କଳି କଳ୍ମଷ ନାଶ ଯିବ, ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବାକ୍ୟ ନ ଟଳିବ।
କହନ୍ତି ବ୍ରହ୍ମାଣ୍ଡ କରତା, ଅନନ୍ତ ଯୁଗର ବ୍ୟବସ୍ଥା।
ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ନଥିବେ ଏକ ଜାଣ, ଭକ୍ତ ଭଗବାନ ମିଳନ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Atyanta gupatu gupata, Tetishi aṅke heba khyāta.
Bhakatamāne je jāṇibe, samaste haraṣa hoibe.
Saṃsāra ushwāsa hoiba, kāḷara bhaya je chhāḍiba.
Anna bastrare sukhī hebe, dharma pathaku āchoribe.
Shasya je bahuta hoiba, Laxmī kṛpāru sabu heba.
Satyare sarbe ātajāta, satya kaḷi je heba khyāta.
Purāṇa Bhāgabata māna, karibe gāna saṅkīrttana.
Kaḷi kalmaṣa nāsha jiba, Achyuta bākya na ṭaliba.
Kahanti Brahmāṇḍa karatā, ananta jugara byabasthā.
Mlechha nathibe eka jāṇa, bhakta Bhagavān milana.

Tikanga:
I kī te Mahapurush he take tino ngaro tēnei, ā, ka puta i te tau 33 (te tau 2033). Ko ngā kaumātua anake ka mōhio ki tēnei. Ka tino koa rātou ki te mōhio ki tēnei. Ka wātea te ao i te hara; kāore he wehi, he rangirua rānei i roto i te hinengaro o tētahi. Ka koa katoa te tangata, ka nui ngā kai me ngā kākahu mō te katoa, ka whai te katoa i te ara o Dharma. Katahi ka puta he hauhake pai rawa, ā, ka puta tēnei nā te atawhai o Whaea Lakshmi. Ka haere te katoa i te ara o te Pono; ka tae mai te wā mutunga kore. Ka pānui te katoa i ngā Puranas me Bhagavat, ka mahi himene, ka karakia. Ka tino whakangaro a Kaliyuga; e kore rawa tēnei kōrero e heke. I kī anō te Mahapurush ko tēnei te wā mutunga kore, ā, ko tēnei te whakaritenga o te wā mutunga kore (Adya Satyayuga). Kāore he tangata hē, kino, mlechha rānei i runga i te Ao. Ko ngā kaumātua anake ka toe, ā, ka kotahi katoa rātou ki te Atua.

He Mahapurush i whānau i te rautau 20 (i kōrerohia e tātou i mua), i a ia e whakahua ana i Te Ariki Nui, Whaea Yogamaya, me ngā atua me ngā atua wahine, ka tuhia –

Odia Sloka:

ତିନି ତିନି ଚାରି ତିନି ଧାଡିରେ ତ ଜାଣ,
ଏହି ଦିନ ଠାରୁ ହେବ ସତ୍ୟର ନିୟମ।
ଗୁରୁବାର ଦିନ ଥିବ ଶୁଣୁଥାଅ ନର,
ସେହି ଦିନଠାରୁ ସତ୍ୟ ଭୋଗ ତ ଆମର।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Tini tini chāri tini dhāḍire ta jāṇa,
ehi dina thāru heba satyara niyama.
Gurubāra dina thiba śhuṇuthāa nara,
sehi dinathāru satya bhoga ta amara.

Tikanga:
I konei e tono ana te Mahapurush kia tuhia te “3” e whā ngā wā, tētahi i muri i tētahi. Arā, 3333. E kī ana te Mahapurush mai i tēnei rā, ka tīmata a Satyayuga i runga i te Ao. I konei mā te 3333, ko te tikanga o te Mahapurush – te rā tuatoru, te marama tuatoru, me te toru tekau mā toru tau, arā, Maehe 3, 2033. Ka tuhia anō e te Mahapurush ko taua rā he Rāpare, ā, ka tīmata a Satyayuga mai i taua rā tonu.

Ka tuhia anō e te Mahapurush –

Odia Sloka:

ତିନି ତାରିଖ ତ ତିନି ମାସ ବିଂଶ ଶାଳ,
ତେତିଶ ଅଙ୍କ ତ ପରେ ସତ୍ୟ ସତ୍ୟ ବୋଲ।

Whakawhitinga Pūāhua:

Tini tārikha ta tini māsa biṃśha śhāḷa,
tetishā aṅka ta pare satya satya bola.

Tikanga:
Arā, i muri i te rā tuatoru o te marama tuatoru o te tau toru tekau mā toru o te rua tekau tau, arā, Maehe 3, 2033, ka tae mai a Satyayuga.

Nō reira, kāore he ruarua inaianei ka tīmata he wā hou (Ananta Yuga, Adi Satyayuga rānei) i te tau 2033. Ko te wā tēnei mō te kaumātua anake me te Atua. Arā, ko ngā kaumātua anake ka uru ki tēnei wā. Ka tiaki Te Ariki i te Ao ki te Pono, te Dharma, me te Tika, ā, ka mahi ngā kaumātua katoa i te Pono, te Rangimārie, te Atawhai, me te Murunga hara i roto i ō rātou ao.

14 Whakarāpopototanga

Ko Bhabishya Malika he kohinga o ngā tuhinga maha i tuhia e ngā Panchasakhas i whānau i te 600 tau ki muri, me ētahi atu ngā wairua tapu Odia me ngā tangata nunui i whānau i ngā rautau 17, 18, 19, me 20. Ko ngā tuhinga Malika i tuhia e ngā Panchasakhas o Te Ariki Shri Chaitanya Mahaprabhu. Ko ēnei Panchasakhas e whakahou ana hei hoa tata o Te Ariki i ia wā me ngā ingoa rerekē, ā, i Kaliyuga hoki, i te 600 tau ki muri, i kitea e ēnei Panchasakhas te tata o tētahi whakahounga o Te Ariki, Chaitanya Mahaprabhu. Ko ngā ingoa o ēnei Panchasakhas i whānau i Kaliyuga ko – Achyutananda Das, Balaram Das, Jashobanta Das, Shishu Ananta Das, me Jagannath Das.

E kiia ana ko ngā Panchasakhas he whakahounga-wāhanga (Anshavatar) o Te Ariki, ā, i te 600 tau ki muri, i titoa ngā tuhinga Malika e rātou anake i runga i te whakahau a Te Ariki. Ko te kaupapa anake o te tito o ngā tuhinga Malika ko te oranga o ngā kaumātua – ki te whakaoho i te hinengaro moe o ngā kaumātua i te mutunga o Kaliyuga, me te whakaora i a rātou mā te ārahi i a rātou. I tuhia mārama e ngā Panchasakhas i roto i ā rātou tuhinga kaua ēnei tuhinga e whakaaturia ki tētahi atu engari ko ngā kaumātua. I whakamārama anō ngā Panchasakhas ko rātou anake ka āhei ki te whakaae ki ngā kupu o Malika kua noho hei kaumātua o Te Ariki mai i ngā whānautanga me ngā wā o mua.

Nā te mea ko Malika te reo o Mahaprabhu Tonu, ā, i tuhia e ngā Panchasakhas i runga anake i te whakahau a Te Ariki, nō reira, ko ia kupu o Malika he rite ki te rārangi i whaoa ki te kōhatu e kore rawa e he e kore rawa e panoni. Kāore he mea miharo i roto i tēnei, he maha ngā poropititanga o Malika i tuhia mō te wā o nāianei kua tino pono 100%. Hei tauira – te poropititanga o te rangatiratanga o Inia, te poropititanga o te whānautanga o Gandhiji, te poropititanga o te hanganga o tētahi whenua ko Pakistan te ingoa, te poropititanga o te hanganga o te papuni Hirakud i Odisha, me ētahi atu.

E kōrero ana a Bhabishya Malika i roto i te whakaahuatanga taipitopito i ngā kaupapa pērā i te mutunga o Kaliyuga, Kalki Avatar, te whakangaro o te hunga kino me te hunga hara, te tiaki i ngā kaumātua, te whakaturanga o Dharma, te taenga mai o Ananta Yuga, me ētahi atu. E kī ana a Malika ko te tau o Kaliyuga, i 4,32,000 tau, ka whakaitihia ki te 5,000 tau anake nā te maha o ngā mahi hara i mahia i Kaliyuga. I muri i te paunga o ngā tau 5,000, ka wehe a Te Ariki Jagannath i tōna Nilachala Dham, Puri, ka whānau ki te tinana tangata i te Biraja Kshetra, Jajpur o Odisha, ā, ka noho ki Khandagiri, Bhubaneswar. E ai ki a Malika, ko tēnei te Kalki Avatar o te Atua.

E kī ana a Malika i te mutunga o Kaliyuga, ka whiwhi ngā kaumātua i ngā tohu maha pērā mai i te temepara Puri Jagannath me ētahi atu ngā whare karakia me ngā temepara o Inia, mā te kite i ēnei ka mārama rātou kua mutu a Kaliyuga, ā, kua whānau a Te Ariki Kalki. Hei tauira, te rere atu o te haki matua mai i te temepara Jagannath, te hinganga auau o ngā kōhatu mai i te temepara, te honga o te Nilachakra, te noho auau o te kahu i runga i te temepara, me ētahi atu. E mōhiotia ana kua kitea ēnei tohu katoa e ngā kaumātua i ngā tau tata nei.

E tohu ana a Malika ki te whānautanga o Te Ariki Kalki i tētahi tau i waenga i te 2005-2008. E ai ki a Malika, ko te kaupapa o Kalki Avatar ko te whakangaro i te hunga kino, te tiaki i ngā kaumātua, me te whakatu anō i a Dharma i runga i te Ao. E kī ana a Malika ka tīmata tēnei Yuga-parivartana i te 2026-2027 i te wā o te Meena-Shani yoga e Te Ariki Kalki, ā, ka mutu katoa i te 2031-2032. I roto i tēnei wā, ka puta ngā mahi whakangaro nui katoa i mua i te tau 2029.

E kī ana hoki a Malika i te wā ka whakatu Te Ariki i a Dharma i runga i te Ao, ko te ingoa o te Kingi o Puri ko Shri Dibyasingha Deb, arā, ka puta ngā mahi whakangaro katoa a Te Ariki i te wā o te rangatiratanga o Puri Kingi Dibyasingha Deb. E ai ki a Malika, ko ēnei mahi whakangaro ka puta mā te whakangaromanga o ngā Huanga e Rima (Panchabhuta) me te Pakanga o te Ao.

E kī ana a Malika i muri i te mutunga katoa o ngā mahi whakangaro i te tau 2031-2032, ka tīmata a Adi-Satyayuga, a Anantayuga rānei, i te tau 2033.
